**1.1.1**

a̱gnim ī̍ḻe pu̱rohi̍taṁ ya̱jñasya̍ de̱vam ṛ̱tvija̍m | hotā̍raṁ ratna̱dhāta̍mam ||

**1.1.2**

a̱gniḥ pūrve̍bhi̱r ṛṣi̍bhi̱r īḍyo̱ nūta̍nair u̱ta | sa de̱vām̐ eha va̍kṣati ||

**1.1.3**

a̱gninā̍ ra̱yim a̍śnava̱t poṣa̍m e̱va di̱ve-di̍ve | ya̱śasa̍ṁ vī̱rava̍ttamam ||

**1.1.4**

agne̱ yaṁ ya̱jñam a̍dhva̱raṁ vi̱śvata̍ḥ pari̱bhūr asi̍ | sa id de̱veṣu̍ gacchati ||

**1.1.5**

a̱gnir hotā̍ ka̱vikra̍tuḥ sa̱tyaś ci̱traśra̍vastamaḥ | de̱vo de̱vebhi̱r ā ga̍mat ||

**1.1.6**

yad a̱ṅga dā̱śuṣe̱ tvam agne̍ bha̱draṁ ka̍ri̱ṣyasi̍ | tavet tat sa̱tyam a̍ṅgiraḥ ||

**1.1.7**

upa̍ tvāgne di̱ve-di̍ve̱ doṣā̍vastar dhi̱yā va̱yam | namo̱ bhara̍nta̱ ema̍si ||

**1.1.8**

rāja̍ntam adhva̱rāṇā̍ṁ go̱pām ṛ̱tasya̱ dīdi̍vim | vardha̍māna̱ṁ sve dame̍ ||

**1.1.9**

sa na̍ḥ pi̱teva̍ sū̱nave 'gne̍ sūpāya̱no bha̍va | saca̍svā naḥ sva̱staye̍ ||

**1.2.1**

vāya̱v ā yā̍hi darśate̱me somā̱ ara̍ṁkṛtāḥ | teṣā̍m pāhi śru̱dhī hava̍m ||

**1.2.2**

vāya̍ u̱kthebhi̍r jarante̱ tvām acchā̍ jari̱tāra̍ḥ | su̱taso̍mā aha̱rvida̍ḥ ||

**1.2.3**

vāyo̱ tava̍ prapṛñca̱tī dhenā̍ jigāti dā̱śuṣe̍ | u̱rū̱cī soma̍pītaye ||

**1.2.4**

indra̍vāyū i̱me su̱tā upa̱ prayo̍bhi̱r ā ga̍tam | inda̍vo vām u̱śanti̱ hi ||

**1.2.5**

vāya̱v indra̍ś ca cetathaḥ su̱tānā̍ṁ vājinīvasū | tāv ā yā̍ta̱m upa̍ dra̱vat ||

**1.2.6**

vāya̱v indra̍ś ca sunva̱ta ā yā̍ta̱m upa̍ niṣkṛ̱tam | ma̱kṣv i1̱̍tthā dhi̱yā na̍rā ||

**1.2.7**

mi̱traṁ hu̍ve pū̱tada̍kṣa̱ṁ varu̍ṇaṁ ca ri̱śāda̍sam | dhiya̍ṁ ghṛ̱tācī̱ṁ sādha̍ntā ||

**1.2.8**

ṛ̱tena̍ mitrāvaruṇāv ṛtāvṛdhāv ṛtaspṛśā | kratu̍m bṛ̱hanta̍m āśāthe ||

**1.2.9**

ka̱vī no̍ mi̱trāvaru̍ṇā tuvijā̱tā u̍ru̱kṣayā̍ | dakṣa̍ṁ dadhāte a̱pasa̍m ||

**1.3.1**

aśvi̍nā̱ yajva̍rī̱r iṣo̱ drava̍tpāṇī̱ śubha̍s patī | puru̍bhujā cana̱syata̍m ||

**1.3.2**

aśvi̍nā̱ puru̍daṁsasā̱ narā̱ śavī̍rayā dhi̱yā | dhiṣṇyā̱ vana̍ta̱ṁ gira̍ḥ ||

**1.3.3**

dasrā̍ yu̱vāka̍vaḥ su̱tā nāsa̍tyā vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣaḥ | ā yā̍taṁ rudravartanī ||

**1.3.4**

indrā yā̍hi citrabhāno su̱tā i̱me tvā̱yave̍ḥ | aṇvī̍bhi̱s tanā̍ pū̱tāsa̍ḥ ||

**1.3.5**

indrā yā̍hi dhi̱yeṣi̱to vipra̍jūtaḥ su̱tāva̍taḥ | upa̱ brahmā̍ṇi vā̱ghata̍ḥ ||

**1.3.6**

indrā yā̍hi̱ tūtu̍jāna̱ upa̱ brahmā̍ṇi harivaḥ | su̱te da̍dhiṣva na̱ś cana̍ḥ ||

**1.3.7**

omā̍saś carṣaṇīdhṛto̱ viśve̍ devāsa̱ ā ga̍ta | dā̱śvāṁso̍ dā̱śuṣa̍ḥ su̱tam ||

**1.3.8**

viśve̍ de̱vāso̍ a̱ptura̍ḥ su̱tam ā ga̍nta̱ tūrṇa̍yaḥ | u̱srā i̍va̱ svasa̍rāṇi ||

**1.3.9**

viśve̍ de̱vāso̍ a̱sridha̱ ehi̍māyāso a̱druha̍ḥ | medha̍ṁ juṣanta̱ vahna̍yaḥ ||

**1.3.10**

pā̱va̱kā na̱ḥ sara̍svatī̱ vāje̍bhir vā̱jinī̍vatī | ya̱jñaṁ va̍ṣṭu dhi̱yāva̍suḥ ||

**1.3.11**

co̱da̱yi̱trī sū̱nṛtā̍nā̱ṁ ceta̍ntī sumatī̱nām | ya̱jñaṁ da̍dhe̱ sara̍svatī ||

**1.3.12**

ma̱ho arṇa̱ḥ sara̍svatī̱ pra ce̍tayati ke̱tunā̍ | dhiyo̱ viśvā̱ vi rā̍jati ||

**1.4.1**

su̱rū̱pa̱kṛ̱tnum ū̱taye̍ su̱dughā̍m iva go̱duhe̍ | ju̱hū̱masi̱ dyavi̍-dyavi ||

**1.4.2**

upa̍ na̱ḥ sava̱nā ga̍hi̱ soma̍sya somapāḥ piba | go̱dā id re̱vato̱ mada̍ḥ ||

**1.4.3**

athā̍ te̱ anta̍mānāṁ vi̱dyāma̍ sumatī̱nām | mā no̱ ati̍ khya̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.4.4**

pare̍hi̱ vigra̱m astṛ̍ta̱m indra̍m pṛcchā vipa̱ścita̍m | yas te̱ sakhi̍bhya̱ ā vara̍m ||

**1.4.5**

u̱ta bru̍vantu no̱ nido̱ nir a̱nyata̍ś cid ārata | dadhā̍nā̱ indra̱ id duva̍ḥ ||

**1.4.6**

u̱ta na̍ḥ su̱bhagā̍m̐ a̱rir vo̱ceyu̍r dasma kṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ | syāmed indra̍sya̱ śarma̍ṇi ||

**1.4.7**

em ā̱śum ā̱śave̍ bhara yajña̱śriya̍ṁ nṛ̱māda̍nam | pa̱ta̱yan ma̍nda̱yatsa̍kham ||

**1.4.8**

a̱sya pī̱tvā śa̍takrato gha̱no vṛ̱trāṇā̍m abhavaḥ | prāvo̱ vāje̍ṣu vā̱jina̍m ||

**1.4.9**

taṁ tvā̱ vāje̍ṣu vā̱jina̍ṁ vā̱jayā̍maḥ śatakrato | dhanā̍nām indra sā̱taye̍ ||

**1.4.10**

yo rā̱yo̱3̱̍ 'vani̍r ma̱hān su̍pā̱raḥ su̍nva̱taḥ sakhā̍ | tasmā̱ indrā̍ya gāyata ||

**1.5.1**

ā tv etā̱ ni ṣī̍da̱tendra̍m a̱bhi pra gā̍yata | sakhā̍ya̱ḥ stoma̍vāhasaḥ ||

**1.5.2**

pu̱rū̱tama̍m purū̱ṇām īśā̍na̱ṁ vāryā̍ṇām | indra̱ṁ some̱ sacā̍ su̱te ||

**1.5.3**

sa ghā̍ no̱ yoga̱ ā bhu̍va̱t sa rā̱ye sa pura̍ṁdhyām | gama̱d vāje̍bhi̱r ā sa na̍ḥ ||

**1.5.4**

yasya̍ sa̱ṁsthe na vṛ̱ṇvate̱ harī̍ sa̱matsu̱ śatra̍vaḥ | tasmā̱ indrā̍ya gāyata ||

**1.5.5**

su̱ta̱pāvne̍ su̱tā i̱me śuca̍yo yanti vī̱taye̍ | somā̍so̱ dadhyā̍śiraḥ ||

**1.5.6**

tvaṁ su̱tasya̍ pī̱taye̍ sa̱dyo vṛ̱ddho a̍jāyathāḥ | indra̱ jyaiṣṭhyā̍ya sukrato ||

**1.5.7**

ā tvā̍ viśantv ā̱śava̱ḥ somā̍sa indra girvaṇaḥ | śaṁ te̍ santu̱ prace̍tase ||

**1.5.8**

tvāṁ stomā̍ avīvṛdha̱n tvām u̱kthā śa̍takrato | tvāṁ va̍rdhantu no̱ gira̍ḥ ||

**1.5.9**

akṣi̍totiḥ saned i̱maṁ vāja̱m indra̍ḥ saha̱sriṇa̍m | yasmi̱n viśvā̍ni̱ pauṁsyā̍ ||

**1.5.10**

mā no̱ martā̍ a̱bhi dru̍han ta̱nūnā̍m indra girvaṇaḥ | īśā̍no yavayā va̱dham ||

**1.6.1**

yu̱ñjanti̍ bra̱dhnam a̍ru̱ṣaṁ cara̍nta̱m pari̍ ta̱sthuṣa̍ḥ | roca̍nte roca̱nā di̱vi ||

**1.6.2**

yu̱ñjanty a̍sya̱ kāmyā̱ harī̱ vipa̍kṣasā̱ rathe̍ | śoṇā̍ dhṛ̱ṣṇū nṛ̱vāha̍sā ||

**1.6.3**

ke̱tuṁ kṛ̱ṇvann a̍ke̱tave̱ peśo̍ maryā ape̱śase̍ | sam u̱ṣadbhi̍r ajāyathāḥ ||

**1.6.4**

ād aha̍ sva̱dhām anu̱ puna̍r garbha̱tvam e̍ri̱re | dadhā̍nā̱ nāma̍ ya̱jñiya̍m ||

**1.6.5**

vī̱ḻu ci̍d āruja̱tnubhi̱r guhā̍ cid indra̱ vahni̍bhiḥ | avi̍nda u̱sriyā̱ anu̍ ||

**1.6.6**

de̱va̱yanto̱ yathā̍ ma̱tim acchā̍ vi̱dadva̍su̱ṁ gira̍ḥ | ma̱hām a̍nūṣata śru̱tam ||

**1.6.7**

indre̍ṇa̱ saṁ hi dṛkṣa̍se saṁjagmā̱no abi̍bhyuṣā | ma̱ndū sa̍mā̱nava̍rcasā ||

**1.6.8**

a̱na̱va̱dyair a̱bhidyu̍bhir ma̱khaḥ saha̍svad arcati | ga̱ṇair indra̍sya̱ kāmyai̍ḥ ||

**1.6.9**

ata̍ḥ parijma̱nn ā ga̍hi di̱vo vā̍ roca̱nād adhi̍ | sam a̍sminn ṛñjate̱ gira̍ḥ ||

**1.6.10**

i̱to vā̍ sā̱tim īma̍he di̱vo vā̱ pārthi̍vā̱d adhi̍ | indra̍m ma̱ho vā̱ raja̍saḥ ||

**1.7.1**

indra̱m id gā̱thino̍ bṛ̱had indra̍m a̱rkebhi̍r a̱rkiṇa̍ḥ | indra̱ṁ vāṇī̍r anūṣata ||

**1.7.2**

indra̱ id dharyo̱ḥ sacā̱ sammi̍śla̱ ā va̍co̱yujā̍ | indro̍ va̱jrī hi̍ra̱ṇyaya̍ḥ ||

**1.7.3**

indro̍ dī̱rghāya̱ cakṣa̍sa̱ ā sūrya̍ṁ rohayad di̱vi | vi gobhi̱r adri̍m airayat ||

**1.7.4**

indra̱ vāje̍ṣu no 'va sa̱hasra̍pradhaneṣu ca | u̱gra u̱grābhi̍r ū̱tibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.7.5**

indra̍ṁ va̱yam ma̍hādha̱na indra̱m arbhe̍ havāmahe | yuja̍ṁ vṛ̱treṣu̍ va̱jriṇa̍m ||

**1.7.6**

sa no̍ vṛṣann a̱muṁ ca̱ruṁ satrā̍dāva̱nn apā̍ vṛdhi | a̱smabhya̱m apra̍tiṣkutaḥ ||

**1.7.7**

tu̱ñje-tu̍ñje̱ ya utta̍re̱ stomā̱ indra̍sya va̱jriṇa̍ḥ | na vi̍ndhe asya suṣṭu̱tim ||

**1.7.8**

vṛṣā̍ yū̱theva̱ vaṁsa̍gaḥ kṛ̱ṣṭīr i̍ya̱rty oja̍sā | īśā̍no̱ apra̍tiṣkutaḥ ||

**1.7.9**

ya eka̍ś carṣaṇī̱nāṁ vasū̍nām ira̱jyati̍ | indra̱ḥ pañca̍ kṣitī̱nām ||

**1.7.10**

indra̍ṁ vo vi̱śvata̱s pari̱ havā̍mahe̱ jane̍bhyaḥ | a̱smāka̍m astu̱ keva̍laḥ ||

**1.8.1**

endra̍ sāna̱siṁ ra̱yiṁ sa̱jitvā̍naṁ sadā̱saha̍m | varṣi̍ṣṭham ū̱taye̍ bhara ||

**1.8.2**

ni yena̍ muṣṭiha̱tyayā̱ ni vṛ̱trā ru̱ṇadhā̍mahai | tvotā̍so̱ ny arva̍tā ||

**1.8.3**

indra̱ tvotā̍sa̱ ā va̱yaṁ vajra̍ṁ gha̱nā da̍dīmahi | jaye̍ma̱ saṁ yu̱dhi spṛdha̍ḥ ||

**1.8.4**

va̱yaṁ śūre̍bhi̱r astṛ̍bhi̱r indra̱ tvayā̍ yu̱jā va̱yam | sā̱sa̱hyāma̍ pṛtanya̱taḥ ||

**1.8.5**

ma̱hām̐ indra̍ḥ pa̱raś ca̱ nu ma̍hi̱tvam a̍stu va̱jriṇe̍ | dyaur na pra̍thi̱nā śava̍ḥ ||

**1.8.6**

sa̱mo̱he vā̱ ya āśa̍ta̱ nara̍s to̱kasya̱ sani̍tau | viprā̍so vā dhiyā̱yava̍ḥ ||

**1.8.7**

yaḥ ku̱kṣiḥ so̍ma̱pāta̍maḥ samu̱dra i̍va̱ pinva̍te | u̱rvīr āpo̱ na kā̱kuda̍ḥ ||

**1.8.8**

e̱vā hy a̍sya sū̱nṛtā̍ vira̱pśī goma̍tī ma̱hī | pa̱kvā śākhā̱ na dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**1.8.9**

e̱vā hi te̱ vibhū̍taya ū̱taya̍ indra̱ māva̍te | sa̱dyaś ci̱t santi̍ dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**1.8.10**

e̱vā hy a̍sya̱ kāmyā̱ stoma̍ u̱kthaṁ ca̱ śaṁsyā̍ | indrā̍ya̱ soma̍pītaye ||

**1.9.1**

indrehi̱ matsy andha̍so̱ viśve̍bhiḥ soma̱parva̍bhiḥ | ma̱hām̐ a̍bhi̱ṣṭir oja̍sā ||

**1.9.2**

em e̍naṁ sṛjatā su̱te ma̱ndim indrā̍ya ma̱ndine̍ | cakri̱ṁ viśvā̍ni̱ cakra̍ye ||

**1.9.3**

matsvā̍ suśipra ma̱ndibhi̱ḥ stome̍bhir viśvacarṣaṇe | sacai̱ṣu sava̍ne̱ṣv ā ||

**1.9.4**

asṛ̍gram indra te̱ gira̱ḥ prati̱ tvām ud a̍hāsata | ajo̍ṣā vṛṣa̱bham pati̍m ||

**1.9.5**

saṁ co̍daya ci̱tram a̱rvāg rādha̍ indra̱ vare̍ṇyam | asa̱d it te̍ vi̱bhu pra̱bhu ||

**1.9.6**

a̱smān su tatra̍ coda̱yendra̍ rā̱ye rabha̍svataḥ | tuvi̍dyumna̱ yaśa̍svataḥ ||

**1.9.7**

saṁ goma̍d indra̱ vāja̍vad a̱sme pṛ̱thu śravo̍ bṛ̱hat | vi̱śvāyu̍r dhe̱hy akṣi̍tam ||

**1.9.8**

a̱sme dhe̍hi̱ śravo̍ bṛ̱had dyu̱mnaṁ sa̍hasra̱sāta̍mam | indra̱ tā ra̱thinī̱r iṣa̍ḥ ||

**1.9.9**

vaso̱r indra̱ṁ vasu̍patiṁ gī̱rbhir gṛ̱ṇanta̍ ṛ̱gmiya̍m | homa̱ gantā̍ram ū̱taye̍ ||

**1.9.10**

su̱te-su̍te̱ nyo̍kase bṛ̱had bṛ̍ha̱ta ed a̱riḥ | indrā̍ya śū̱ṣam a̍rcati ||

**1.10.1**

gāya̍nti tvā gāya̱triṇo 'rca̍nty a̱rkam a̱rkiṇa̍ḥ | bra̱hmāṇa̍s tvā śatakrata̱ ud va̱ṁśam i̍va yemire ||

**1.10.2**

yat sāno̱ḥ sānu̱m āru̍ha̱d bhūry aspa̍ṣṭa̱ kartva̍m | tad indro̱ artha̍ṁ cetati yū̱thena̍ vṛ̱ṣṇir e̍jati ||

**1.10.3**

yu̱kṣvā hi ke̱śinā̱ harī̱ vṛṣa̍ṇā kakṣya̱prā | athā̍ na indra somapā gi̱rām upa̍śrutiṁ cara ||

**1.10.4**

ehi̱ stomā̍m̐ a̱bhi sva̍rā̱bhi gṛ̍ṇī̱hy ā ru̍va | brahma̍ ca no vaso̱ sacendra̍ ya̱jñaṁ ca̍ vardhaya ||

**1.10.5**

u̱ktham indrā̍ya̱ śaṁsya̱ṁ vardha̍nam puruni̱ṣṣidhe̍ | śa̱kro yathā̍ su̱teṣu̍ ṇo rā̱raṇa̍t sa̱khyeṣu̍ ca ||

**1.10.6**

tam it sa̍khi̱tva ī̍mahe̱ taṁ rā̱ye taṁ su̱vīrye̍ | sa śa̱kra u̱ta na̍ḥ śaka̱d indro̱ vasu̱ daya̍mānaḥ ||

**1.10.7**

su̱vi̱vṛta̍ṁ suni̱raja̱m indra̱ tvādā̍ta̱m id yaśa̍ḥ | gavā̱m apa̍ vra̱jaṁ vṛ̍dhi kṛṇu̱ṣva rādho̍ adrivaḥ ||

**1.10.8**

na̱hi tvā̱ roda̍sī u̱bhe ṛ̍ghā̱yamā̍ṇa̱m inva̍taḥ | jeṣa̱ḥ sva̍rvatīr a̱paḥ saṁ gā a̱smabhya̍ṁ dhūnuhi ||

**1.10.9**

āśru̍tkarṇa śru̱dhī hava̱ṁ nū ci̍d dadhiṣva me̱ gira̍ḥ | indra̱ stoma̍m i̱mam mama̍ kṛ̱ṣvā yu̱jaś ci̱d anta̍ram ||

**1.10.10**

vi̱dmā hi tvā̱ vṛṣa̍ntama̱ṁ vāje̍ṣu havana̱śruta̍m | vṛṣa̍ntamasya hūmaha ū̱tiṁ sa̍hasra̱sāta̍mām ||

**1.10.11**

ā tū na̍ indra kauśika mandasā̱naḥ su̱tam pi̍ba | navya̱m āyu̱ḥ pra sū ti̍ra kṛ̱dhī sa̍hasra̱sām ṛṣi̍m ||

**1.10.12**

pari̍ tvā girvaṇo̱ gira̍ i̱mā bha̍vantu vi̱śvata̍ḥ | vṛ̱ddhāyu̱m anu̱ vṛddha̍yo̱ juṣṭā̍ bhavantu̱ juṣṭa̍yaḥ ||

**1.11.1**

indra̱ṁ viśvā̍ avīvṛdhan samu̱dravya̍casa̱ṁ gira̍ḥ | ra̱thīta̍maṁ ra̱thīnā̱ṁ vājā̍nā̱ṁ satpa̍ti̱m pati̍m ||

**1.11.2**

sa̱khye ta̍ indra vā̱jino̱ mā bhe̍ma śavasas pate | tvām a̱bhi pra ṇo̍numo̱ jetā̍ra̱m apa̍rājitam ||

**1.11.3**

pū̱rvīr indra̍sya rā̱tayo̱ na vi da̍syanty ū̱taya̍ḥ | yadī̱ vāja̍sya̱ goma̍taḥ sto̱tṛbhyo̱ maṁha̍te ma̱gham ||

**1.11.4**

pu̱rām bhi̱ndur yuvā̍ ka̱vir ami̍taujā ajāyata | indro̱ viśva̍sya̱ karma̍ṇo dha̱rtā va̱jrī pu̍ruṣṭu̱taḥ ||

**1.11.5**

tvaṁ va̱lasya̱ goma̱to 'pā̍var adrivo̱ bila̍m | tvāṁ de̱vā abi̍bhyuṣas tu̱jyamā̍nāsa āviṣuḥ ||

**1.11.6**

tavā̱haṁ śū̍ra rā̱tibhi̱ḥ praty ā̍ya̱ṁ sindhu̍m ā̱vada̍n | upā̍tiṣṭhanta girvaṇo vi̱duṣ ṭe̱ tasya̍ kā̱rava̍ḥ ||

**1.11.7**

mā̱yābhi̍r indra mā̱yina̱ṁ tvaṁ śuṣṇa̱m avā̍tiraḥ | vi̱duṣ ṭe̱ tasya̱ medhi̍rā̱s teṣā̱ṁ śravā̱ṁsy ut ti̍ra ||

**1.11.8**

indra̱m īśā̍na̱m oja̍sā̱bhi stomā̍ anūṣata | sa̱hasra̱ṁ yasya̍ rā̱taya̍ u̱ta vā̱ santi̱ bhūya̍sīḥ ||

**1.12.1**

a̱gniṁ dū̱taṁ vṛ̍ṇīmahe̱ hotā̍raṁ vi̱śvave̍dasam | a̱sya ya̱jñasya̍ su̱kratu̍m ||

**1.12.2**

a̱gnim-a̍gni̱ṁ havī̍mabhi̱ḥ sadā̍ havanta vi̱śpati̍m | ha̱vya̱vāha̍m purupri̱yam ||

**1.12.3**

agne̍ de̱vām̐ i̱hā va̍ha jajñā̱no vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣe | asi̱ hotā̍ na̱ īḍya̍ḥ ||

**1.12.4**

tām̐ u̍śa̱to vi bo̍dhaya̱ yad a̍gne̱ yāsi̍ dū̱tya̍m | de̱vair ā sa̍tsi ba̱rhiṣi̍ ||

**1.12.5**

ghṛtā̍havana dīdiva̱ḥ prati̍ ṣma̱ riṣa̍to daha | agne̱ tvaṁ ra̍kṣa̱svina̍ḥ ||

**1.12.6**

a̱gninā̱gniḥ sam i̍dhyate ka̱vir gṛ̱hapa̍ti̱r yuvā̍ | ha̱vya̱vāḍ ju̱hvā̍syaḥ ||

**1.12.7**

ka̱vim a̱gnim upa̍ stuhi sa̱tyadha̍rmāṇam adhva̱re | de̱vam a̍mīva̱cāta̍nam ||

**1.12.8**

yas tvām a̍gne ha̱viṣpa̍tir dū̱taṁ de̍va sapa̱ryati̍ | tasya̍ sma prāvi̱tā bha̍va ||

**1.12.9**

yo a̱gniṁ de̱vavī̍taye ha̱viṣmā̍m̐ ā̱vivā̍sati | tasmai̍ pāvaka mṛḻaya ||

**1.12.10**

sa na̍ḥ pāvaka dīdi̱vo 'gne̍ de̱vām̐ i̱hā va̍ha | upa̍ ya̱jñaṁ ha̱viś ca̍ naḥ ||

**1.12.11**

sa na̱ḥ stavā̍na̱ ā bha̍ra gāya̱treṇa̱ navī̍yasā | ra̱yiṁ vī̱rava̍tī̱m iṣa̍m ||

**1.12.12**

agne̍ śu̱kreṇa̍ śo̱ciṣā̱ viśvā̍bhir de̱vahū̍tibhiḥ | i̱maṁ stoma̍ṁ juṣasva naḥ ||

**1.13.1**

susa̍middho na̱ ā va̍ha de̱vām̐ a̍gne ha̱viṣma̍te | hota̍ḥ pāvaka̱ yakṣi̍ ca ||

**1.13.2**

madhu̍mantaṁ tanūnapād ya̱jñaṁ de̱veṣu̍ naḥ kave | a̱dyā kṛ̍ṇuhi vī̱taye̍ ||

**1.13.3**

narā̱śaṁsa̍m i̱ha pri̱yam a̱smin ya̱jña upa̍ hvaye | madhu̍jihvaṁ havi̱ṣkṛta̍m ||

**1.13.4**

agne̍ su̱khata̍me̱ rathe̍ de̱vām̐ ī̍ḻi̱ta ā va̍ha | asi̱ hotā̱ manu̍rhitaḥ ||

**1.13.5**

stṛ̱ṇī̱ta ba̱rhir ā̍nu̱ṣag ghṛ̱tapṛ̍ṣṭham manīṣiṇaḥ | yatrā̱mṛta̍sya̱ cakṣa̍ṇam ||

**1.13.6**

vi śra̍yantām ṛtā̱vṛdho̱ dvāro̍ de̱vīr a̍sa̱ścata̍ḥ | a̱dyā nū̱naṁ ca̱ yaṣṭa̍ve ||

**1.13.7**

nakto̱ṣāsā̍ su̱peśa̍sā̱smin ya̱jña upa̍ hvaye | i̱daṁ no̍ ba̱rhir ā̱sade̍ ||

**1.13.8**

tā su̍ji̱hvā upa̍ hvaye̱ hotā̍rā̱ daivyā̍ ka̱vī | ya̱jñaṁ no̍ yakṣatām i̱mam ||

**1.13.9**

iḻā̱ sara̍svatī ma̱hī ti̱sro de̱vīr ma̍yo̱bhuva̍ḥ | ba̱rhiḥ sī̍dantv a̱sridha̍ḥ ||

**1.13.10**

i̱ha tvaṣṭā̍ram agri̱yaṁ vi̱śvarū̱pa̱m upa̍ hvaye | a̱smāka̍m astu̱ keva̍laḥ ||

**1.13.11**

ava̍ sṛjā vanaspate̱ deva̍ de̱vebhyo̍ ha̱viḥ | pra dā̱tur a̍stu̱ ceta̍nam ||

**1.13.12**

svāhā̍ ya̱jñaṁ kṛ̍ṇota̱nendrā̍ya̱ yajva̍no gṛ̱he | tatra̍ de̱vām̐ upa̍ hvaye ||

**1.14.1**

aibhi̍r agne̱ duvo̱ giro̱ viśve̍bhi̱ḥ soma̍pītaye | de̱vebhi̍r yāhi̱ yakṣi̍ ca ||

**1.14.2**

ā tvā̱ kaṇvā̍ ahūṣata gṛ̱ṇanti̍ vipra te̱ dhiya̍ḥ | de̱vebhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.14.3**

i̱ndra̱vā̱yū bṛha̱spati̍m mi̱trāgnim pū̱ṣaṇa̱m bhaga̍m | ā̱di̱tyān māru̍taṁ ga̱ṇam ||

**1.14.4**

pra vo̍ bhriyanta̱ inda̍vo matsa̱rā mā̍dayi̱ṣṇava̍ḥ | dra̱psā madhva̍ś camū̱ṣada̍ḥ ||

**1.14.5**

īḻa̍te̱ tvām a̍va̱syava̱ḥ kaṇvā̍so vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣaḥ | ha̱viṣma̍nto ara̱ṁkṛta̍ḥ ||

**1.14.6**

ghṛ̱tapṛ̍ṣṭhā mano̱yujo̱ ye tvā̱ vaha̍nti̱ vahna̍yaḥ | ā de̱vān soma̍pītaye ||

**1.14.7**

tān yaja̍trām̐ ṛtā̱vṛdho 'gne̱ patnī̍vatas kṛdhi | madhva̍ḥ sujihva pāyaya ||

**1.14.8**

ye yaja̍trā̱ ya īḍyā̱s te te̍ pibantu ji̱hvayā̍ | madho̍r agne̱ vaṣa̍ṭkṛti ||

**1.14.9**

ākī̱ṁ sūrya̍sya roca̱nād viśvā̍n de̱vām̐ u̍ṣa̱rbudha̍ḥ | vipro̱ hote̱ha va̍kṣati ||

**1.14.10**

viśve̍bhiḥ so̱myam madhv agna̱ indre̍ṇa vā̱yunā̍ | pibā̍ mi̱trasya̱ dhāma̍bhiḥ ||

**1.14.11**

tvaṁ hotā̱ manu̍rhi̱to 'gne̍ ya̱jñeṣu̍ sīdasi | semaṁ no̍ adhva̱raṁ ya̍ja ||

**1.14.12**

yu̱kṣvā hy aru̍ṣī̱ rathe̍ ha̱rito̍ deva ro̱hita̍ḥ | tābhi̍r de̱vām̐ i̱hā va̍ha ||

**1.15.1**

indra̱ soma̱m piba̍ ṛ̱tunā tvā̍ viśa̱ntv inda̍vaḥ | ma̱tsa̱rāsa̱s tado̍kasaḥ ||

**1.15.2**

maru̍ta̱ḥ piba̍ta ṛ̱tunā̍ po̱trād ya̱jñam pu̍nītana | yū̱yaṁ hi ṣṭhā su̍dānavaḥ ||

**1.15.3**

a̱bhi ya̱jñaṁ gṛ̍ṇīhi no̱ gnāvo̱ neṣṭa̱ḥ piba̍ ṛ̱tunā̍ | tvaṁ hi ra̍tna̱dhā asi̍ ||

**1.15.4**

agne̍ de̱vām̐ i̱hā va̍ha sā̱dayā̱ yoni̍ṣu tri̱ṣu | pari̍ bhūṣa̱ piba̍ ṛ̱tunā̍ ||

**1.15.5**

brāhma̍ṇād indra̱ rādha̍sa̱ḥ pibā̱ soma̍m ṛ̱tūm̐r anu̍ | taved dhi sa̱khyam astṛ̍tam ||

**1.15.6**

yu̱vaṁ dakṣa̍ṁ dhṛtavrata̱ mitrā̍varuṇa dū̱ḻabha̍m | ṛ̱tunā̍ ya̱jñam ā̍śāthe ||

**1.15.7**

dra̱vi̱ṇo̱dā dravi̍ṇaso̱ grāva̍hastāso adhva̱re | ya̱jñeṣu̍ de̱vam ī̍ḻate ||

**1.15.8**

dra̱vi̱ṇo̱dā da̍dātu no̱ vasū̍ni̱ yāni̍ śṛṇvi̱re | de̱veṣu̱ tā va̍nāmahe ||

**1.15.9**

dra̱vi̱ṇo̱dāḥ pi̍pīṣati ju̱hota̱ pra ca̍ tiṣṭhata | ne̱ṣṭrād ṛ̱tubhi̍r iṣyata ||

**1.15.10**

yat tvā̍ tu̱rīya̍m ṛ̱tubhi̱r dravi̍ṇodo̱ yajā̍mahe | adha̍ smā no da̱dir bha̍va ||

**1.15.11**

aśvi̍nā̱ piba̍ta̱m madhu̱ dīdya̍gnī śucivratā | ṛ̱tunā̍ yajñavāhasā ||

**1.15.12**

gārha̍patyena santya ṛ̱tunā̍ yajña̱nīr a̍si | de̱vān de̍vaya̱te ya̍ja ||

**1.16.1**

ā tvā̍ vahantu̱ hara̍yo̱ vṛṣa̍ṇa̱ṁ soma̍pītaye | indra̍ tvā̱ sūra̍cakṣasaḥ ||

**1.16.2**

i̱mā dhā̱nā ghṛ̍ta̱snuvo̱ harī̍ i̱hopa̍ vakṣataḥ | indra̍ṁ su̱khata̍me̱ rathe̍ ||

**1.16.3**

indra̍m prā̱tar ha̍vāmaha̱ indra̍m praya̱ty a̍dhva̱re | indra̱ṁ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||

**1.16.4**

upa̍ naḥ su̱tam ā ga̍hi̱ hari̍bhir indra ke̱śibhi̍ḥ | su̱te hi tvā̱ havā̍mahe ||

**1.16.5**

semaṁ na̱ḥ stoma̱m ā ga̱hy upe̱daṁ sava̍naṁ su̱tam | gau̱ro na tṛ̍ṣi̱taḥ pi̍ba ||

**1.16.6**

i̱me somā̍sa̱ inda̍vaḥ su̱tāso̱ adhi̍ ba̱rhiṣi̍ | tām̐ i̍ndra̱ saha̍se piba ||

**1.16.7**

a̱yaṁ te̱ stomo̍ agri̱yo hṛ̍di̱spṛg a̍stu̱ śaṁta̍maḥ | athā̱ soma̍ṁ su̱tam pi̍ba ||

**1.16.8**

viśva̱m it sava̍naṁ su̱tam indro̱ madā̍ya gacchati | vṛ̱tra̱hā soma̍pītaye ||

**1.16.9**

semaṁ na̱ḥ kāma̱m ā pṛ̍ṇa̱ gobhi̱r aśvai̍ḥ śatakrato | stavā̍ma tvā svā̱dhya̍ḥ ||

**1.17.1**

indrā̱varu̍ṇayor a̱haṁ sa̱mrājo̱r ava̱ ā vṛ̍ṇe | tā no̍ mṛḻāta ī̱dṛśe̍ ||

**1.17.2**

gantā̍rā̱ hi stho 'va̍se̱ hava̱ṁ vipra̍sya̱ māva̍taḥ | dha̱rtārā̍ carṣaṇī̱nām ||

**1.17.3**

a̱nu̱kā̱maṁ ta̍rpayethā̱m indrā̍varuṇa rā̱ya ā | tā vā̱ṁ nedi̍ṣṭham īmahe ||

**1.17.4**

yu̱vāku̱ hi śacī̍nāṁ yu̱vāku̍ sumatī̱nām | bhū̱yāma̍ vāja̱dāvnā̍m ||

**1.17.5**

indra̍ḥ sahasra̱dāvnā̱ṁ varu̍ṇa̱ḥ śaṁsyā̍nām | kratu̍r bhavaty u̱kthya̍ḥ ||

**1.17.6**

tayo̱r id ava̍sā va̱yaṁ sa̱nema̱ ni ca̍ dhīmahi | syād u̱ta pra̱reca̍nam ||

**1.17.7**

indrā̍varuṇa vām a̱haṁ hu̱ve ci̱trāya̱ rādha̍se | a̱smān su ji̱gyuṣa̍s kṛtam ||

**1.17.8**

indrā̍varuṇa̱ nū nu vā̱ṁ siṣā̍santīṣu dhī̱ṣv ā | a̱smabhya̱ṁ śarma̍ yacchatam ||

**1.17.9**

pra vā̍m aśnotu suṣṭu̱tir indrā̍varuṇa̱ yāṁ hu̱ve | yām ṛ̱dhāthe̍ sa̱dhastu̍tim ||

**1.18.1**

so̱māna̱ṁ svara̍ṇaṁ kṛṇu̱hi bra̍hmaṇas pate | ka̱kṣīva̍nta̱ṁ ya au̍śi̱jaḥ ||

**1.18.2**

yo re̱vān yo a̍mīva̱hā va̍su̱vit pu̍ṣṭi̱vardha̍naḥ | sa na̍ḥ siṣaktu̱ yas tu̱raḥ ||

**1.18.3**

mā na̱ḥ śaṁso̱ ara̍ruṣo dhū̱rtiḥ praṇa̱ṅ martya̍sya | rakṣā̍ ṇo brahmaṇas pate ||

**1.18.4**

sa ghā̍ vī̱ro na ri̍ṣyati̱ yam indro̱ brahma̍ṇa̱s pati̍ḥ | somo̍ hi̱noti̱ martya̍m ||

**1.18.5**

tvaṁ tam bra̍hmaṇas pate̱ soma̱ indra̍ś ca̱ martya̍m | dakṣi̍ṇā pā̱tv aṁha̍saḥ ||

**1.18.6**

sada̍sa̱s pati̱m adbhu̍tam pri̱yam indra̍sya̱ kāmya̍m | sa̱nim me̱dhām a̍yāsiṣam ||

**1.18.7**

yasmā̍d ṛ̱te na sidhya̍ti ya̱jño vi̍pa̱ścita̍ś ca̱na | sa dhī̱nāṁ yoga̍m invati ||

**1.18.8**

ād ṛ̍dhnoti ha̱viṣkṛ̍ti̱m prāñca̍ṁ kṛṇoty adhva̱ram | hotrā̍ de̱veṣu̍ gacchati ||

**1.18.9**

narā̱śaṁsa̍ṁ su̱dhṛṣṭa̍ma̱m apa̍śyaṁ sa̱pratha̍stamam | di̱vo na sadma̍makhasam ||

**1.19.1**

prati̱ tyaṁ cāru̍m adhva̱raṁ go̍pī̱thāya̱ pra hū̍yase | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.2**

na̱hi de̱vo na martyo̍ ma̱has tava̱ kratu̍m pa̱raḥ | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.3**

ye ma̱ho raja̍so vi̱dur viśve̍ de̱vāso̍ a̱druha̍ḥ | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.4**

ya u̱grā a̱rkam ā̍nṛ̱cur anā̍dhṛṣṭāsa̱ oja̍sā | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.5**

ye śu̱bhrā gho̱rava̍rpasaḥ sukṣa̱trāso̍ ri̱śāda̍saḥ | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.6**

ye nāka̱syādhi̍ roca̱ne di̱vi de̱vāsa̱ āsa̍te | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.7**

ya ī̱ṅkhaya̍nti̱ parva̍tān ti̱raḥ sa̍mu̱dram a̍rṇa̱vam | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.8**

ā ye ta̱nvanti̍ ra̱śmibhi̍s ti̱raḥ sa̍mu̱dram oja̍sā | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.19.9**

a̱bhi tvā̍ pū̱rvapī̍taye sṛ̱jāmi̍ so̱myam madhu̍ | ma̱rudbhi̍r agna̱ ā ga̍hi ||

**1.20.1**

a̱yaṁ de̱vāya̱ janma̍ne̱ stomo̱ vipre̍bhir āsa̱yā | akā̍ri ratna̱dhāta̍maḥ ||

**1.20.2**

ya indrā̍ya vaco̱yujā̍ tata̱kṣur mana̍sā̱ harī̍ | śamī̍bhir ya̱jñam ā̍śata ||

**1.20.3**

takṣa̱n nāsa̍tyābhyā̱m pari̍jmānaṁ su̱khaṁ ratha̍m | takṣa̍n dhe̱nuṁ sa̍ba̱rdughā̍m ||

**1.20.4**

yuvā̍nā pi̱tarā̱ puna̍ḥ sa̱tyama̍ntrā ṛjū̱yava̍ḥ | ṛ̱bhavo̍ vi̱ṣṭy a̍krata ||

**1.20.5**

saṁ vo̱ madā̍so agma̱tendre̍ṇa ca ma̱rutva̍tā | ā̱di̱tyebhi̍ś ca̱ rāja̍bhiḥ ||

**1.20.6**

u̱ta tyaṁ ca̍ma̱saṁ nava̱ṁ tvaṣṭu̍r de̱vasya̱ niṣkṛ̍tam | aka̍rta ca̱tura̱ḥ puna̍ḥ ||

**1.20.7**

te no̱ ratnā̍ni dhattana̱ trir ā sāptā̍ni sunva̱te | eka̍m-ekaṁ suśa̱stibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.20.8**

adhā̍rayanta̱ vahna̱yo 'bha̍janta sukṛ̱tyayā̍ | bhā̱gaṁ de̱veṣu̍ ya̱jñiya̍m ||

**1.21.1**

i̱hendrā̱gnī upa̍ hvaye̱ tayo̱r it stoma̍m uśmasi | tā soma̍ṁ soma̱pāta̍mā ||

**1.21.2**

tā ya̱jñeṣu̱ pra śa̍ṁsatendrā̱gnī śu̍mbhatā naraḥ | tā gā̍ya̱treṣu̍ gāyata ||

**1.21.3**

tā mi̱trasya̱ praśa̍staya indrā̱gnī tā ha̍vāmahe | so̱ma̱pā soma̍pītaye ||

**1.21.4**

u̱grā santā̍ havāmaha̱ upe̱daṁ sava̍naṁ su̱tam | i̱ndrā̱gnī eha ga̍cchatām ||

**1.21.5**

tā ma̱hāntā̱ sada̱spatī̱ indrā̍gnī̱ rakṣa̍ ubjatam | apra̍jāḥ santv a̱triṇa̍ḥ ||

**1.21.6**

tena̍ sa̱tyena̍ jāgṛta̱m adhi̍ prace̱tune̍ pa̱de | indrā̍gnī̱ śarma̍ yacchatam ||

**1.22.1**

prā̱ta̱ryujā̱ vi bo̍dhayā̱śvinā̱v eha ga̍cchatām | a̱sya soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||

**1.22.2**

yā su̱rathā̍ ra̱thīta̍mo̱bhā de̱vā di̍vi̱spṛśā̍ | a̱śvinā̱ tā ha̍vāmahe ||

**1.22.3**

yā vā̱ṁ kaśā̱ madhu̍ma̱ty aśvi̍nā sū̱nṛtā̍vatī | tayā̍ ya̱jñam mi̍mikṣatam ||

**1.22.4**

na̱hi vā̱m asti̍ dūra̱ke yatrā̱ rathe̍na̱ gaccha̍thaḥ | aśvi̍nā so̱mino̍ gṛ̱ham ||

**1.22.5**

hira̍ṇyapāṇim ū̱taye̍ savi̱tāra̱m upa̍ hvaye | sa cettā̍ de̱vatā̍ pa̱dam ||

**1.22.6**

a̱pāṁ napā̍ta̱m ava̍se savi̱tāra̱m upa̍ stuhi | tasya̍ vra̱tāny u̍śmasi ||

**1.22.7**

vi̱bha̱ktāra̍ṁ havāmahe̱ vaso̍ś ci̱trasya̱ rādha̍saḥ | sa̱vi̱tāra̍ṁ nṛ̱cakṣa̍sam ||

**1.22.8**

sakhā̍ya̱ ā ni ṣī̍data savi̱tā stomyo̱ nu na̍ḥ | dātā̱ rādhā̍ṁsi śumbhati ||

**1.22.9**

agne̱ patnī̍r i̱hā va̍ha de̱vānā̍m uśa̱tīr upa̍ | tvaṣṭā̍ra̱ṁ soma̍pītaye ||

**1.22.10**

ā gnā a̍gna i̱hāva̍se̱ hotrā̍ṁ yaviṣṭha̱ bhāra̍tīm | varū̍trīṁ dhi̱ṣaṇā̍ṁ vaha ||

**1.22.11**

a̱bhi no̍ de̱vīr ava̍sā ma̱haḥ śarma̍ṇā nṛ̱patnī̍ḥ | acchi̍nnapatrāḥ sacantām ||

**1.22.12**

i̱hendrā̱ṇīm upa̍ hvaye varuṇā̱nīṁ sva̱staye̍ | a̱gnāyī̱ṁ soma̍pītaye ||

**1.22.13**

ma̱hī dyauḥ pṛ̍thi̱vī ca̍ na i̱maṁ ya̱jñam mi̍mikṣatām | pi̱pṛ̱tāṁ no̱ bharī̍mabhiḥ ||

**1.22.14**

tayo̱r id ghṛ̱tava̱t payo̱ viprā̍ rihanti dhī̱tibhi̍ḥ | ga̱ndha̱rvasya̍ dhru̱ve pa̱de ||

**1.22.15**

syo̱nā pṛ̍thivi bhavānṛkṣa̱rā ni̱veśa̍nī | yacchā̍ na̱ḥ śarma̍ sa̱pratha̍ḥ ||

**1.22.16**

ato̍ de̱vā a̍vantu no̱ yato̱ viṣṇu̍r vicakra̱me | pṛ̱thi̱vyāḥ sa̱pta dhāma̍bhiḥ ||

**1.22.17**

i̱daṁ viṣṇu̱r vi ca̍krame tre̱dhā ni da̍dhe pa̱dam | samū̍ḻham asya pāṁsu̱re ||

**1.22.18**

trīṇi̍ pa̱dā vi ca̍krame̱ viṣṇu̍r go̱pā adā̍bhyaḥ | ato̱ dharmā̍ṇi dhā̱raya̍n ||

**1.22.19**

viṣṇo̱ḥ karmā̍ṇi paśyata̱ yato̍ vra̱tāni̍ paspa̱śe | indra̍sya̱ yujya̱ḥ sakhā̍ ||

**1.22.20**

tad viṣṇo̍ḥ para̱mam pa̱daṁ sadā̍ paśyanti sū̱raya̍ḥ | di̱vī̍va̱ cakṣu̱r āta̍tam ||

**1.22.21**

tad viprā̍so vipa̱nyavo̍ jāgṛ̱vāṁsa̱ḥ sam i̍ndhate | viṣṇo̱r yat pa̍ra̱mam pa̱dam ||

**1.23.1**

tī̱vrāḥ somā̍sa̱ ā ga̍hy ā̱śīrva̍ntaḥ su̱tā i̱me | vāyo̱ tān prasthi̍tān piba ||

**1.23.2**

u̱bhā de̱vā di̍vi̱spṛśe̍ndravā̱yū ha̍vāmahe | a̱sya soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||

**1.23.3**

i̱ndra̱vā̱yū ma̍no̱juvā̱ viprā̍ havanta ū̱taye̍ | sa̱ha̱srā̱kṣā dhi̱yas patī̍ ||

**1.23.4**

mi̱traṁ va̱yaṁ ha̍vāmahe̱ varu̍ṇa̱ṁ soma̍pītaye | ja̱jñā̱nā pū̱tada̍kṣasā ||

**1.23.5**

ṛ̱tena̱ yāv ṛ̍tā̱vṛdhā̍v ṛ̱tasya̱ jyoti̍ṣa̱s patī̍ | tā mi̱trāvaru̍ṇā huve ||

**1.23.6**

varu̍ṇaḥ prāvi̱tā bhu̍van mi̱tro viśvā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | kara̍tāṁ naḥ su̱rādha̍saḥ ||

**1.23.7**

ma̱rutva̍ntaṁ havāmaha̱ indra̱m ā soma̍pītaye | sa̱jūr ga̱ṇena̍ tṛmpatu ||

**1.23.8**

indra̍jyeṣṭhā̱ maru̍dgaṇā̱ devā̍sa̱ḥ pūṣa̍rātayaḥ | viśve̱ mama̍ śrutā̱ hava̍m ||

**1.23.9**

ha̱ta vṛ̱traṁ su̍dānava̱ indre̍ṇa̱ saha̍sā yu̱jā | mā no̍ du̱ḥśaṁsa̍ īśata ||

**1.23.10**

viśvā̍n de̱vān ha̍vāmahe ma̱ruta̱ḥ soma̍pītaye | u̱grā hi pṛśni̍mātaraḥ ||

**1.23.11**

jaya̍tām iva tanya̱tur ma̱rutā̍m eti dhṛṣṇu̱yā | yac chubha̍ṁ yā̱thanā̍ naraḥ ||

**1.23.12**

ha̱skā̱rād vi̱dyuta̱s pary ato̍ jā̱tā a̍vantu naḥ | ma̱ruto̍ mṛḻayantu naḥ ||

**1.23.13**

ā pū̍ṣañ ci̱traba̍rhiṣa̱m āghṛ̍ṇe dha̱ruṇa̍ṁ di̱vaḥ | ājā̍ na̱ṣṭaṁ yathā̍ pa̱śum ||

**1.23.14**

pū̱ṣā rājā̍na̱m āghṛ̍ṇi̱r apa̍gūḻha̱ṁ guhā̍ hi̱tam | avi̍ndac ci̱traba̍rhiṣam ||

**1.23.15**

u̱to sa mahya̱m indu̍bhi̱ḥ ṣaḍ yu̱ktām̐ a̍nu̱seṣi̍dhat | gobhi̱r yava̱ṁ na ca̍rkṛṣat ||

**1.23.16**

a̱mbayo̍ ya̱nty adhva̍bhir jā̱mayo̍ adhvarīya̱tām | pṛ̱ñca̱tīr madhu̍nā̱ paya̍ḥ ||

**1.23.17**

a̱mūr yā upa̱ sūrye̱ yābhi̍r vā̱ sūrya̍ḥ sa̱ha | tā no̍ hinvantv adhva̱ram ||

**1.23.18**

a̱po de̱vīr upa̍ hvaye̱ yatra̱ gāva̱ḥ piba̍nti naḥ | sindhu̍bhya̱ḥ kartva̍ṁ ha̱viḥ ||

**1.23.19**

a̱psv a1̱̍ntar a̱mṛta̍m a̱psu bhe̍ṣa̱jam a̱pām u̱ta praśa̍staye | devā̱ bhava̍ta vā̱jina̍ḥ ||

**1.23.20**

a̱psu me̱ somo̍ abravīd a̱ntar viśvā̍ni bheṣa̱jā | a̱gniṁ ca̍ vi̱śvaśa̍mbhuva̱m āpa̍ś ca vi̱śvabhe̍ṣajīḥ ||

**1.23.21**

āpa̍ḥ pṛṇī̱ta bhe̍ṣa̱jaṁ varū̍thaṁ ta̱nve̱3̱̍ mama̍ | jyok ca̱ sūrya̍ṁ dṛ̱śe ||

**1.23.22**

i̱dam ā̍pa̱ḥ pra va̍hata̱ yat kiṁ ca̍ duri̱tam mayi̍ | yad vā̱ham a̍bhidu̱droha̱ yad vā̍ śe̱pa u̱tānṛ̍tam ||

**1.23.23**

āpo̍ a̱dyānv a̍cāriṣa̱ṁ rase̍na̱ sam a̍gasmahi | paya̍svān agna̱ ā ga̍hi̱ tam mā̱ saṁ sṛ̍ja̱ varca̍sā ||

**1.23.24**

sam mā̍gne̱ varca̍sā sṛja̱ sam pra̱jayā̱ sam āyu̍ṣā | vi̱dyur me̍ asya de̱vā indro̍ vidyāt sa̱ha ṛṣi̍bhiḥ ||

**1.24.1**

kasya̍ nū̱naṁ ka̍ta̱masyā̱mṛtā̍nā̱m manā̍mahe̱ cāru̍ de̱vasya̱ nāma̍ | ko no̍ ma̱hyā adi̍taye̱ puna̍r dāt pi̱tara̍ṁ ca dṛ̱śeya̍m mā̱tara̍ṁ ca ||

**1.24.2**

a̱gner va̱yam pra̍tha̱masyā̱mṛtā̍nā̱m manā̍mahe̱ cāru̍ de̱vasya̱ nāma̍ | sa no̍ ma̱hyā adi̍taye̱ puna̍r dāt pi̱tara̍ṁ ca dṛ̱śeya̍m mā̱tara̍ṁ ca ||

**1.24.3**

a̱bhi tvā̍ deva savita̱r īśā̍na̱ṁ vāryā̍ṇām | sadā̍van bhā̱gam ī̍mahe ||

**1.24.4**

yaś ci̱d dhi ta̍ i̱tthā bhaga̍ḥ śaśamā̱naḥ pu̱rā ni̱daḥ | a̱dve̱ṣo hasta̍yor da̱dhe ||

**1.24.5**

bhaga̍bhaktasya te va̱yam ud a̍śema̱ tavāva̍sā | mū̱rdhāna̍ṁ rā̱ya ā̱rabhe̍ ||

**1.24.6**

na̱hi te̍ kṣa̱traṁ na saho̱ na ma̱nyuṁ vaya̍ś ca̱nāmī pa̱taya̍nta ā̱puḥ | nemā āpo̍ animi̱ṣaṁ cara̍ntī̱r na ye vāta̍sya prami̱nanty abhva̍m ||

**1.24.7**

a̱bu̱dhne rājā̱ varu̍ṇo̱ vana̍syo̱rdhvaṁ stūpa̍ṁ dadate pū̱tada̍kṣaḥ ||  nī̱cīnā̍ḥ sthur u̱pari̍ bu̱dhna e̍ṣām a̱sme a̱ntar nihi̍tāḥ ke̱tava̍ḥ syuḥ ||

**1.24.8**

u̱ruṁ hi rājā̱ varu̍ṇaś ca̱kāra̱ sūryā̍ya̱ panthā̱m anve̍ta̱vā u̍ | a̱pade̱ pādā̱ prati̍dhātave 'kar u̱tāpa̍va̱ktā hṛ̍dayā̱vidha̍ś cit ||

**1.24.9**

śa̱taṁ te̍ rājan bhi̱ṣaja̍ḥ sa̱hasra̍m u̱rvī ga̍bhī̱rā su̍ma̱tiṣ ṭe̍ astu | bādha̍sva dū̱re nirṛ̍tim parā̱caiḥ kṛ̱taṁ ci̱d ena̱ḥ pra mu̍mugdhy a̱smat ||

**1.24.10**

a̱mī ya ṛkṣā̱ nihi̍tāsa u̱ccā nakta̱ṁ dadṛ̍śre̱ kuha̍ ci̱d dive̍yuḥ | ada̍bdhāni̱ varu̍ṇasya vra̱tāni̍ vi̱cāka̍śac ca̱ndramā̱ nakta̍m eti ||

**1.24.11**

tat tvā̍ yāmi̱ brahma̍ṇā̱ vanda̍māna̱s tad ā śā̍ste̱ yaja̍māno ha̱virbhi̍ḥ | ahe̍ḻamāno varuṇe̱ha bo̱dhy uru̍śaṁsa̱ mā na̱ āyu̱ḥ pra mo̍ṣīḥ ||

**1.24.12**

tad in nakta̱ṁ tad divā̱ mahya̍m āhu̱s tad a̱yaṁ keto̍ hṛ̱da ā vi ca̍ṣṭe | śuna̱ḥśepo̱ yam ahva̍d gṛbhī̱taḥ so a̱smān rājā̱ varu̍ṇo mumoktu ||

**1.24.13**

śuna̱ḥśepo̱ hy ahva̍d gṛbhī̱tas tri̱ṣv ā̍di̱tyaṁ dru̍pa̱deṣu̍ ba̱ddhaḥ | avai̍na̱ṁ rājā̱ varu̍ṇaḥ sasṛjyād vi̱dvām̐ ada̍bdho̱ vi mu̍moktu̱ pāśā̍n ||

**1.24.14**

ava̍ te̱ heḻo̍ varuṇa̱ namo̍bhi̱r ava̍ ya̱jñebhi̍r īmahe ha̱virbhi̍ḥ | kṣaya̍nn a̱smabhya̍m asura pracetā̱ rāja̱nn enā̍ṁsi śiśrathaḥ kṛ̱tāni̍ ||

**1.24.15**

ud u̍tta̱maṁ va̍ruṇa̱ pāśa̍m a̱smad avā̍dha̱maṁ vi ma̍dhya̱maṁ śra̍thāya | athā̍ va̱yam ā̍ditya vra̱te tavānā̍gaso̱ adi̍taye syāma ||

**1.25.1**

yac ci̱d dhi te̱ viśo̍ yathā̱ pra de̍va varuṇa vra̱tam | mi̱nī̱masi̱ dyavi̍-dyavi ||

**1.25.2**

mā no̍ va̱dhāya̍ ha̱tnave̍ jihīḻā̱nasya̍ rīradhaḥ | mā hṛ̍ṇā̱nasya̍ ma̱nyave̍ ||

**1.25.3**

vi mṛ̍ḻī̱kāya̍ te̱ mano̍ ra̱thīr aśva̱ṁ na saṁdi̍tam | gī̱rbhir va̍ruṇa sīmahi ||

**1.25.4**

parā̱ hi me̱ vima̍nyava̱ḥ pata̍nti̱ vasya̍ïṣṭaye | vayo̱ na va̍sa̱tīr upa̍ ||

**1.25.5**

ka̱dā kṣa̍tra̱śriya̱ṁ nara̱m ā varu̍ṇaṁ karāmahe | mṛ̱ḻī̱kāyo̍ru̱cakṣa̍sam ||

**1.25.6**

tad it sa̍mā̱nam ā̍śāte̱ vena̍ntā̱ na pra yu̍cchataḥ | dhṛ̱tavra̍tāya dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**1.25.7**

vedā̱ yo vī̱nām pa̱dam a̱ntari̍kṣeṇa̱ pata̍tām | veda̍ nā̱vaḥ sa̍mu̱driya̍ḥ ||

**1.25.8**

veda̍ mā̱so dhṛ̱tavra̍to̱ dvāda̍śa pra̱jāva̍taḥ | vedā̱ ya u̍pa̱jāya̍te ||

**1.25.9**

veda̱ vāta̍sya varta̱nim u̱ror ṛ̱ṣvasya̍ bṛha̱taḥ | vedā̱ ye a̱dhyāsa̍te ||

**1.25.10**

ni ṣa̍sāda dhṛ̱tavra̍to̱ varu̍ṇaḥ pa̱styā̱3̱̍sv ā | sāmrā̍jyāya su̱kratu̍ḥ ||

**1.25.11**

ato̱ viśvā̱ny adbhu̍tā ciki̱tvām̐ a̱bhi pa̍śyati | kṛ̱tāni̱ yā ca̱ kartvā̍ ||

**1.25.12**

sa no̍ vi̱śvāhā̍ su̱kratu̍r ādi̱tyaḥ su̱pathā̍ karat | pra ṇa̱ āyū̍ṁṣi tāriṣat ||

**1.25.13**

bibhra̍d drā̱piṁ hi̍ra̱ṇyaya̱ṁ varu̍ṇo vasta ni̱rṇija̍m | pari̱ spaśo̱ ni ṣe̍dire ||

**1.25.14**

na yaṁ dipsa̍nti di̱psavo̱ na druhvā̍ṇo̱ janā̍nām | na de̱vam a̱bhimā̍tayaḥ ||

**1.25.15**

u̱ta yo mānu̍ṣe̱ṣv ā yaśa̍ś ca̱kre asā̱my ā | a̱smāka̍m u̱dare̱ṣv ā ||

**1.25.16**

parā̍ me yanti dhī̱tayo̱ gāvo̱ na gavyū̍tī̱r anu̍ | i̱cchantī̍r uru̱cakṣa̍sam ||

**1.25.17**

saṁ nu vo̍cāvahai̱ puna̱r yato̍ me̱ madhv ābhṛ̍tam | hote̍va̱ kṣada̍se pri̱yam ||

**1.25.18**

darśa̱ṁ nu vi̱śvada̍rśata̱ṁ darśa̱ṁ ratha̱m adhi̱ kṣami̍ | e̱tā ju̍ṣata me̱ gira̍ḥ ||

**1.25.19**

i̱mam me̍ varuṇa śrudhī̱ hava̍m a̱dyā ca̍ mṛḻaya | tvām a̍va̱syur ā ca̍ke ||

**1.25.20**

tvaṁ viśva̍sya medhira di̱vaś ca̱ gmaś ca̍ rājasi | sa yāma̍ni̱ prati̍ śrudhi ||

**1.25.21**

ud u̍tta̱mam mu̍mugdhi no̱ vi pāśa̍m madhya̱maṁ cṛ̍ta | avā̍dha̱māni̍ jī̱vase̍ ||

**1.26.1**

vasi̍ṣvā̱ hi mi̍yedhya̱ vastrā̍ṇy ūrjām pate | semaṁ no̍ adhva̱raṁ ya̍ja ||

**1.26.2**

ni no̱ hotā̍ vare̍ṇya̱ḥ sadā̍ yaviṣṭha̱ manma̍bhiḥ | agne̍ di̱vitma̍tā̱ vaca̍ḥ ||

**1.26.3**

ā hi ṣmā̍ sū̱nave̍ pi̱tāpir yaja̍ty ā̱paye̍ | sakhā̱ sakhye̱ vare̍ṇyaḥ ||

**1.26.4**

ā no̍ ba̱rhī ri̱śāda̍so̱ varu̍ṇo mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā | sīda̍ntu̱ manu̍ṣo yathā ||

**1.26.5**

pūrvya̍ hotar a̱sya no̱ manda̍sva sa̱khyasya̍ ca | i̱mā u̱ ṣu śru̍dhī̱ gira̍ḥ ||

**1.26.6**

yac ci̱d dhi śaśva̍tā̱ tanā̍ de̱vaṁ-de̍va̱ṁ yajā̍mahe | tve id dhū̍yate ha̱viḥ ||

**1.26.7**

pri̱yo no̍ astu vi̱śpati̱r hotā̍ ma̱ndro vare̍ṇyaḥ | pri̱yāḥ sva̱gnayo̍ va̱yam ||

**1.26.8**

sva̱gnayo̱ hi vārya̍ṁ de̱vāso̍ dadhi̱re ca̍ naḥ | sva̱gnayo̍ manāmahe ||

**1.26.9**

athā̍ na u̱bhaye̍ṣā̱m amṛ̍ta̱ martyā̍nām | mi̱thaḥ sa̍ntu̱ praśa̍stayaḥ ||

**1.26.10**

viśve̍bhir agne a̱gnibhi̍r i̱maṁ ya̱jñam i̱daṁ vaca̍ḥ | cano̍ dhāḥ sahaso yaho ||

**1.27.1**

aśva̱ṁ na tvā̱ vāra̍vantaṁ va̱ndadhyā̍ a̱gniṁ namo̍bhiḥ | sa̱mrāja̍ntam adhva̱rāṇā̍m ||

**1.27.2**

sa ghā̍ naḥ sū̱nuḥ śava̍sā pṛ̱thupra̍gāmā su̱śeva̍ḥ | mī̱ḍhvām̐ a̱smāka̍m babhūyāt ||

**1.27.3**

sa no̍ dū̱rāc cā̱sāc ca̱ ni martyā̍d aghā̱yoḥ | pā̱hi sada̱m id vi̱śvāyu̍ḥ ||

**1.27.4**

i̱mam ū̱ ṣu tvam a̱smāka̍ṁ sa̱niṁ gā̍ya̱traṁ navyā̍ṁsam | agne̍ de̱veṣu̱ pra vo̍caḥ ||

**1.27.5**

ā no̍ bhaja para̱meṣv ā vāje̍ṣu madhya̱meṣu̍ | śikṣā̱ vasvo̱ anta̍masya ||

**1.27.6**

vi̱bha̱ktāsi̍ citrabhāno̱ sindho̍r ū̱rmā u̍pā̱ka ā | sa̱dyo dā̱śuṣe̍ kṣarasi ||

**1.27.7**

yam a̍gne pṛ̱tsu martya̱m avā̱ vāje̍ṣu̱ yaṁ ju̱nāḥ | sa yantā̱ śaśva̍tī̱r iṣa̍ḥ ||

**1.27.8**

naki̍r asya sahantya parye̱tā kaya̍sya cit | vājo̍ asti śra̱vāyya̍ḥ ||

**1.27.9**

sa vāja̍ṁ vi̱śvaca̍rṣaṇi̱r arva̍dbhir astu̱ taru̍tā | vipre̍bhir astu̱ sani̍tā ||

**1.27.10**

jarā̍bodha̱ tad vi̍viḍḍhi vi̱śe-vi̍śe ya̱jñiyā̍ya | stoma̍ṁ ru̱drāya̱ dṛśī̍kam ||

**1.27.11**

sa no̍ ma̱hām̐ a̍nimā̱no dhū̱make̍tuḥ puruśca̱ndraḥ | dhi̱ye vājā̍ya hinvatu ||

**1.27.12**

sa re̱vām̐ i̍va vi̱śpati̱r daivya̍ḥ ke̱tuḥ śṛ̍ṇotu naḥ | u̱kthair a̱gnir bṛ̱hadbhā̍nuḥ ||

**1.27.13**

namo̍ ma̱hadbhyo̱ namo̍ arbha̱kebhyo̱ namo̱ yuva̍bhyo̱ nama̍ āśi̱nebhya̍ḥ | yajā̍ma de̱vān yadi̍ śa̱knavā̍ma̱ mā jyāya̍sa̱ḥ śaṁsa̱m ā vṛ̍kṣi devāḥ ||

**1.28.1**

yatra̱ grāvā̍ pṛ̱thubu̍dhna ū̱rdhvo bhava̍ti̱ sota̍ve | u̱lūkha̍lasutānā̱m aved v i̍ndra jalgulaḥ ||

**1.28.2**

yatra̱ dvāv i̍va ja̱ghanā̍dhiṣava̱ṇyā̍ kṛ̱tā | u̱lūkha̍lasutānā̱m aved v i̍ndra jalgulaḥ ||

**1.28.3**

yatra̱ nāry a̍pacya̱vam u̍pacya̱vaṁ ca̱ śikṣa̍te | u̱lūkha̍lasutānā̱m aved v i̍ndra jalgulaḥ ||

**1.28.4**

yatra̱ manthā̍ṁ viba̱dhnate̍ ra̱śmīn yami̍ta̱vā i̍va | u̱lūkha̍lasutānā̱m aved v i̍ndra jalgulaḥ ||

**1.28.5**

yac ci̱d dhi tvaṁ gṛ̱he-gṛ̍ha̱ ulū̍khalaka yu̱jyase̍ | i̱ha dyu̱matta̍maṁ vada̱ jaya̍tām iva dundu̱bhiḥ ||

**1.28.6**

u̱ta sma̍ te vanaspate̱ vāto̱ vi vā̱ty agra̱m it | atho̱ indrā̍ya̱ pāta̍ve su̱nu soma̍m ulūkhala ||

**1.28.7**

ā̱ya̱jī vā̍ja̱sāta̍mā̱ tā hy u1̱̍ccā vi̍jarbhṛ̱taḥ | harī̍ i̱vāndhā̍ṁsi̱ bapsa̍tā ||

**1.28.8**

tā no̍ a̱dya va̍naspatī ṛ̱ṣvāv ṛ̱ṣvebhi̍ḥ so̱tṛbhi̍ḥ | indrā̍ya̱ madhu̍mat sutam ||

**1.28.9**

uc chi̱ṣṭaṁ ca̱mvo̍r bhara̱ soma̍m pa̱vitra̱ ā sṛ̍ja | ni dhe̍hi̱ gor adhi̍ tva̱ci ||

**1.29.1**

yac ci̱d dhi sa̍tya somapā anāśa̱stā i̍va̱ smasi̍ | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.29.2**

śipri̍n vājānām pate̱ śacī̍va̱s tava̍ da̱ṁsanā̍ | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.29.3**

ni ṣvā̍payā mithū̱dṛśā̍ sa̱stām abu̍dhyamāne | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.29.4**

sa̱santu̱ tyā arā̍tayo̱ bodha̍ntu śūra rā̱taya̍ḥ | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.29.5**

sam i̍ndra garda̱bham mṛ̍ṇa nu̱vanta̍m pā̱payā̍mu̱yā | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.29.6**

patā̍ti kuṇḍṛ̱ṇācyā̍ dū̱raṁ vāto̱ vanā̱d adhi̍ | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.29.7**

sarva̍m parikro̱śaṁ ja̍hi ja̱mbhayā̍ kṛkadā̱śva̍m | ā tū na̍ indra śaṁsaya̱ goṣv aśve̍ṣu śu̱bhriṣu̍ sa̱hasre̍ṣu tuvīmagha ||

**1.30.1**

ā va̱ indra̱ṁ krivi̍ṁ yathā vāja̱yanta̍ḥ śa̱takra̍tum | maṁhi̍ṣṭhaṁ siñca̱ indu̍bhiḥ ||

**1.30.2**

śa̱taṁ vā̱ yaḥ śucī̍nāṁ sa̱hasra̍ṁ vā̱ samā̍śirām | ed u̍ ni̱mnaṁ na rī̍yate ||

**1.30.3**

saṁ yan madā̍ya śu̱ṣmiṇa̍ e̱nā hy a̍syo̱dare̍ | sa̱mu̱dro na vyaco̍ da̱dhe ||

**1.30.4**

a̱yam u̍ te̱ sam a̍tasi ka̱pota̍ iva garbha̱dhim | vaca̱s tac ci̍n na ohase ||

**1.30.5**

sto̱traṁ rā̍dhānām pate̱ girvā̍ho vīra̱ yasya̍ te | vibhū̍tir astu sū̱nṛtā̍ ||

**1.30.6**

ū̱rdhvas ti̍ṣṭhā na ū̱taye̱ 'smin vāje̍ śatakrato | sam a̱nyeṣu̍ bravāvahai ||

**1.30.7**

yoge̍-yoge ta̱vasta̍ra̱ṁ vāje̍-vāje havāmahe | sakhā̍ya̱ indra̍m ū̱taye̍ ||

**1.30.8**

ā ghā̍ gama̱d yadi̱ śrava̍t saha̱sriṇī̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ | vāje̍bhi̱r upa̍ no̱ hava̍m ||

**1.30.9**

anu̍ pra̱tnasyauka̍so hu̱ve tu̍vipra̱tiṁ nara̍m | yaṁ te̱ pūrva̍m pi̱tā hu̱ve ||

**1.30.10**

taṁ tvā̍ va̱yaṁ vi̍śvavā̱rā śā̍smahe puruhūta | sakhe̍ vaso jari̱tṛbhya̍ḥ ||

**1.30.11**

a̱smāka̍ṁ śi̱priṇī̍nā̱ṁ soma̍pāḥ soma̱pāvnā̍m | sakhe̍ vajri̱n sakhī̍nām ||

**1.30.12**

tathā̱ tad a̍stu somapā̱ḥ sakhe̍ vajri̱n tathā̍ kṛṇu | yathā̍ ta u̱śmasī̱ṣṭaye̍ ||

**1.30.13**

re̱vatī̍r naḥ sadha̱māda̱ indre̍ santu tu̱vivā̍jāḥ | kṣu̱manto̱ yābhi̱r made̍ma ||

**1.30.14**

ā gha̱ tvāvā̱n tmanā̱ptaḥ sto̱tṛbhyo̍ dhṛṣṇav iyā̱naḥ | ṛ̱ṇor akṣa̱ṁ na ca̱kryo̍ḥ ||

**1.30.15**

ā yad duva̍ḥ śatakrata̱v ā kāma̍ṁ jaritṝ̱ṇām | ṛ̱ṇor akṣa̱ṁ na śacī̍bhiḥ ||

**1.30.16**

śaśva̱d indra̱ḥ popru̍thadbhir jigāya̱ nāna̍dadbhi̱ḥ śāśva̍sadbhi̱r dhanā̍ni | sa no̍ hiraṇyara̱thaṁ da̱ṁsanā̍vā̱n sa na̍ḥ sani̱tā sa̱naye̱ sa no̍ 'dāt ||

**1.30.17**

āśvi̍nā̱v aśvā̍vatye̱ṣā yā̍ta̱ṁ śavī̍rayā | goma̍d dasrā̱ hira̍ṇyavat ||

**1.30.18**

sa̱mā̱nayo̍jano̱ hi vā̱ṁ ratho̍ dasrā̱v ama̍rtyaḥ | sa̱mu̱dre a̍śvi̱neya̍te ||

**1.30.19**

ny a1̱̍ghnyasya̍ mū̱rdhani̍ ca̱kraṁ ratha̍sya yemathuḥ | pari̱ dyām a̱nyad ī̍yate ||

**1.30.20**

kas ta̍ uṣaḥ kadhapriye bhu̱je marto̍ amartye | kaṁ na̍kṣase vibhāvari ||

**1.30.21**

va̱yaṁ hi te̱ ama̍nma̱hy āntā̱d ā pa̍rā̱kāt | aśve̱ na ci̍tre aruṣi ||

**1.30.22**

tvaṁ tyebhi̱r ā ga̍hi̱ vāje̍bhir duhitar divaḥ | a̱sme ra̱yiṁ ni dhā̍raya ||

**1.31.1**

tvam a̍gne pratha̱mo aṅgi̍rā̱ ṛṣi̍r de̱vo de̱vānā̍m abhavaḥ śi̱vaḥ sakhā̍ | tava̍ vra̱te ka̱vayo̍ vidma̱nāpa̱so 'jā̍yanta ma̱ruto̱ bhrāja̍dṛṣṭayaḥ ||

**1.31.2**

tvam a̍gne pratha̱mo aṅgi̍rastamaḥ ka̱vir de̱vānā̱m pari̍ bhūṣasi vra̱tam | vi̱bhur viśva̍smai̱ bhuva̍nāya̱ medhi̍ro dvimā̱tā śa̱yuḥ ka̍ti̱dhā ci̍d ā̱yave̍ ||

**1.31.3**

tvam a̍gne pratha̱mo mā̍ta̱riśva̍na ā̱vir bha̍va sukratū̱yā vi̱vasva̍te | are̍jetā̱ṁ roda̍sī hotṛ̱vūrye 'sa̍ghnor bhā̱ram aya̍jo ma̱ho va̍so ||

**1.31.4**

tvam a̍gne̱ mana̍ve̱ dyām a̍vāśayaḥ purū̱rava̍se su̱kṛte̍ su̱kṛtta̍raḥ | śvā̱treṇa̱ yat pi̱tror mucya̍se̱ pary ā tvā̱ pūrva̍m anaya̱nn āpa̍ra̱m puna̍ḥ ||

**1.31.5**

tvam a̍gne vṛṣa̱bhaḥ pu̍ṣṭi̱vardha̍na̱ udya̍tasruce bhavasi śra̱vāyya̍ḥ | ya āhu̍ti̱m pari̱ vedā̱ vaṣa̍ṭkṛti̱m ekā̍yu̱r agre̱ viśa̍ ā̱vivā̍sasi ||

**1.31.6**

tvam a̍gne vṛji̱nava̍rtani̱ṁ nara̱ṁ sakma̍n piparṣi vi̱dathe̍ vicarṣaṇe | yaḥ śūra̍sātā̱ pari̍takmye̱ dhane̍ da̱bhrebhi̍ś ci̱t samṛ̍tā̱ haṁsi̱ bhūya̍saḥ ||

**1.31.7**

tvaṁ tam a̍gne amṛta̱tva u̍tta̱me marta̍ṁ dadhāsi̱ śrava̍se di̱ve-di̍ve | yas tā̍tṛṣā̱ṇa u̱bhayā̍ya̱ janma̍ne̱ maya̍ḥ kṛ̱ṇoṣi̱ praya̱ ā ca̍ sū̱raye̍ ||

**1.31.8**

tvaṁ no̍ agne sa̱naye̱ dhanā̍nāṁ ya̱śasa̍ṁ kā̱ruṁ kṛ̍ṇuhi̱ stavā̍naḥ | ṛ̱dhyāma̱ karmā̱pasā̱ nave̍na de̱vair dyā̍vāpṛthivī̱ prāva̍taṁ naḥ ||

**1.31.9**

tvaṁ no̍ agne pi̱tror u̱pastha̱ ā de̱vo de̱veṣv a̍navadya̱ jāgṛ̍viḥ | ta̱nū̱kṛd bo̍dhi̱ prama̍tiś ca kā̱rave̱ tvaṁ ka̍lyāṇa̱ vasu̱ viśva̱m opi̍ṣe ||

**1.31.10**

tvam a̍gne̱ prama̍ti̱s tvam pi̱tāsi̍ na̱s tvaṁ va̍ya̱skṛt tava̍ jā̱mayo̍ va̱yam | saṁ tvā̱ rāya̍ḥ śa̱tina̱ḥ saṁ sa̍ha̱sriṇa̍ḥ su̱vīra̍ṁ yanti vrata̱pām a̍dābhya ||

**1.31.11**

tvām a̍gne pratha̱mam ā̱yum ā̱yave̍ de̱vā a̍kṛṇva̱n nahu̍ṣasya vi̱śpati̍m | iḻā̍m akṛṇva̱n manu̍ṣasya̱ śāsa̍nīm pi̱tur yat pu̱tro mama̍kasya̱ jāya̍te ||

**1.31.12**

tvaṁ no̍ agne̱ tava̍ deva pā̱yubhi̍r ma̱ghono̍ rakṣa ta̱nva̍ś ca vandya | trā̱tā to̱kasya̱ tana̍ye̱ gavā̍m a̱sy ani̍meṣa̱ṁ rakṣa̍māṇa̱s tava̍ vra̱te ||

**1.31.13**

tvam a̍gne̱ yajya̍ve pā̱yur anta̍ro 'niṣa̱ṅgāya̍ catura̱kṣa i̍dhyase | yo rā̱taha̍vyo 'vṛ̱kāya̱ dhāya̍se kī̱reś ci̱n mantra̱m mana̍sā va̱noṣi̱ tam ||

**1.31.14**

tvam a̍gna uru̱śaṁsā̍ya vā̱ghate̍ spā̱rhaṁ yad rekṇa̍ḥ para̱maṁ va̱noṣi̱ tat | ā̱dhrasya̍ ci̱t prama̍tir ucyase pi̱tā pra pāka̱ṁ śāssi̱ pra diśo̍ vi̱duṣṭa̍raḥ ||

**1.31.15**

tvam a̍gne̱ praya̍tadakṣiṇa̱ṁ nara̱ṁ varme̍va syū̱tam pari̍ pāsi vi̱śvata̍ḥ | svā̱du̱kṣadmā̱ yo va̍sa̱tau syo̍na̱kṛj jī̍vayā̱jaṁ yaja̍te̱ sopa̱mā di̱vaḥ ||

**1.31.16**

i̱mām a̍gne śa̱raṇi̍m mīmṛṣo na i̱mam adhvā̍na̱ṁ yam agā̍ma dū̱rāt | ā̱piḥ pi̱tā prama̍tiḥ so̱myānā̱m bhṛmi̍r asy ṛṣi̱kṛn martyā̍nām ||

**1.31.17**

ma̱nu̱ṣvad a̍gne aṅgira̱svad a̍ṅgiro yayāti̱vad sada̍ne pūrva̱vac chu̍ce | accha̍ yā̱hy ā va̍hā̱ daivya̱ṁ jana̱m ā sā̍daya ba̱rhiṣi̱ yakṣi̍ ca pri̱yam ||

**1.31.18**

e̱tenā̍gne̱ brahma̍ṇā vāvṛdhasva̱ śaktī̍ vā̱ yat te̍ cakṛ̱mā vi̱dā vā̍ | u̱ta pra ṇe̍ṣy a̱bhi vasyo̍ a̱smān saṁ na̍ḥ sṛja suma̱tyā vāja̍vatyā ||

**1.32.1**

indra̍sya̱ nu vī̱ryā̍ṇi̱ pra vo̍ca̱ṁ yāni̍ ca̱kāra̍ pratha̱māni̍ va̱jrī | aha̱nn ahi̱m anv a̱pas ta̍tarda̱ pra va̱kṣaṇā̍ abhina̱t parva̍tānām ||

**1.32.2**

aha̱nn ahi̱m parva̍te śiśriyā̱ṇaṁ tvaṣṭā̍smai̱ vajra̍ṁ sva̱rya̍ṁ tatakṣa | vā̱śrā i̍va dhe̱nava̱ḥ syanda̍mānā̱ añja̍ḥ samu̱dram ava̍ jagmu̱r āpa̍ḥ ||

**1.32.3**

vṛ̱ṣā̱yamā̍ṇo 'vṛṇīta̱ soma̱ṁ trika̍drukeṣv apibat su̱tasya̍ | ā sāya̍kam ma̱ghavā̍datta̱ vajra̱m aha̍nn enam prathama̱jām ahī̍nām ||

**1.32.4**

yad i̱ndrāha̍n prathama̱jām ahī̍nā̱m ān mā̱yinā̱m ami̍nā̱ḥ prota mā̱yāḥ | āt sūrya̍ṁ ja̱naya̱n dyām u̱ṣāsa̍ṁ tā̱dītnā̱ śatru̱ṁ na kilā̍ vivitse ||

**1.32.5**

aha̍n vṛ̱traṁ vṛ̍tra̱tara̱ṁ vya̍ṁsa̱m indro̱ vajre̍ṇa maha̱tā va̱dhena̍ | skandhā̍ṁsīva̱ kuli̍śenā̱ vivṛ̱kṇāhi̍ḥ śayata upa̱pṛk pṛ̍thi̱vyāḥ ||

**1.32.6**

a̱yo̱ddheva̍ du̱rmada̱ ā hi ju̱hve ma̍hāvī̱raṁ tu̍vibā̱dham ṛ̍jī̱ṣam | nātā̍rīd asya̱ samṛ̍tiṁ va̱dhānā̱ṁ saṁ ru̱jānā̍ḥ pipiṣa̱ indra̍śatruḥ ||

**1.32.7**

a̱pād a̍ha̱sto a̍pṛtanya̱d indra̱m āsya̱ vajra̱m adhi̱ sānau̍ jaghāna | vṛṣṇo̱ vadhri̍ḥ prati̱māna̱m bubhū̍ṣan puru̱trā vṛ̱tro a̍śaya̱d vya̍staḥ ||

**1.32.8**

na̱daṁ na bhi̱nnam a̍mu̱yā śayā̍na̱m mano̱ ruhā̍ṇā̱ ati̍ ya̱nty āpa̍ḥ | yāś ci̍d vṛ̱tro ma̍hi̱nā pa̱ryati̍ṣṭha̱t tāsā̱m ahi̍ḥ patsuta̱ḥśīr ba̍bhūva ||

**1.32.9**

nī̱cāva̍yā abhavad vṛ̱trapu̱trendro̍ asyā̱ ava̱ vadha̍r jabhāra | utta̍rā̱ sūr adha̍raḥ pu̱tra ā̍sī̱d dānu̍ḥ śaye sa̱hava̍tsā̱ na dhe̱nuḥ ||

**1.32.10**

ati̍ṣṭhantīnām aniveśa̱nānā̱ṁ kāṣṭhā̍nā̱m madhye̱ nihi̍ta̱ṁ śarī̍ram | vṛ̱trasya̍ ni̱ṇyaṁ vi ca̍ra̱nty āpo̍ dī̱rghaṁ tama̱ āśa̍ya̱d indra̍śatruḥ ||

**1.32.11**

dā̱sapa̍tnī̱r ahi̍gopā atiṣṭha̱n niru̍ddhā̱ āpa̍ḥ pa̱ṇine̍va̱ gāva̍ḥ | a̱pām bila̱m api̍hita̱ṁ yad āsī̍d vṛ̱traṁ ja̍gha̱nvām̐ apa̱ tad va̍vāra ||

**1.32.12**

aśvyo̱ vāro̍ abhava̱s tad i̍ndra sṛ̱ke yat tvā̍ pra̱tyaha̍n de̱va eka̍ḥ | aja̍yo̱ gā aja̍yaḥ śūra̱ soma̱m avā̍sṛja̱ḥ sarta̍ve sa̱pta sindhū̍n ||

**1.32.13**

nāsmai̍ vi̱dyun na ta̍nya̱tuḥ si̍ṣedha̱ na yām miha̱m aki̍rad dhrā̱duni̍ṁ ca | indra̍ś ca̱ yad yu̍yu̱dhāte̱ ahi̍ś co̱tāpa̱rībhyo̍ ma̱ghavā̱ vi ji̍gye ||

**1.32.14**

ahe̍r yā̱tāra̱ṁ kam a̍paśya indra hṛ̱di yat te̍ ja̱ghnuṣo̱ bhīr aga̍cchat | nava̍ ca̱ yan na̍va̱tiṁ ca̱ srava̍ntīḥ śye̱no na bhī̱to ata̍ro̱ rajā̍ṁsi ||

**1.32.15**

indro̍ yā̱to 'va̍sitasya̱ rājā̱ śama̍sya ca śṛ̱ṅgiṇo̱ vajra̍bāhuḥ | sed u̱ rājā̍ kṣayati carṣaṇī̱nām a̱rān na ne̱miḥ pari̱ tā ba̍bhūva ||

**1.33.1**

etāyā̱mopa̍ ga̱vyanta̱ indra̍m a̱smāka̱ṁ su prama̍tiṁ vāvṛdhāti | a̱nā̱mṛ̱ṇaḥ ku̱vid ād a̱sya rā̱yo gavā̱ṁ keta̱m para̍m ā̱varja̍te naḥ ||

**1.33.2**

uped a̱haṁ dha̍na̱dām apra̍tīta̱ṁ juṣṭā̱ṁ na śye̱no va̍sa̱tim pa̍tāmi | indra̍ṁ nama̱syann u̍pa̱mebhi̍r a̱rkair yaḥ sto̱tṛbhyo̱ havyo̱ asti̱ yāma̍n ||

**1.33.3**

ni sarva̍sena iṣu̱dhīm̐r a̍sakta̱ sam a̱ryo gā a̍jati̱ yasya̱ vaṣṭi̍ | co̱ṣkū̱yamā̍ṇa indra̱ bhūri̍ vā̱mam mā pa̱ṇir bhū̍r a̱smad adhi̍ pravṛddha ||

**1.33.4**

vadhī̱r hi dasyu̍ṁ dha̱nina̍ṁ gha̱nena̱m̐ eka̱ś cara̍nn upaśā̱kebhi̍r indra | dhano̱r adhi̍ viṣu̱ṇak te vy ā̍ya̱nn aya̍jvānaḥ sana̱kāḥ preti̍m īyuḥ ||

**1.33.5**

parā̍ cic chī̱rṣā va̍vṛju̱s ta i̱ndrāya̍jvāno̱ yajva̍bhi̱ḥ spardha̍mānāḥ | pra yad di̱vo ha̍rivaḥ sthātar ugra̱ nir a̍vra̱tām̐ a̍dhamo̱ roda̍syoḥ ||

**1.33.6**

ayu̍yutsann anava̱dyasya̱ senā̱m ayā̍tayanta kṣi̱tayo̱ nava̍gvāḥ ||

**1.33.7**

tvam e̱tān ru̍da̱to jakṣa̍ta̱ś cāyo̍dhayo̱ raja̍sa indra pā̱re | avā̍daho di̱va ā dasyu̍m u̱ccā pra su̍nva̱taḥ stu̍va̱taḥ śaṁsa̍m āvaḥ ||

**1.33.8**

ca̱krā̱ṇāsa̍ḥ parī̱ṇaha̍m pṛthi̱vyā hira̍ṇyena ma̱ṇinā̱ śumbha̍mānāḥ | na hi̍nvā̱nāsa̍s titiru̱s ta indra̱m pari̱ spaśo̍ adadhā̱t sūrye̍ṇa ||

**1.33.9**

pari̱ yad i̍ndra̱ roda̍sī u̱bhe abu̍bhojīr mahi̱nā vi̱śvata̍ḥ sīm | ama̍nyamānām̐ a̱bhi manya̍mānai̱r nir bra̱hmabhi̍r adhamo̱ dasyu̍m indra ||

**1.33.10**

na ye di̱vaḥ pṛ̍thi̱vyā anta̍m ā̱pur na mā̱yābhi̍r dhana̱dām pa̱ryabhū̍van | yuja̱ṁ vajra̍ṁ vṛṣa̱bhaś ca̍kra̱ indro̱ nir jyoti̍ṣā̱ tama̍so̱ gā a̍dukṣat ||

**1.33.11**

anu̍ sva̱dhām a̍kṣara̱nn āpo̍ a̱syāva̍rdhata̱ madhya̱ ā nā̱vyā̍nām | sa̱dhrī̱cīne̍na̱ mana̍sā̱ tam indra̱ oji̍ṣṭhena̱ hanma̍nāhann a̱bhi dyūn ||

**1.33.12**

ny ā̍vidhyad ilī̱biśa̍sya dṛ̱ḻhā vi śṛ̱ṅgiṇa̍m abhina̱c chuṣṇa̱m indra̍ḥ | yāva̱t taro̍ maghava̱n yāva̱d ojo̱ vajre̍ṇa̱ śatru̍m avadhīḥ pṛta̱nyum ||

**1.33.13**

a̱bhi si̱dhmo a̍jigād asya̱ śatrū̱n vi ti̱gmena̍ vṛṣa̱bheṇā̱ puro̍ 'bhet | saṁ vajre̍ṇāsṛjad vṛ̱tram indra̱ḥ pra svām ma̱tim a̍tira̱c chāśa̍dānaḥ ||

**1.33.14**

āva̱ḥ kutsa̍m indra̱ yasmi̍ñ cā̱kan prāvo̱ yudhya̍ntaṁ vṛṣa̱bhaṁ daśa̍dyum | śa̱phacyu̍to re̱ṇur na̍kṣata̱ dyām uc chvai̍tre̱yo nṛ̍ṣāhyā̍ya tasthau ||

**1.33.15**

āva̱ḥ śama̍ṁ vṛṣa̱bhaṁ tugryā̍su kṣetraje̱ṣe ma̍ghava̱ñ chvitrya̱ṁ gām | jyok ci̱d atra̍ tasthi̱vāṁso̍ akrañ chatrūya̱tām adha̍rā̱ veda̍nākaḥ ||

**1.34.1**

triś ci̍n no a̱dyā bha̍vataṁ navedasā vi̱bhur vā̱ṁ yāma̍ u̱ta rā̱tir a̍śvinā | yu̱vor hi ya̱ntraṁ hi̱myeva̱ vāsa̍so 'bhyāya̱ṁsenyā̍ bhavatam manī̱ṣibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.34.2**

traya̍ḥ pa̱vayo̍ madhu̱vāha̍ne̱ rathe̱ soma̍sya ve̱nām anu̱ viśva̱ id vi̍duḥ | traya̍ḥ ska̱mbhāsa̍ḥ skabhi̱tāsa̍ ā̱rabhe̱ trir nakta̍ṁ yā̱thas trir v a̍śvinā̱ divā̍ ||

**1.34.3**

sa̱mā̱ne aha̱n trir a̍vadyagohanā̱ trir a̱dya ya̱jñam madhu̍nā mimikṣatam | trir vāja̍vatī̱r iṣo̍ aśvinā yu̱vaṁ do̱ṣā a̱smabhya̍m u̱ṣasa̍ś ca pinvatam ||

**1.34.4**

trir va̱rtir yā̍ta̱ṁ trir anu̍vrate ja̱ne triḥ su̍prā̱vye̍ tre̱dheva̍ śikṣatam | trir nā̱ndya̍ṁ vahatam aśvinā yu̱vaṁ triḥ pṛkṣo̍ a̱sme a̱kṣare̍va pinvatam ||

**1.34.5**

trir no̍ ra̱yiṁ va̍hatam aśvinā yu̱vaṁ trir de̱vatā̍tā̱ trir u̱tāva̍ta̱ṁ dhiya̍ḥ | triḥ sau̍bhaga̱tvaṁ trir u̱ta śravā̍ṁsi nas tri̱ṣṭhaṁ vā̱ṁ sūre̍ duhi̱tā ru̍ha̱d ratha̍m ||

**1.34.6**

trir no̍ aśvinā di̱vyāni̍ bheṣa̱jā triḥ pārthi̍vāni̱ trir u̍ dattam a̱dbhyaḥ | o̱māna̍ṁ śa̱ṁyor mama̍kāya sū̱nave̍ tri̱dhātu̱ śarma̍ vahataṁ śubhas patī ||

**1.34.7**

trir no̍ aśvinā yaja̱tā di̱ve-di̍ve̱ pari̍ tri̱dhātu̍ pṛthi̱vīm a̍śāyatam | ti̱sro nā̍satyā rathyā parā̱vata̍ ā̱tmeva̱ vāta̱ḥ svasa̍rāṇi gacchatam ||

**1.34.8**

trir a̍śvinā̱ sindhu̍bhiḥ sa̱ptamā̍tṛbhi̱s traya̍ āhā̱vās tre̱dhā ha̱viṣ kṛ̱tam | ti̱sraḥ pṛ̍thi̱vīr u̱pari̍ pra̱vā di̱vo nāka̍ṁ rakṣethe̱ dyubhi̍r a̱ktubhi̍r hi̱tam ||

**1.34.9**

kva1̱̍ trī ca̱krā tri̱vṛto̱ ratha̍sya̱ kva1̱̍ trayo̍ va̱ndhuro̱ ye sanī̍ḻāḥ | ka̱dā yogo̍ vā̱jino̱ rāsa̍bhasya̱ yena̍ ya̱jñaṁ nā̍satyopayā̱thaḥ ||

**1.34.10**

ā nā̍satyā̱ gaccha̍taṁ hū̱yate̍ ha̱vir madhva̍ḥ pibatam madhu̱pebhi̍r ā̱sabhi̍ḥ | yu̱vor hi pūrva̍ṁ savi̱toṣaso̱ ratha̍m ṛ̱tāya̍ ci̱traṁ ghṛ̱tava̍nta̱m iṣya̍ti ||

**1.34.11**

ā nā̍satyā tri̱bhir e̍kāda̱śair i̱ha de̱vebhi̍r yātam madhu̱peya̍m aśvinā | prāyu̱s tāri̍ṣṭa̱ṁ nī rapā̍ṁsi mṛkṣata̱ṁ sedha̍ta̱ṁ dveṣo̱ bhava̍taṁ sacā̱bhuvā̍ ||

**1.34.12**

ā no̍ aśvinā tri̱vṛtā̱ rathe̍nā̱rvāñca̍ṁ ra̱yiṁ va̍hataṁ su̱vīra̍m | śṛ̱ṇvantā̍ vā̱m ava̍se johavīmi vṛ̱dhe ca̍ no bhavata̱ṁ vāja̍sātau ||

**1.35.1**

hvayā̍my a̱gnim pra̍tha̱maṁ sva̱staye̱ hvayā̍mi mi̱trāvaru̍ṇāv i̱hāva̍se | hvayā̍mi̱ rātrī̱ṁ jaga̍to ni̱veśa̍nī̱ṁ hvayā̍mi de̱vaṁ sa̍vi̱tāra̍m ū̱taye̍ ||

**1.35.2**

ā kṛ̱ṣṇena̱ raja̍sā̱ varta̍māno nive̱śaya̍nn a̱mṛta̱m martya̍ṁ ca | hi̱ra̱ṇyaye̍na savi̱tā rathe̱nā de̱vo yā̍ti̱ bhuva̍nāni̱ paśya̍n ||

**1.35.3**

yāti̍ de̱vaḥ pra̱vatā̱ yāty u̱dvatā̱ yāti̍ śu̱bhrābhyā̍ṁ yaja̱to hari̍bhyām | ā de̱vo yā̍ti savi̱tā pa̍rā̱vato 'pa̱ viśvā̍ duri̱tā bādha̍mānaḥ ||

**1.35.4**

a̱bhīvṛ̍ta̱ṁ kṛśa̍nair vi̱śvarū̍pa̱ṁ hira̍ṇyaśamyaṁ yaja̱to bṛ̱hanta̍m | āsthā̱d ratha̍ṁ savi̱tā ci̱trabhā̍nuḥ kṛ̱ṣṇā rajā̍ṁsi̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ṁ dadhā̍naḥ ||

**1.35.5**

vi janā̍ñ chyā̱vāḥ śi̍ti̱pādo̍ akhya̱n ratha̱ṁ hira̍ṇyapraüga̱ṁ vaha̍ntaḥ | śaśva̱d viśa̍ḥ savi̱tur daivya̍syo̱pasthe̱ viśvā̱ bhuva̍nāni tasthuḥ ||

**1.35.6**

ti̱sro dyāva̍ḥ savi̱tur dvā u̱pasthā̱m̐ ekā̍ ya̱masya̱ bhuva̍ne virā̱ṣāṭ | ā̱ṇiṁ na rathya̍m a̱mṛtādhi̍ tasthur i̱ha bra̍vītu̱ ya u̱ tac cike̍tat ||

**1.35.7**

vi su̍pa̱rṇo a̱ntari̍kṣāṇy akhyad gabhī̱rave̍pā̱ asu̍raḥ sunī̱thaḥ | kve̱3̱̍dānī̱ṁ sūrya̱ḥ kaś ci̍keta kata̱māṁ dyāṁ ra̱śmir a̱syā ta̍tāna ||

**1.35.8**

a̱ṣṭau vy a̍khyat ka̱kubha̍ḥ pṛthi̱vyās trī dhanva̱ yoja̍nā sa̱pta sindhū̍n | hi̱ra̱ṇyā̱kṣaḥ sa̍vi̱tā de̱va āgā̱d dadha̱d ratnā̍ dā̱śuṣe̱ vāryā̍ṇi ||

**1.35.9**

hira̍ṇyapāṇiḥ savi̱tā vica̍rṣaṇir u̱bhe dyāvā̍pṛthi̱vī a̱ntar ī̍yate | apāmī̍vā̱m bādha̍te̱ veti̱ sūrya̍m a̱bhi kṛ̱ṣṇena̱ raja̍sā̱ dyām ṛ̍ṇoti ||

**1.35.10**

hira̍ṇyahasto̱ asu̍raḥ sunī̱thaḥ su̍mṛḻī̱kaḥ svavā̍m̐ yātv a̱rvāṅ | a̱pa̱sedha̍n ra̱kṣaso̍ yātu̱dhānā̱n asthā̍d de̱vaḥ pra̍tido̱ṣaṁ gṛ̍ṇā̱naḥ ||

**1.35.11**

ye te̱ panthā̍ḥ savitaḥ pū̱rvyāso̍ 're̱ṇava̱ḥ sukṛ̍tā a̱ntari̍kṣe | tebhi̍r no a̱dya pa̱thibhi̍ḥ su̱gebhī̱ rakṣā̍ ca no̱ adhi̍ ca brūhi deva ||

**1.36.1**

pra vo̍ ya̱hvam pu̍rū̱ṇāṁ vi̱śāṁ de̍vaya̱tīnā̍m | a̱gniṁ sū̱ktebhi̱r vaco̍bhir īmahe̱ yaṁ sī̱m id a̱nya īḻa̍te ||

**1.36.2**

janā̍so a̱gniṁ da̍dhire saho̱vṛdha̍ṁ ha̱viṣma̍nto vidhema te | sa tvaṁ no̍ a̱dya su̱manā̍ i̱hāvi̱tā bhavā̱ vāje̍ṣu santya ||

**1.36.3**

pra tvā̍ dū̱taṁ vṛ̍ṇīmahe̱ hotā̍raṁ vi̱śvave̍dasam | ma̱has te̍ sa̱to vi ca̍ranty a̱rcayo̍ di̱vi spṛ̍śanti bhā̱nava̍ḥ ||

**1.36.4**

de̱vāsa̍s tvā̱ varu̍ṇo mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā saṁ dū̱tam pra̱tnam i̍ndhate | viśva̱ṁ so a̍gne jayati̱ tvayā̱ dhana̱ṁ yas te̍ da̱dāśa̱ martya̍ḥ ||

**1.36.5**

ma̱ndro hotā̍ gṛ̱hapa̍ti̱r agne̍ dū̱to vi̱śām a̍si | tve viśvā̱ saṁga̍tāni vra̱tā dhru̱vā yāni̍ de̱vā akṛ̍ṇvata ||

**1.36.6**

tve id a̍gne su̱bhage̍ yaviṣṭhya̱ viśva̱m ā hū̍yate ha̱viḥ | sa tvaṁ no̍ a̱dya su̱manā̍ u̱tāpa̱raṁ yakṣi̍ de̱vān su̱vīryā̍ ||

**1.36.7**

taṁ ghe̍m i̱tthā na̍ma̱svina̱ upa̍ sva̱rāja̍m āsate | hotrā̍bhir a̱gnim manu̍ṣa̱ḥ sam i̍ndhate titi̱rvāṁso̱ ati̱ sridha̍ḥ ||

**1.36.8**

ghnanto̍ vṛ̱tram a̍tara̱n roda̍sī a̱pa u̱ru kṣayā̍ya cakrire | bhuva̱t kaṇve̱ vṛṣā̍ dyu̱mny āhu̍ta̱ḥ kranda̱d aśvo̱ gavi̍ṣṭiṣu ||

**1.36.9**

saṁ sī̍dasva ma̱hām̐ a̍si̱ śoca̍sva deva̱vīta̍maḥ | vi dhū̱mam a̍gne aru̱ṣam mi̍yedhya sṛ̱ja pra̍śasta darśa̱tam ||

**1.36.10**

yaṁ tvā̍ de̱vāso̱ mana̍ve da̱dhur i̱ha yaji̍ṣṭhaṁ havyavāhana | yaṁ kaṇvo̱ medhyā̍tithir dhana̱spṛta̱ṁ yaṁ vṛṣā̱ yam u̍pastu̱taḥ ||

**1.36.11**

yam a̱gnim medhyā̍tithi̱ḥ kaṇva̍ ī̱dha ṛ̱tād adhi̍ | tasya̱ preṣo̍ dīdiyu̱s tam i̱mā ṛca̱s tam a̱gniṁ va̍rdhayāmasi ||

**1.36.12**

rā̱yas pū̍rdhi svadhā̱vo 'sti̱ hi te 'gne̍ de̱veṣv āpya̍m | tvaṁ vāja̍sya̱ śrutya̍sya rājasi̱ sa no̍ mṛḻa ma̱hām̐ a̍si ||

**1.36.13**

ū̱rdhva ū̱ ṣu ṇa̍ ū̱taye̱ tiṣṭhā̍ de̱vo na sa̍vi̱tā | ū̱rdhvo vāja̍sya̱ sani̍tā̱ yad a̱ñjibhi̍r vā̱ghadbhi̍r vi̱hvayā̍mahe ||

**1.36.14**

ū̱rdhvo na̍ḥ pā̱hy aṁha̍so̱ ni ke̱tunā̱ viśva̱ṁ sam a̱triṇa̍ṁ daha | kṛ̱dhī na̍ ū̱rdhvāñ ca̱rathā̍ya jī̱vase̍ vi̱dā de̱veṣu̍ no̱ duva̍ḥ ||

**1.36.15**

pā̱hi no̍ agne ra̱kṣasa̍ḥ pā̱hi dhū̱rter arā̍vṇaḥ | pā̱hi rīṣa̍ta u̱ta vā̱ jighā̍ṁsato̱ bṛha̍dbhāno̱ yavi̍ṣṭhya ||

**1.36.16**

gha̱neva̱ viṣva̱g vi ja̱hy arā̍vṇa̱s tapu̍rjambha̱ yo a̍sma̱dhruk | yo martya̱ḥ śiśī̍te̱ aty a̱ktubhi̱r mā na̱ḥ sa ri̱pur ī̍śata ||

**1.36.17**

a̱gnir va̍vne su̱vīrya̍m a̱gniḥ kaṇvā̍ya̱ saubha̍gam | a̱gniḥ prāva̍n mi̱trota medhyā̍tithim a̱gniḥ sā̱tā u̍pastu̱tam ||

**1.36.18**

a̱gninā̍ tu̱rvaśa̱ṁ yadu̍m parā̱vata̍ u̱grāde̍vaṁ havāmahe | a̱gnir na̍ya̱n nava̍vāstvam bṛ̱hadra̍thaṁ tu̱rvīti̱ṁ dasya̍ve̱ saha̍ḥ ||

**1.36.19**

ni tvām a̍gne̱ manu̍r dadhe̱ jyoti̱r janā̍ya̱ śaśva̍te | dī̱detha̱ kaṇva̍ ṛ̱tajā̍ta ukṣi̱to yaṁ na̍ma̱syanti̍ kṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ ||

**1.36.20**

tve̱ṣāso̍ a̱gner ama̍vanto a̱rcayo̍ bhī̱māso̱ na pratī̍taye | ra̱kṣa̱svina̱ḥ sada̱m id yā̍tu̱māva̍to̱ viśva̱ṁ sam a̱triṇa̍ṁ daha ||

**1.37.1**

krī̱ḻaṁ va̱ḥ śardho̱ māru̍tam ana̱rvāṇa̍ṁ rathe̱śubha̍m | kaṇvā̍ a̱bhi pra gā̍yata ||

**1.37.2**

ye pṛṣa̍tībhir ṛ̱ṣṭibhi̍ḥ sā̱kaṁ vāśī̍bhir a̱ñjibhi̍ḥ | ajā̍yanta̱ svabhā̍navaḥ ||

**1.37.3**

i̱heva̍ śṛṇva eṣā̱ṁ kaśā̱ haste̍ṣu̱ yad vadā̍n | ni yāma̍ñ ci̱tram ṛ̍ñjate ||

**1.37.4**

pra va̱ḥ śardhā̍ya̱ ghṛṣva̍ye tve̱ṣadyu̍mnāya śu̱ṣmiṇe̍ | de̱vatta̱m brahma̍ gāyata ||

**1.37.5**

pra śa̍ṁsā̱ goṣv aghnya̍ṁ krī̱ḻaṁ yac chardho̱ māru̍tam | jambhe̱ rasa̍sya vāvṛdhe ||

**1.37.6**

ko vo̱ varṣi̍ṣṭha̱ ā na̍ro di̱vaś ca̱ gmaś ca̍ dhūtayaḥ | yat sī̱m anta̱ṁ na dhū̍nu̱tha ||

**1.37.7**

ni vo̱ yāmā̍ya̱ mānu̍ṣo da̱dhra u̱grāya̍ ma̱nyave̍ | jihī̍ta̱ parva̍to gi̱riḥ ||

**1.37.8**

yeṣā̱m ajme̍ṣu pṛthi̱vī ju̍ju̱rvām̐ i̍va vi̱śpati̍ḥ | bhi̱yā yāme̍ṣu̱ reja̍te ||

**1.37.9**

sthi̱raṁ hi jāna̍m eṣā̱ṁ vayo̍ mā̱tur nire̍tave | yat sī̱m anu̍ dvi̱tā śava̍ḥ ||

**1.37.10**

ud u̱ tye sū̱navo̱ gira̱ḥ kāṣṭhā̱ ajme̍ṣv atnata | vā̱śrā a̍bhi̱jñu yāta̍ve ||

**1.37.11**

tyaṁ ci̍d ghā dī̱rgham pṛ̱thum mi̱ho napā̍ta̱m amṛ̍dhram | pra cyā̍vayanti̱ yāma̍bhiḥ ||

**1.37.12**

maru̍to̱ yad dha̍ vo̱ bala̱ṁ janā̍m̐ acucyavītana | gi̱rīm̐r a̍cucyavītana ||

**1.37.13**

yad dha̱ yānti̍ ma̱ruta̱ḥ saṁ ha̍ bruva̱te 'dhva̱nn ā | śṛ̱ṇoti̱ kaś ci̍d eṣām ||

**1.37.14**

pra yā̍ta̱ śībha̍m ā̱śubhi̱ḥ santi̱ kaṇve̍ṣu vo̱ duva̍ḥ | tatro̱ ṣu mā̍dayādhvai ||

**1.37.15**

asti̱ hi ṣmā̱ madā̍ya va̱ḥ smasi̍ ṣmā va̱yam e̍ṣām | viśva̍ṁ ci̱d āyu̍r jī̱vase̍ ||

**1.38.1**

kad dha̍ nū̱naṁ ka̍dhapriyaḥ pi̱tā pu̱traṁ na hasta̍yoḥ | da̱dhi̱dhve vṛ̍ktabarhiṣaḥ ||

**1.38.2**

kva̍ nū̱naṁ kad vo̱ artha̱ṁ gantā̍ di̱vo na pṛ̍thi̱vyāḥ | kva̍ vo̱ gāvo̱ na ra̍ṇyanti ||

**1.38.3**

kva̍ vaḥ su̱mnā navyā̍ṁsi̱ maru̍ta̱ḥ kva̍ suvi̱tā | kvo̱3̱̍ viśvā̍ni̱ saubha̍gā ||

**1.38.4**

yad yū̱yam pṛ̍śnimātaro̱ martā̍sa̱ḥ syāta̍na | sto̱tā vo̍ a̱mṛta̍ḥ syāt ||

**1.38.5**

mā vo̍ mṛ̱go na yava̍se jari̱tā bhū̱d ajo̍ṣyaḥ | pa̱thā ya̱masya̍ gā̱d upa̍ ||

**1.38.6**

mo ṣu ṇa̱ḥ parā̍-parā̱ nirṛ̍tir du̱rhaṇā̍ vadhīt | pa̱dī̱ṣṭa tṛṣṇa̍yā sa̱ha ||

**1.38.7**

sa̱tyaṁ tve̱ṣā ama̍vanto̱ dhanva̍ñ ci̱d ā ru̱driyā̍saḥ | miha̍ṁ kṛṇvanty avā̱tām ||

**1.38.8**

vā̱śreva̍ vi̱dyun mi̍māti va̱tsaṁ na mā̱tā si̍ṣakti | yad e̍ṣāṁ vṛ̱ṣṭir asa̍rji ||

**1.38.9**

divā̍ ci̱t tama̍ḥ kṛṇvanti pa̱rjanye̍nodavā̱hena̍ | yat pṛ̍thi̱vīṁ vyu̱ndanti̍ ||

**1.38.10**

adha̍ sva̱nān ma̱rutā̱ṁ viśva̱m ā sadma̱ pārthi̍vam | are̍janta̱ pra mānu̍ṣāḥ ||

**1.38.11**

maru̍to vīḻupā̱ṇibhi̍ś ci̱trā rodha̍svatī̱r anu̍ | yā̱tem akhi̍drayāmabhiḥ ||

**1.38.12**

sthi̱rā va̍ḥ santu ne̱mayo̱ rathā̱ aśvā̍sa eṣām | susa̍ṁskṛtā a̱bhīśa̍vaḥ ||

**1.38.13**

acchā̍ vadā̱ tanā̍ gi̱rā ja̱rāyai̱ brahma̍ṇa̱s pati̍m | a̱gnim mi̱traṁ na da̍rśa̱tam ||

**1.38.14**

mi̱mī̱hi śloka̍m ā̱sye̍ pa̱rjanya̍ iva tatanaḥ | gāya̍ gāya̱tram u̱kthya̍m ||

**1.38.15**

vanda̍sva̱ māru̍taṁ ga̱ṇaṁ tve̱ṣam pa̍na̱syum a̱rkiṇa̍m | a̱sme vṛ̱ddhā a̍sann i̱ha ||

**1.39.1**

pra yad i̱tthā pa̍rā̱vata̍ḥ śo̱cir na māna̱m asya̍tha | kasya̱ kratvā̍ maruta̱ḥ kasya̱ varpa̍sā̱ kaṁ yā̍tha̱ kaṁ ha̍ dhūtayaḥ ||

**1.39.2**

sthi̱rā va̍ḥ sa̱ntv āyu̍dhā parā̱ṇude̍ vī̱ḻū u̱ta pra̍ti̱ṣkabhe̍ | yu̱ṣmāka̍m astu̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ panī̍yasī̱ mā martya̍sya mā̱yina̍ḥ ||

**1.39.3**

parā̍ ha̱ yat sthi̱raṁ ha̱tha naro̍ va̱rtaya̍thā gu̱ru | vi yā̍thana va̱nina̍ḥ pṛthi̱vyā vy āśā̱ḥ parva̍tānām ||

**1.39.4**

na̱hi va̱ḥ śatru̍r vivi̱de adhi̱ dyavi̱ na bhūmyā̍ṁ riśādasaḥ | yu̱ṣmāka̍m astu̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ tanā̍ yu̱jā rudrā̍so̱ nū ci̍d ā̱dhṛṣe̍ ||

**1.39.5**

pra ve̍payanti̱ parva̍tā̱n vi vi̍ñcanti̱ vana̱spatī̍n | pro ā̍rata maruto du̱rmadā̍ iva̱ devā̍sa̱ḥ sarva̍yā vi̱śā ||

**1.39.6**

upo̱ rathe̍ṣu̱ pṛṣa̍tīr ayugdhva̱m praṣṭi̍r vahati̱ rohi̍taḥ ||

**1.39.7**

ā vo̍ ma̱kṣū tanā̍ya̱ kaṁ rudrā̱ avo̍ vṛṇīmahe | gantā̍ nū̱naṁ no 'va̍sā̱ yathā̍ pu̱retthā kaṇvā̍ya bi̱bhyuṣe̍ ||

**1.39.8**

yu̱ṣmeṣi̍to maruto̱ martye̍ṣita̱ ā yo no̱ abhva̱ īṣa̍te | vi taṁ yu̍yota̱ śava̍sā̱ vy oja̍sā̱ vi yu̱ṣmākā̍bhir ū̱tibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.39.9**

asā̍mi̱ hi pra̍yajyava̱ḥ kaṇva̍ṁ da̱da pra̍cetasaḥ | asā̍mibhir maruta̱ ā na̍ ū̱tibhi̱r gantā̍ vṛ̱ṣṭiṁ na vi̱dyuta̍ḥ ||

**1.39.10**

asā̱my ojo̍ bibhṛthā sudāna̱vo 'sā̍mi dhūtaya̱ḥ śava̍ḥ ||

**1.40.1**

ut ti̍ṣṭha brahmaṇas pate deva̱yanta̍s tvemahe | upa̱ pra ya̍ntu ma̱ruta̍ḥ su̱dāna̍va̱ indra̍ prā̱śūr bha̍vā̱ sacā̍ ||

**1.40.2**

tvām id dhi sa̍hasas putra̱ martya̍ upabrū̱te dhane̍ hi̱te | su̱vīrya̍m maruta̱ ā svaśvya̱ṁ dadhī̍ta̱ yo va̍ āca̱ke ||

**1.40.3**

praitu̱ brahma̍ṇa̱s pati̱ḥ pra de̱vy e̍tu sū̱nṛtā̍ | acchā̍ vī̱raṁ narya̍m pa̱ṅktirā̍dhasaṁ de̱vā ya̱jñaṁ na̍yantu naḥ ||

**1.40.4**

yo vā̱ghate̱ dadā̍ti sū̱nara̱ṁ vasu̱ sa dha̍tte̱ akṣi̍ti̱ śrava̍ḥ | tasmā̱ iḻā̍ṁ su̱vīrā̱m ā ya̍jāmahe su̱pratū̍rtim ane̱hasa̍m ||

**1.40.5**

pra nū̱nam brahma̍ṇa̱s pati̱r mantra̍ṁ vadaty u̱kthya̍m | yasmi̱nn indro̱ varu̍ṇo mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā de̱vā okā̍ṁsi cakri̱re ||

**1.40.6**

tam id vo̍cemā vi̱dathe̍ṣu śa̱mbhuva̱m mantra̍ṁ devā ane̱hasa̍m | i̱māṁ ca̱ vāca̍m prati̱harya̍thā naro̱ viśved vā̱mā vo̍ aśnavat ||

**1.40.7**

ko de̍va̱yanta̍m aśnava̱j jana̱ṁ ko vṛ̱ktaba̍rhiṣam | pra-pra̍ dā̱śvān pa̱styā̍bhir asthitānta̱rvāva̱t kṣaya̍ṁ dadhe ||

**1.40.8**

upa̍ kṣa̱tram pṛ̍ñcī̱ta hanti̱ rāja̍bhir bha̱ye ci̍t sukṣi̱tiṁ da̍dhe | nāsya̍ va̱rtā na ta̍ru̱tā ma̍hādha̱ne nārbhe̍ asti va̱jriṇa̍ḥ ||

**1.41.1**

yaṁ rakṣa̍nti̱ prace̍taso̱ varu̍ṇo mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā | nū ci̱t sa da̍bhyate̱ jana̍ḥ ||

**1.41.2**

yam bā̱hute̍va̱ pipra̍ti̱ pānti̱ martya̍ṁ ri̱ṣaḥ | ari̍ṣṭa̱ḥ sarva̍ edhate ||

**1.41.3**

vi du̱rgā vi dviṣa̍ḥ pu̱ro ghnanti̱ rājā̍na eṣām | naya̍nti duri̱tā ti̱raḥ ||

**1.41.4**

su̱gaḥ panthā̍ anṛkṣa̱ra ādi̍tyāsa ṛ̱taṁ ya̱te | nātrā̍vakhā̱do a̍sti vaḥ ||

**1.41.5**

yaṁ ya̱jñaṁ naya̍thā nara̱ ādi̍tyā ṛ̱junā̍ pa̱thā | pra va̱ḥ sa dhī̱taye̍ naśat ||

**1.41.6**

sa ratna̱m martyo̱ vasu̱ viśva̍ṁ to̱kam u̱ta tmanā̍ | acchā̍ gaccha̱ty astṛ̍taḥ ||

**1.41.7**

ka̱thā rā̍dhāma sakhāya̱ḥ stoma̍m mi̱trasyā̍rya̱mṇaḥ | mahi̱ psaro̱ varu̍ṇasya ||

**1.41.8**

mā vo̱ ghnanta̱m mā śapa̍nta̱m prati̍ voce deva̱yanta̍m | su̱mnair id va̱ ā vi̍vāse ||

**1.41.9**

ca̱tura̍ś ci̱d dada̍mānād bibhī̱yād ā nidhā̍toḥ | na du̍ru̱ktāya̍ spṛhayet ||

**1.42.1**

sam pū̍ṣa̱nn adhva̍nas tira̱ vy aṁho̍ vimuco napāt | sakṣvā̍ deva̱ pra ṇa̍s pu̱raḥ ||

**1.42.2**

yo na̍ḥ pūṣann a̱gho vṛko̍ du̱ḥśeva̍ ā̱dide̍śati | apa̍ sma̱ tam pa̱tho ja̍hi ||

**1.42.3**

apa̱ tyam pa̍ripa̱nthina̍m muṣī̱vāṇa̍ṁ hura̱ścita̍m | dū̱ram adhi̍ sru̱ter a̍ja ||

**1.42.4**

tvaṁ tasya̍ dvayā̱vino̱ 'ghaśa̍ṁsasya̱ kasya̍ cit | pa̱dābhi ti̍ṣṭha̱ tapu̍ṣim ||

**1.42.5**

ā tat te̍ dasra mantuma̱ḥ pūṣa̱nn avo̍ vṛṇīmahe | yena̍ pi̱tṝn aco̍dayaḥ ||

**1.42.6**

adhā̍ no viśvasaubhaga̱ hira̍ṇyavāśīmattama | dhanā̍ni su̱ṣaṇā̍ kṛdhi ||

**1.42.7**

ati̍ naḥ sa̱ścato̍ naya su̱gā na̍ḥ su̱pathā̍ kṛṇu | pūṣa̍nn i̱ha kratu̍ṁ vidaḥ ||

**1.42.8**

a̱bhi sū̱yava̍saṁ naya̱ na na̍vajvā̱ro adhva̍ne | pūṣa̍nn i̱ha kratu̍ṁ vidaḥ ||

**1.42.9**

śa̱gdhi pū̱rdhi pra ya̍ṁsi ca śiśī̱hi prāsy u̱dara̍m | pūṣa̍nn i̱ha kratu̍ṁ vidaḥ ||

**1.42.10**

na pū̱ṣaṇa̍m methāmasi sū̱ktair a̱bhi gṛ̍ṇīmasi | vasū̍ni da̱smam ī̍mahe ||

**1.43.1**

kad ru̱drāya̱ prace̍tase mī̱ḻhuṣṭa̍māya̱ tavya̍se | vo̱cema̱ śaṁta̍maṁ hṛ̱de ||

**1.43.2**

yathā̍ no̱ adi̍ti̱ḥ kara̱t paśve̱ nṛbhyo̱ yathā̱ gave̍ | yathā̍ to̱kāya̍ ru̱driya̍m ||

**1.43.3**

yathā̍ no mi̱tro varu̍ṇo̱ yathā̍ ru̱draś cike̍tati | yathā̱ viśve̍ sa̱joṣa̍saḥ ||

**1.43.4**

gā̱thapa̍tim me̱dhapa̍tiṁ ru̱draṁ jalā̍ṣabheṣajam | tac cha̱ṁyoḥ su̱mnam ī̍mahe ||

**1.43.5**

yaḥ śu̱kra i̍va̱ sūryo̱ hira̍ṇyam iva̱ roca̍te | śreṣṭho̍ de̱vānā̱ṁ vasu̍ḥ ||

**1.43.6**

śaṁ na̍ḥ kara̱ty arva̍te su̱gam me̱ṣāya̍ me̱ṣye̍ | nṛbhyo̱ nāri̍bhyo̱ gave̍ ||

**1.43.7**

a̱sme so̍ma̱ śriya̱m adhi̱ ni dhe̍hi śa̱tasya̍ nṛ̱ṇām | mahi̱ śrava̍s tuvinṛ̱mṇam ||

**1.43.8**

mā na̍ḥ somapari̱bādho̱ mārā̍tayo juhuranta | ā na̍ indo̱ vāje̍ bhaja ||

**1.43.9**

yās te̍ pra̱jā a̱mṛta̍sya̱ para̍smi̱n dhāma̍nn ṛ̱tasya̍ | mū̱rdhā nābhā̍ soma vena ā̱bhūṣa̍ntīḥ soma vedaḥ ||

**1.44.1**

agne̱ viva̍svad u̱ṣasa̍ś ci̱traṁ rādho̍ amartya | ā dā̱śuṣe̍ jātavedo vahā̱ tvam a̱dyā de̱vām̐ u̍ṣa̱rbudha̍ḥ ||

**1.44.2**

juṣṭo̱ hi dū̱to asi̍ havya̱vāha̱no 'gne̍ ra̱thīr a̍dhva̱rāṇā̍m | sa̱jūr a̱śvibhyā̍m u̱ṣasā̍ su̱vīrya̍m a̱sme dhe̍hi̱ śravo̍ bṛ̱hat ||

**1.44.3**

a̱dyā dū̱taṁ vṛ̍ṇīmahe̱ vasu̍m a̱gnim pu̍rupri̱yam | dhū̱make̍tu̱m bhāṛ̍jīka̱ṁ vyu̍ṣṭiṣu ya̱jñānā̍m adhvara̱śriya̍m ||

**1.44.4**

śreṣṭha̱ṁ yavi̍ṣṭha̱m ati̍thi̱ṁ svā̍huta̱ṁ juṣṭa̱ṁ janā̍ya dā̱śuṣe̍ | de̱vām̐ acchā̱ yāta̍ve jā̱tave̍dasam a̱gnim ī̍ḻe̱ vyu̍ṣṭiṣu ||

**1.44.5**

sta̱vi̱ṣyāmi̱ tvām a̱haṁ viśva̍syāmṛta bhojana | agne̍ trā̱tāra̍m a̱mṛta̍m miyedhya̱ yaji̍ṣṭhaṁ havyavāhana ||

**1.44.6**

su̱śaṁso̍ bodhi gṛṇa̱te ya̍viṣṭhya̱ madhu̍jihva̱ḥ svā̍hutaḥ | praska̍ṇvasya prati̱rann āyu̍r jī̱vase̍ nama̱syā daivya̱ṁ jana̍m ||

**1.44.7**

hotā̍raṁ vi̱śvave̍dasa̱ṁ saṁ hi tvā̱ viśa̍ i̱ndhate̍ | sa ā va̍ha puruhūta̱ prace̍ta̱so 'gne̍ de̱vām̐ i̱ha dra̱vat ||

**1.44.8**

sa̱vi̱tāra̍m u̱ṣasa̍m a̱śvinā̱ bhaga̍m a̱gniṁ vyu̍ṣṭiṣu̱ kṣapa̍ḥ | kaṇvā̍sas tvā su̱taso̍māsa indhate havya̱vāha̍ṁ svadhvara ||

**1.44.9**

pati̱r hy a̍dhva̱rāṇā̱m agne̍ dū̱to vi̱śām asi̍ | u̱ṣa̱rbudha̱ ā va̍ha̱ soma̍pītaye de̱vām̐ a̱dya sva̱rdṛśa̍ḥ ||

**1.44.10**

agne̱ pūrvā̱ anū̱ṣaso̍ vibhāvaso dī̱detha̍ vi̱śvada̍rśataḥ | asi̱ grāme̍ṣv avi̱tā pu̱rohi̱to 'si̍ ya̱jñeṣu̱ mānu̍ṣaḥ ||

**1.44.11**

ni tvā̍ ya̱jñasya̱ sādha̍na̱m agne̱ hotā̍ram ṛ̱tvija̍m | ma̱nu̱ṣvad de̍va dhīmahi̱ prace̍tasaṁ jī̱raṁ dū̱tam ama̍rtyam ||

**1.44.12**

yad de̱vānā̍m mitramahaḥ pu̱rohi̱to 'nta̍ro̱ yāsi̍ dū̱tya̍m | sindho̍r iva̱ prasva̍nitāsa ū̱rmayo̱ 'gner bhrā̍jante a̱rcaya̍ḥ ||

**1.44.13**

śru̱dhi śru̍tkarṇa̱ vahni̍bhir de̱vair a̍gne sa̱yāva̍bhiḥ | ā sī̍dantu ba̱rhiṣi̍ mi̱tro a̍rya̱mā prā̍ta̱ryāvā̍ṇo adhva̱ram ||

**1.44.14**

śṛ̱ṇvantu̱ stoma̍m ma̱ruta̍ḥ su̱dāna̍vo 'gniji̱hvā ṛ̍tā̱vṛdha̍ḥ | piba̍tu̱ soma̱ṁ varu̍ṇo dhṛ̱tavra̍to̱ 'śvibhyā̍m u̱ṣasā̍ sa̱jūḥ ||

**1.45.1**

tvam a̍gne̱ vasū̍m̐r i̱ha ru̱drām̐ ā̍di̱tyām̐ u̱ta | yajā̍ svadhva̱raṁ jana̱m manu̍jātaṁ ghṛta̱pruṣa̍m ||

**1.45.2**

śru̱ṣṭī̱vāno̱ hi dā̱śuṣe̍ de̱vā a̍gne̱ vice̍tasaḥ | tān ro̍hidaśva girvaṇa̱s traya̍striṁśata̱m ā va̍ha ||

**1.45.3**

pri̱ya̱me̱dha̱vad a̍tri̱vaj jāta̍vedo virūpa̱vat | a̱ṅgi̱ra̱svan ma̍hivrata̱ praska̍ṇvasya śrudhī̱ hava̍m ||

**1.45.4**

mahi̍kerava ū̱taye̍ pri̱yame̍dhā ahūṣata | rāja̍ntam adhva̱rāṇā̍m a̱gniṁ śu̱kreṇa̍ śo̱ciṣā̍ ||

**1.45.5**

ghṛtā̍havana santye̱mā u̱ ṣu śru̍dhī̱ gira̍ḥ | yābhi̱ḥ kaṇva̍sya sū̱navo̱ hava̍nte̱ 'va̍se tvā ||

**1.45.6**

tvāṁ ci̍traśravastama̱ hava̍nte vi̱kṣu ja̱ntava̍ḥ | śo̱ciṣke̍śam purupri̱yāgne̍ ha̱vyāya̱ voḻha̍ve ||

**1.45.7**

ni tvā̱ hotā̍ram ṛ̱tvija̍ṁ dadhi̱re va̍su̱vitta̍mam | śrutka̍rṇaṁ sa̱pratha̍stama̱ṁ viprā̍ agne̱ divi̍ṣṭiṣu ||

**1.45.8**

ā tvā̱ viprā̍ acucyavuḥ su̱taso̍mā a̱bhi praya̍ḥ | bṛ̱had bhā bibhra̍to ha̱vir agne̱ martā̍ya dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**1.45.9**

prā̱ta̱ryāvṇa̍ḥ sahaskṛta soma̱peyā̍ya santya | i̱hādya daivya̱ṁ jana̍m ba̱rhir ā sā̍dayā vaso ||

**1.45.10**

a̱rvāñca̱ṁ daivya̱ṁ jana̱m agne̱ yakṣva̱ sahū̍tibhiḥ | a̱yaṁ soma̍ḥ sudānava̱s tam pā̍ta ti̱roa̍hnyam ||

**1.46.1**

e̱ṣo u̱ṣā apū̍rvyā̱ vy u̍cchati pri̱yā di̱vaḥ | stu̱ṣe vā̍m aśvinā bṛ̱hat ||

**1.46.2**

yā da̱srā sindhu̍mātarā mano̱tarā̍ rayī̱ṇām | dhi̱yā de̱vā va̍su̱vidā̍ ||

**1.46.3**

va̱cyante̍ vāṁ kaku̱hāso̍ jū̱rṇāyā̱m adhi̍ vi̱ṣṭapi̍ | yad vā̱ṁ ratho̱ vibhi̱ṣ patā̍t ||

**1.46.4**

ha̱viṣā̍ jā̱ro a̱pām pipa̍rti̱ papu̍rir narā | pi̱tā kuṭa̍sya carṣa̱ṇiḥ ||

**1.46.5**

ā̱dā̱ro vā̍m matī̱nāṁ nāsa̍tyā matavacasā | pā̱taṁ soma̍sya dhṛṣṇu̱yā ||

**1.46.6**

yā na̱ḥ pīpa̍rad aśvinā̱ jyoti̍ṣmatī̱ tama̍s ti̱raḥ | tām a̱sme rā̍sāthā̱m iṣa̍m ||

**1.46.7**

ā no̍ nā̱vā ma̍tī̱nāṁ yā̱tam pā̱rāya̱ ganta̍ve | yu̱ñjāthā̍m aśvinā̱ ratha̍m ||

**1.46.8**

a̱ritra̍ṁ vāṁ di̱vas pṛ̱thu tī̱rthe sindhū̍nā̱ṁ ratha̍ḥ | dhi̱yā yu̍yujra̱ inda̍vaḥ ||

**1.46.9**

di̱vas ka̍ṇvāsa̱ inda̍vo̱ vasu̱ sindhū̍nām pa̱de | svaṁ va̱vriṁ kuha̍ dhitsathaḥ ||

**1.46.10**

abhū̍d u̱ bhā u̍ a̱ṁśave̱ hira̍ṇya̱m prati̱ sūrya̍ḥ ||

**1.46.11**

abhū̍d u pā̱ram eta̍ve̱ panthā̍ ṛ̱tasya̍ sādhu̱yā | ada̍rśi̱ vi sru̱tir di̱vaḥ ||

**1.46.12**

tat-ta̱d id a̱śvino̱r avo̍ jari̱tā prati̍ bhūṣati | made̱ soma̍sya̱ pipra̍toḥ ||

**1.46.13**

vā̱va̱sā̱nā vi̱vasva̍ti̱ soma̍sya pī̱tyā gi̱rā | ma̱nu̱ṣvac cha̍mbhū̱ ā ga̍tam ||

**1.46.14**

yu̱vor u̱ṣā anu̱ śriya̱m pari̍jmanor u̱pāca̍rat | ṛ̱tā va̍natho a̱ktubhi̍ḥ ||

**1.46.15**

u̱bhā pi̍batam aśvino̱bhā na̱ḥ śarma̍ yacchatam | a̱vi̱dri̱yābhi̍r ū̱tibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.47.1**

a̱yaṁ vā̱m madhu̍mattamaḥ su̱taḥ soma̍ ṛtāvṛdhā | tam a̍śvinā pibataṁ ti̱roa̍hnyaṁ dha̱ttaṁ ratnā̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**1.47.2**

tri̱va̱ndhu̱reṇa̍ tri̱vṛtā̍ su̱peśa̍sā̱ rathe̱nā yā̍tam aśvinā | kaṇvā̍so vā̱m brahma̍ kṛṇvanty adhva̱re teṣā̱ṁ su śṛ̍ṇuta̱ṁ hava̍m ||

**1.47.3**

aśvi̍nā̱ madhu̍mattamam pā̱taṁ soma̍m ṛtāvṛdhā | athā̱dya da̍srā̱ vasu̱ bibhra̍tā̱ rathe̍ dā̱śvāṁsa̱m upa̍ gacchatam ||

**1.47.4**

tri̱ṣa̱dha̱sthe ba̱rhiṣi̍ viśvavedasā̱ madhvā̍ ya̱jñam mi̍mikṣatam | kaṇvā̍so vāṁ su̱taso̍mā a̱bhidya̍vo yu̱vāṁ ha̍vante aśvinā ||

**1.47.5**

yābhi̱ḥ kaṇva̍m a̱bhiṣṭi̍bhi̱ḥ prāva̍taṁ yu̱vam a̍śvinā | tābhi̱ḥ ṣv a1̱̍smām̐ a̍vataṁ śubhas patī pā̱taṁ soma̍m ṛtāvṛdhā ||

**1.47.6**

su̱dāse̍ dasrā̱ vasu̱ bibhra̍tā̱ rathe̱ pṛkṣo̍ vahatam aśvinā | ra̱yiṁ sa̍mu̱drād u̱ta vā̍ di̱vas pary a̱sme dha̍ttam puru̱spṛha̍m ||

**1.47.7**

yan nā̍satyā parā̱vati̱ yad vā̱ stho adhi̍ tu̱rvaśe̍ | ato̱ rathe̍na su̱vṛtā̍ na̱ ā ga̍taṁ sā̱kaṁ sūrya̍sya ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.47.8**

a̱rvāñcā̍ vā̱ṁ sapta̍yo 'dhvara̱śriyo̱ vaha̍ntu̱ sava̱ned upa̍ | iṣa̍m pṛ̱ñcantā̍ su̱kṛte̍ su̱dāna̍va̱ ā ba̱rhiḥ sī̍dataṁ narā ||

**1.47.9**

tena̍ nāsa̱tyā ga̍ta̱ṁ rathe̍na̱ sūrya̍tvacā | yena̱ śaśva̍d ū̱hathu̍r dā̱śuṣe̱ vasu̱ madhva̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ ||

**1.47.10**

u̱kthebhi̍r a̱rvāg ava̍se purū̱vasū̍ a̱rkaiś ca̱ ni hva̍yāmahe | śaśva̱t kaṇvā̍nā̱ṁ sada̍si pri̱ye hi ka̱ṁ soma̍m pa̱pathu̍r aśvinā ||

**1.48.1**

sa̱ha vā̱mena̍ na uṣo̱ vy u̍cchā duhitar divaḥ | sa̱ha dyu̱mnena̍ bṛha̱tā vi̍bhāvari rā̱yā de̍vi̱ dāsva̍tī ||

**1.48.2**

aśvā̍vatī̱r goma̍tīr viśvasu̱vido̱ bhūri̍ cyavanta̱ vasta̍ve | ud ī̍raya̱ prati̍ mā sū̱nṛtā̍ uṣa̱ś coda̱ rādho̍ ma̱ghonā̍m ||

**1.48.3**

u̱vāso̱ṣā u̱cchāc ca̱ nu de̱vī jī̱rā rathā̍nām | ye a̍syā ā̱cara̍ṇeṣu dadhri̱re sa̍mu̱dre na śra̍va̱syava̍ḥ ||

**1.48.4**

uṣo̱ ye te̱ pra yāme̍ṣu yu̱ñjate̱ mano̍ dā̱nāya̍ sū̱raya̍ḥ ||

**1.48.5**

ā ghā̱ yoṣe̍va sū̱nary u̱ṣā yā̍ti prabhuñja̱tī | ja̱raya̍ntī̱ vṛja̍nam pa̱dvad ī̍yata̱ ut pā̍tayati pa̱kṣiṇa̍ḥ ||

**1.48.6**

vi yā sṛ̱jati̱ sama̍na̱ṁ vy a1̱̍rthina̍ḥ pa̱daṁ na ve̱ty oda̍tī | vayo̱ naki̍ṣ ṭe papti̱vāṁsa̍ āsate̱ vyu̍ṣṭau vājinīvati ||

**1.48.7**

e̱ṣāyu̍kta parā̱vata̱ḥ sūrya̍syo̱daya̍nā̱d adhi̍ | śa̱taṁ rathe̍bhiḥ su̱bhago̱ṣā i̱yaṁ vi yā̍ty a̱bhi mānu̍ṣān ||

**1.48.8**

viśva̍m asyā nānāma̱ cakṣa̍se̱ jaga̱j jyoti̍ṣ kṛṇoti sū̱narī̍ | apa̱ dveṣo̍ ma̱ghonī̍ duhi̱tā di̱va u̱ṣā u̍ccha̱d apa̱ sridha̍ḥ ||

**1.48.9**

uṣa̱ ā bhā̍hi bhā̱nunā̍ ca̱ndreṇa̍ duhitar divaḥ | ā̱vaha̍ntī̱ bhūry a̱smabhya̱ṁ saubha̍gaṁ vyu̱cchantī̱ divi̍ṣṭiṣu ||

**1.48.10**

viśva̍sya̱ hi prāṇa̍na̱ṁ jīva̍na̱ṁ tve vi yad u̱cchasi̍ sūnari | sā no̱ rathe̍na bṛha̱tā vi̍bhāvari śru̱dhi ci̍trāmaghe̱ hava̍m ||

**1.48.11**

uṣo̱ vāja̱ṁ hi vaṁsva̱ yaś ci̱tro mānu̍ṣe̱ jane̍ | tenā va̍ha su̱kṛto̍ adhva̱rām̐ upa̱ ye tvā̍ gṛ̱ṇanti̱ vahna̍yaḥ ||

**1.48.12**

viśvā̍n de̱vām̐ ā va̍ha̱ soma̍pītaye̱ 'ntari̍kṣād uṣa̱s tvam | sāsmāsu̍ dhā̱ goma̱d aśvā̍vad u̱kthya1̱̍m uṣo̱ vāja̍ṁ su̱vīrya̍m ||

**1.48.13**

yasyā̱ ruśa̍nto a̱rcaya̱ḥ prati̍ bha̱drā adṛ̍kṣata | sā no̍ ra̱yiṁ vi̱śvavā̍raṁ su̱peśa̍sam u̱ṣā da̍dātu̱ sugmya̍m ||

**1.48.14**

ye ci̱d dhi tvām ṛṣa̍ya̱ḥ pūrva̍ ū̱taye̍ juhū̱re 'va̍se mahi | sā na̱ḥ stomā̍m̐ a̱bhi gṛ̍ṇīhi̱ rādha̱soṣa̍ḥ śu̱kreṇa̍ śo̱ciṣā̍ ||

**1.48.15**

uṣo̱ yad a̱dya bhā̱nunā̱ vi dvārā̍v ṛ̱ṇavo̍ di̱vaḥ | pra no̍ yacchatād avṛ̱kam pṛ̱thu ccha̱rdiḥ pra de̍vi̱ goma̍tī̱r iṣa̍ḥ ||

**1.48.16**

saṁ no̍ rā̱yā bṛ̍ha̱tā vi̱śvape̍śasā mimi̱kṣvā sam iḻā̍bhi̱r ā | saṁ dyu̱mnena̍ viśva̱turo̍ṣo mahi̱ saṁ vājai̍r vājinīvati ||

**1.49.1**

uṣo̍ bha̱drebhi̱r ā ga̍hi di̱vaś ci̍d roca̱nād adhi̍ | vaha̍ntv aru̱ṇapsa̍va̱ upa̍ tvā so̱mino̍ gṛ̱ham ||

**1.49.2**

su̱peśa̍saṁ su̱khaṁ ratha̱ṁ yam a̱dhyasthā̍ uṣa̱s tvam | tenā̍ su̱śrava̍sa̱ṁ jana̱m prāvā̱dya du̍hitar divaḥ ||

**1.49.3**

vaya̍ś cit te pata̱triṇo̍ dvi̱pac catu̍ṣpad arjuni | uṣa̱ḥ prāra̍nn ṛ̱tūm̐r anu̍ di̱vo ante̍bhya̱s pari̍ ||

**1.49.4**

vyu̱cchantī̱ hi ra̱śmibhi̱r viśva̍m ā̱bhāsi̍ roca̱nam ||

**1.50.1**

ud u̱ tyaṁ jā̱tave̍dasaṁ de̱vaṁ va̍hanti ke̱tava̍ḥ | dṛ̱śe viśvā̍ya̱ sūrya̍m ||

**1.50.2**

apa̱ tye tā̱yavo̍ yathā̱ nakṣa̍trā yanty a̱ktubhi̍ḥ | sūrā̍ya vi̱śvaca̍kṣase ||

**1.50.3**

adṛ̍śram asya ke̱tavo̱ vi ra̱śmayo̱ janā̱m̐ anu̍ | bhrāja̍nto a̱gnayo̍ yathā ||

**1.50.4**

ta̱raṇi̍r vi̱śvada̍rśato jyoti̱ṣkṛd a̍si sūrya | viśva̱m ā bhā̍si roca̱nam ||

**1.50.5**

pra̱tyaṅ de̱vānā̱ṁ viśa̍ḥ pra̱tyaṅṅ ud e̍ṣi̱ mānu̍ṣān | pra̱tyaṅ viśva̱ṁ sva̍r dṛ̱śe ||

**1.50.6**

yenā̍ pāvaka̱ cakṣa̍sā bhura̱ṇyanta̱ṁ janā̱m̐ anu̍ | tvaṁ va̍ruṇa̱ paśya̍si ||

**1.50.7**

vi dyām e̍ṣi̱ raja̍s pṛ̱thv ahā̱ mimā̍no a̱ktubhi̍ḥ | paśya̱ñ janmā̍ni sūrya ||

**1.50.8**

sa̱pta tvā̍ ha̱rito̱ rathe̱ vaha̍nti deva sūrya | śo̱ciṣke̍śaṁ vicakṣaṇa ||

**1.50.9**

ayu̍kta sa̱pta śu̱ndhyuva̱ḥ sūro̱ ratha̍sya na̱ptya̍ḥ | tābhi̍r yāti̱ svayu̍ktibhiḥ ||

**1.50.10**

ud va̱yaṁ tama̍sa̱s pari̱ jyoti̱ṣ paśya̍nta̱ utta̍ram | de̱vaṁ de̍va̱trā sūrya̱m aga̍nma̱ jyoti̍r utta̱mam ||

**1.50.11**

u̱dyann a̱dya mi̍tramaha ā̱roha̱nn utta̍rā̱ṁ diva̍m | hṛ̱dro̱gam mama̍ sūrya hari̱māṇa̍ṁ ca nāśaya ||

**1.50.12**

śuke̍ṣu me hari̱māṇa̍ṁ ropa̱ṇākā̍su dadhmasi | atho̍ hāridra̱veṣu̍ me hari̱māṇa̱ṁ ni da̍dhmasi ||

**1.50.13**

ud a̍gād a̱yam ā̍di̱tyo viśve̍na̱ saha̍sā sa̱ha | dvi̱ṣanta̱m mahya̍ṁ ra̱ndhaya̱n mo a̱haṁ dvi̍ṣa̱te ra̍dham ||

**1.51.1**

a̱bhi tyam me̱ṣam pu̍ruhū̱tam ṛ̱gmiya̱m indra̍ṁ gī̱rbhir ma̍datā̱ vasvo̍ arṇa̱vam | yasya̱ dyāvo̱ na vi̱cara̍nti̱ mānu̍ṣā bhu̱je maṁhi̍ṣṭham a̱bhi vipra̍m arcata ||

**1.51.2**

a̱bhīm a̍vanvan svabhi̱ṣṭim ū̱tayo̍ 'ntarikṣa̱prāṁ tavi̍ṣībhi̱r āvṛ̍tam | indra̱ṁ dakṣā̍sa ṛ̱bhavo̍ mada̱cyuta̍ṁ śa̱takra̍tu̱ṁ java̍nī sū̱nṛtāru̍hat ||

**1.51.3**

tvaṁ go̱tram aṅgi̍robhyo 'vṛṇo̱r apo̱tātra̍ye śa̱tadu̍reṣu gātu̱vit | sa̱sena̍ cid vima̱dāyā̍vaho̱ vasv ā̱jāv adri̍ṁ vāvasā̱nasya̍ na̱rtaya̍n ||

**1.51.4**

tvam a̱pām a̍pi̱dhānā̍vṛṇo̱r apādhā̍raya̱ḥ parva̍te̱ dānu̍ma̱d vasu̍ | vṛ̱traṁ yad i̍ndra̱ śava̱sāva̍dhī̱r ahi̱m ād it sūrya̍ṁ di̱vy āro̍hayo dṛ̱śe ||

**1.51.5**

tvam mā̱yābhi̱r apa̍ mā̱yino̍ 'dhamaḥ sva̱dhābhi̱r ye adhi̱ śuptā̱v aju̍hvata | tvam pipro̍r nṛmaṇa̱ḥ prāru̍ja̱ḥ pura̱ḥ pra ṛ̱jiśvā̍naṁ dasyu̱hatye̍ṣv āvitha ||

**1.51.6**

tvaṁ kutsa̍ṁ śuṣṇa̱hatye̍ṣv āvi̱thāra̍ndhayo 'tithi̱gvāya̱ śamba̍ram | ma̱hānta̍ṁ cid arbu̱daṁ ni kra̍mīḥ pa̱dā sa̱nād e̱va da̍syu̱hatyā̍ya jajñiṣe ||

**1.51.7**

tve viśvā̱ tavi̍ṣī sa̱dhrya̍g ghi̱tā tava̱ rādha̍ḥ somapī̱thāya̍ harṣate | tava̱ vajra̍ś cikite bā̱hvor hi̱to vṛ̱ścā śatro̱r ava̱ viśvā̍ni̱ vṛṣṇyā̍ ||

**1.51.8**

vi jā̍nī̱hy āryā̱n ye ca̱ dasya̍vo ba̱rhiṣma̍te randhayā̱ śāsa̍d avra̱tān | śākī̍ bhava̱ yaja̍mānasya codi̱tā viśvet tā te̍ sadha̱māde̍ṣu cākana ||

**1.51.9**

anu̍vratāya ra̱ndhaya̱nn apa̍vratān ā̱bhūbhi̱r indra̍ḥ śna̱thaya̱nn anā̍bhuvaḥ | vṛ̱ddhasya̍ ci̱d vardha̍to̱ dyām ina̍kṣata̱ḥ stavā̍no va̱mro vi ja̍ghāna sa̱ṁdiha̍ḥ ||

**1.51.10**

takṣa̱d yat ta̍ u̱śanā̱ saha̍sā̱ saho̱ vi roda̍sī ma̱jmanā̍ bādhate̱ śava̍ḥ | ā tvā̱ vāta̍sya nṛmaṇo mano̱yuja̱ ā pūrya̍māṇam avahann a̱bhi śrava̍ḥ ||

**1.51.11**

mandi̍ṣṭa̱ yad u̱śane̍ kā̱vye sacā̱m̐ indro̍ va̱ṅkū va̍ṅku̱tarādhi̍ tiṣṭhati | u̱gro ya̱yiṁ nir a̱paḥ srota̍sāsṛja̱d vi śuṣṇa̍sya dṛṁhi̱tā ai̍raya̱t pura̍ḥ ||

**1.51.12**

ā smā̱ ratha̍ṁ vṛṣa̱pāṇe̍ṣu tiṣṭhasi śāryā̱tasya̱ prabhṛ̍tā̱ yeṣu̱ manda̍se | indra̱ yathā̍ su̱taso̍meṣu cā̱kano̍ 'na̱rvāṇa̱ṁ śloka̱m ā ro̍hase di̱vi ||

**1.51.13**

ada̍dā̱ arbhā̍m maha̱te va̍ca̱syave̍ ka̱kṣīva̍te vṛca̱yām i̍ndra sunva̱te | menā̍bhavo vṛṣaṇa̱śvasya̍ sukrato̱ viśvet tā te̱ sava̍neṣu pra̱vācyā̍ ||

**1.51.14**

indro̍ aśrāyi su̱dhyo̍ nire̱ke pa̱jreṣu̱ stomo̱ duryo̱ na yūpa̍ḥ | a̱śva̱yur ga̱vyū ra̍tha̱yur va̍sū̱yur indra̱ id rā̱yaḥ kṣa̍yati praya̱ntā ||

**1.51.15**

i̱daṁ namo̍ vṛṣa̱bhāya̍ sva̱rāje̍ sa̱tyaśu̍ṣmāya ta̱vase̍ 'vāci | a̱sminn i̍ndra vṛ̱jane̱ sarva̍vīrā̱ḥ smat sū̱ribhi̱s tava̱ śarma̍n syāma ||

**1.52.1**

tyaṁ su me̱ṣam ma̍hayā sva̱rvida̍ṁ śa̱taṁ yasya̍ su̱bhva̍ḥ sā̱kam īra̍te | atya̱ṁ na vāja̍ṁ havana̱syada̍ṁ ratha̱m endra̍ṁ vavṛtyā̱m ava̍se suvṛ̱ktibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.52.2**

sa parva̍to̱ na dha̱ruṇe̱ṣv acyu̍taḥ sa̱hasra̍mūti̱s tavi̍ṣīṣu vāvṛdhe | indro̱ yad vṛ̱tram ava̍dhīn nadī̱vṛta̍m u̱bjann arṇā̍ṁsi̱ jarhṛ̍ṣāṇo̱ andha̍sā ||

**1.52.3**

sa hi dva̱ro dva̱riṣu̍ va̱vra ūdha̍ni ca̱ndrabu̍dhno̱ mada̍vṛddho manī̱ṣibhi̍ḥ | indra̱ṁ tam a̍hve svapa̱syayā̍ dhi̱yā maṁhi̍ṣṭharāti̱ṁ sa hi papri̱r andha̍saḥ ||

**1.52.4**

ā yam pṛ̱ṇanti̍ di̱vi sadma̍barhiṣaḥ samu̱draṁ na su̱bhva1̱̍ḥ svā a̱bhiṣṭa̍yaḥ | taṁ vṛ̍tra̱hatye̱ anu̍ tasthur ū̱taya̱ḥ śuṣmā̱ indra̍m avā̱tā ahru̍tapsavaḥ ||

**1.52.5**

a̱bhi svavṛ̍ṣṭi̱m made̍ asya̱ yudhya̍to ra̱ghvīr i̍va prava̱ṇe sa̍srur ū̱taya̍ḥ | indro̱ yad va̱jrī dhṛ̱ṣamā̍ṇo̱ andha̍sā bhi̱nad va̱lasya̍ pari̱dhīm̐r i̍va tri̱taḥ ||

**1.52.6**

parī̍ṁ ghṛ̱ṇā ca̍rati titvi̱ṣe śavo̱ 'po vṛ̱tvī raja̍so bu̱dhnam āśa̍yat | vṛ̱trasya̱ yat pra̍va̱ṇe du̱rgṛbhi̍śvano nija̱ghantha̱ hanvo̍r indra tanya̱tum ||

**1.52.7**

hra̱daṁ na hi tvā̍ nyṛ̱ṣanty ū̱rmayo̱ brahmā̍ṇīndra̱ tava̱ yāni̱ vardha̍nā | tvaṣṭā̍ cit te̱ yujya̍ṁ vāvṛdhe̱ śava̍s ta̱takṣa̱ vajra̍m a̱bhibhū̍tyojasam ||

**1.52.8**

ja̱gha̱nvām̐ u̱ hari̍bhiḥ sambhṛtakrata̱v indra̍ vṛ̱tram manu̍ṣe gātu̱yann a̱paḥ | aya̍cchathā bā̱hvor vajra̍m āya̱sam adhā̍rayo di̱vy ā sūrya̍ṁ dṛ̱śe ||

**1.52.9**

bṛ̱hat svaśca̍ndra̱m ama̍va̱d yad u̱kthya1̱̍m akṛ̍ṇvata bhi̱yasā̱ roha̍ṇaṁ di̱vaḥ | yan mānu̍ṣapradhanā̱ indra̍m ū̱taya̱ḥ sva̍r nṛ̱ṣāco̍ ma̱ruto 'ma̍da̱nn anu̍ ||

**1.52.10**

dyauś ci̍d a̱syāma̍vā̱m̐ ahe̍ḥ sva̱nād ayo̍yavīd bhi̱yasā̱ vajra̍ indra te | vṛ̱trasya̱ yad ba̍dbadhā̱nasya̍ rodasī̱ made̍ su̱tasya̱ śava̱sābhi̍na̱c chira̍ḥ ||

**1.52.11**

yad in nv i̍ndra pṛthi̱vī daśa̍bhuji̱r ahā̍ni̱ viśvā̍ ta̱tana̍nta kṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ ||

**1.52.12**

tvam a̱sya pā̱re raja̍so̱ vyo̍mana̱ḥ svabhū̍tyojā̱ ava̍se dhṛṣanmanaḥ | ca̱kṛ̱ṣe bhūmi̍m prati̱māna̱m oja̍so̱ 'paḥ sva̍ḥ pari̱bhūr e̱ṣy ā diva̍m ||

**1.52.13**

tvam bhu̍vaḥ prati̱māna̍m pṛthi̱vyā ṛ̱ṣvavī̍rasya bṛha̱taḥ pati̍r bhūḥ | viśva̱m āprā̍ a̱ntari̍kṣam mahi̱tvā sa̱tyam a̱ddhā naki̍r a̱nyas tvāvā̍n ||

**1.52.14**

na yasya̱ dyāvā̍pṛthi̱vī anu̱ vyaco̱ na sindha̍vo̱ raja̍so̱ anta̍m āna̱śuḥ | nota svavṛ̍ṣṭi̱m made̍ asya̱ yudhya̍ta̱ eko̍ a̱nyac ca̍kṛṣe̱ viśva̍m ānu̱ṣak ||

**1.52.15**

ārca̱nn atra̍ ma̱ruta̱ḥ sasmi̍nn ā̱jau viśve̍ de̱vāso̍ amada̱nn anu̍ tvā | vṛ̱trasya̱ yad bhṛ̍ṣṭi̱matā̍ va̱dhena̱ ni tvam i̍ndra̱ praty ā̱naṁ ja̱ghantha̍ ||

**1.53.1**

ny ū̱3̱̍ ṣu vāca̱m pra ma̱he bha̍rāmahe̱ gira̱ indrā̍ya̱ sada̍ne vi̱vasva̍taḥ | nū ci̱d dhi ratna̍ṁ sasa̱tām i̱vāvi̍da̱n na du̍ṣṭu̱tir dra̍viṇo̱deṣu̍ śasyate ||

**1.53.2**

du̱ro aśva̍sya du̱ra i̍ndra̱ gor a̍si du̱ro yava̍sya̱ vasu̍na i̱nas pati̍ḥ | śi̱kṣā̱na̱raḥ pra̱divo̱ akā̍makarśana̱ḥ sakhā̱ sakhi̍bhya̱s tam i̱daṁ gṛ̍ṇīmasi ||

**1.53.3**

śacī̍va indra purukṛd dyumattama̱ taved i̱dam a̱bhita̍ś cekite̱ vasu̍ | ata̍ḥ sa̱ṁgṛbhyā̍bhibhūta̱ ā bha̍ra̱ mā tvā̍ya̱to ja̍ri̱tuḥ kāma̍m ūnayīḥ ||

**1.53.4**

e̱bhir dyubhi̍ḥ su̱manā̍ e̱bhir indu̍bhir nirundhā̱no ama̍ti̱ṁ gobhi̍r a̱śvinā̍ | indre̍ṇa̱ dasyu̍ṁ da̱raya̍nta̱ indu̍bhir yu̱tadve̍ṣasa̱ḥ sam i̱ṣā ra̍bhemahi ||

**1.53.5**

sam i̍ndra rā̱yā sam i̱ṣā ra̍bhemahi̱ saṁ vāje̍bhiḥ puruśca̱ndrair a̱bhidyu̍bhiḥ | saṁ de̱vyā prama̍tyā vī̱raśu̍ṣmayā̱ goa̍gra̱yāśvā̍vatyā rabhemahi ||

**1.53.6**

te tvā̱ madā̍ amada̱n tāni̱ vṛṣṇyā̱ te somā̍so vṛtra̱hatye̍ṣu satpate | yat kā̱rave̱ daśa̍ vṛ̱trāṇy a̍pra̱ti ba̱rhiṣma̍te̱ ni sa̱hasrā̍ṇi ba̱rhaya̍ḥ ||

**1.53.7**

yu̱dhā yudha̱m upa̱ ghed e̍ṣi dhṛṣṇu̱yā pu̱rā pura̱ṁ sam i̱daṁ ha̱ṁsy oja̍sā | namyā̱ yad i̍ndra̱ sakhyā̍ parā̱vati̍ niba̱rhayo̱ namu̍ci̱ṁ nāma̍ mā̱yina̍m ||

**1.53.8**

tvaṁ kara̍ñjam u̱ta pa̱rṇaya̍ṁ vadhī̱s teji̍ṣṭhayātithi̱gvasya̍ varta̱nī | tvaṁ śa̱tā vaṅgṛ̍dasyābhina̱t puro̍ 'nānu̱daḥ pari̍ṣūtā ṛ̱jiśva̍nā ||

**1.53.9**

tvam e̱tāñ ja̍na̱rājño̱ dvir daśā̍ba̱ndhunā̍ su̱śrava̍sopaja̱gmuṣa̍ḥ | ṣa̱ṣṭiṁ sa̱hasrā̍ nava̱tiṁ nava̍ śru̱to ni ca̱kreṇa̱ rathyā̍ du̱ṣpadā̍vṛṇak ||

**1.53.10**

tvam ā̍vitha su̱śrava̍sa̱ṁ tavo̱tibhi̱s tava̱ trāma̍bhir indra̱ tūrva̍yāṇam | tvam a̍smai̱ kutsa̍m atithi̱gvam ā̱yum ma̱he rājñe̱ yūne̍ arandhanāyaḥ ||

**1.53.11**

ya u̱dṛcī̍ndra de̱vago̍pā̱ḥ sakhā̍yas te śi̱vata̍mā̱ asā̍ma | tvāṁ sto̍ṣāma̱ tvayā̍ su̱vīrā̱ drāghī̍ya̱ āyu̍ḥ prata̱raṁ dadhā̍nāḥ ||

**1.54.1**

mā no̍ a̱smin ma̍ghavan pṛ̱tsv aṁha̍si na̱hi te̱ anta̱ḥ śava̍saḥ parī̱ṇaśe̍ | akra̍ndayo na̱dyo̱3̱̍ roru̍va̱d vanā̍ ka̱thā na kṣo̱ṇīr bhi̱yasā̱ sam ā̍rata ||

**1.54.2**

arcā̍ śa̱krāya̍ śā̱kine̱ śacī̍vate śṛ̱ṇvanta̱m indra̍m ma̱haya̍nn a̱bhi ṣṭu̍hi | yo dhṛ̱ṣṇunā̱ śava̍sā̱ roda̍sī u̱bhe vṛṣā̍ vṛṣa̱tvā vṛ̍ṣa̱bho nyṛ̱ñjate̍ ||

**1.54.3**

arcā̍ di̱ve bṛ̍ha̱te śū̱ṣya1̱̍ṁ vaca̱ḥ svakṣa̍tra̱ṁ yasya̍ dhṛṣa̱to dhṛ̱ṣan mana̍ḥ | bṛ̱hacchra̍vā̱ asu̍ro ba̱rhaṇā̍ kṛ̱taḥ pu̱ro hari̍bhyāṁ vṛṣa̱bho ratho̱ hi ṣaḥ ||

**1.54.4**

tvaṁ di̱vo bṛ̍ha̱taḥ sānu̍ kopa̱yo 'va̱ tmanā̍ dhṛṣa̱tā śamba̍ram bhinat | yan mā̱yino̍ vra̱ndino̍ ma̱ndinā̍ dhṛ̱ṣac chi̱tāṁ gabha̍stim a̱śani̍m pṛta̱nyasi̍ ||

**1.54.5**

ni yad vṛ̱ṇakṣi̍ śvasa̱nasya̍ mū̱rdhani̱ śuṣṇa̍sya cid vra̱ndino̱ roru̍va̱d vanā̍ | prā̱cīne̍na̱ mana̍sā ba̱rhaṇā̍vatā̱ yad a̱dyā ci̍t kṛ̱ṇava̱ḥ kas tvā̱ pari̍ ||

**1.54.6**

tvam ā̍vitha̱ narya̍ṁ tu̱rvaśa̱ṁ yadu̱ṁ tvaṁ tu̱rvīti̍ṁ va̱yya̍ṁ śatakrato | tvaṁ ratha̱m eta̍śa̱ṁ kṛtvye̱ dhane̱ tvam puro̍ nava̱tiṁ da̍mbhayo̱ nava̍ ||

**1.54.7**

sa ghā̱ rājā̱ satpa̍tiḥ śūśuva̱j jano̍ rā̱taha̍vya̱ḥ prati̱ yaḥ śāsa̱m inva̍ti | u̱kthā vā̱ yo a̍bhigṛ̱ṇāti̱ rādha̍sā̱ dānu̍r asmā̱ upa̍rā pinvate di̱vaḥ ||

**1.54.8**

asa̍maṁ kṣa̱tram asa̍mā manī̱ṣā pra so̍ma̱pā apa̍sā santu̱ neme̍ | ye ta̍ indra da̱duṣo̍ va̱rdhaya̍nti̱ mahi̍ kṣa̱traṁ sthavi̍ra̱ṁ vṛṣṇya̍ṁ ca ||

**1.54.9**

tubhyed e̱te ba̍hu̱lā adri̍dugdhāś camū̱ṣada̍ś cama̱sā i̍ndra̱pānā̍ḥ | vy a̍śnuhi ta̱rpayā̱ kāma̍m eṣā̱m athā̱ mano̍ vasu̱deyā̍ya kṛṣva ||

**1.54.10**

a̱pām a̍tiṣṭhad dha̱ruṇa̍hvara̱ṁ tamo̱ 'ntar vṛ̱trasya̍ ja̱ṭhare̍ṣu̱ parva̍taḥ | a̱bhīm indro̍ na̱dyo̍ va̱vriṇā̍ hi̱tā viśvā̍ anu̱ṣṭhāḥ pra̍va̱ṇeṣu̍ jighnate ||

**1.54.11**

sa śevṛ̍dha̱m adhi̍ dhā dyu̱mnam a̱sme mahi̍ kṣa̱traṁ ja̍nā̱ṣāḻ i̍ndra̱ tavya̍m | rakṣā̍ ca no ma̱ghona̍ḥ pā̱hi sū̱rīn rā̱ye ca̍ naḥ svapa̱tyā i̱ṣe dhā̍ḥ ||

**1.55.1**

di̱vaś ci̍d asya vari̱mā vi pa̍pratha̱ indra̱ṁ na ma̱hnā pṛ̍thi̱vī ca̱na prati̍ | bhī̱mas tuvi̍ṣmāñ carṣa̱ṇibhya̍ āta̱paḥ śiśī̍te̱ vajra̱ṁ teja̍se̱ na vaṁsa̍gaḥ ||

**1.55.2**

so a̍rṇa̱vo na na̱dya̍ḥ samu̱driya̱ḥ prati̍ gṛbhṇāti̱ viśri̍tā̱ varī̍mabhiḥ | indra̱ḥ soma̍sya pī̱taye̍ vṛṣāyate sa̱nāt sa yu̱dhma oja̍sā panasyate ||

**1.55.3**

tvaṁ tam i̍ndra̱ parva̍ta̱ṁ na bhoja̍se ma̱ho nṛ̱mṇasya̱ dharma̍ṇām irajyasi | pra vī̱rye̍ṇa de̱vatāti̍ cekite̱ viśva̍smā u̱graḥ karma̍ṇe pu̱rohi̍taḥ ||

**1.55.4**

sa id vane̍ nama̱syubhi̍r vacasyate̱ cāru̱ jane̍ṣu prabruvā̱ṇa i̍ndri̱yam | vṛṣā̱ chandu̍r bhavati harya̱to vṛṣā̱ kṣeme̍ṇa̱ dhenā̍m ma̱ghavā̱ yad inva̍ti ||

**1.55.5**

sa in ma̱hāni̍ sami̱thāni̍ ma̱jmanā̍ kṛ̱ṇoti̍ yu̱dhma oja̍sā̱ jane̍bhyaḥ | adhā̍ ca̱na śrad da̍dhati̱ tviṣī̍mata̱ indrā̍ya̱ vajra̍ṁ ni̱ghani̍ghnate va̱dham ||

**1.55.6**

sa hi śra̍va̱syuḥ sada̍nāni kṛ̱trimā̍ kṣma̱yā vṛ̍dhā̱na oja̍sā vinā̱śaya̍n | jyotī̍ṁṣi kṛ̱ṇvann a̍vṛ̱kāṇi̱ yajya̱ve 'va̍ su̱kratu̱ḥ sarta̱vā a̱paḥ sṛ̍jat ||

**1.55.7**

dā̱nāya̱ mana̍ḥ somapāvann astu te̱ 'rvāñcā̱ harī̍ vandanaśru̱d ā kṛ̍dhi | yami̍ṣṭhāsa̱ḥ sāra̍thayo̱ ya i̍ndra te̱ na tvā̱ ketā̱ ā da̍bhnuvanti̱ bhūrṇa̍yaḥ ||

**1.55.8**

apra̍kṣita̱ṁ vasu̍ bibharṣi̱ hasta̍yo̱r aṣā̍ḻha̱ṁ saha̍s ta̱nvi̍ śru̱to da̍dhe | āvṛ̍tāso 'va̱tāso̱ na ka̱rtṛbhi̍s ta̱nūṣu̍ te̱ krata̍va indra̱ bhūra̍yaḥ ||

**1.56.1**

e̱ṣa pra pū̱rvīr ava̱ tasya̍ ca̱mriṣo 'tyo̱ na yoṣā̱m ud a̍yaṁsta bhu̱rvaṇi̍ḥ ||

**1.56.2**

taṁ gū̱rtayo̍ nema̱nniṣa̱ḥ parī̍ṇasaḥ samu̱draṁ na sa̱ṁcara̍ṇe sani̱ṣyava̍ḥ ||

**1.56.3**

sa tu̱rvaṇi̍r ma̱hām̐ a̍re̱ṇu pauṁsye̍ gi̱rer bhṛ̱ṣṭir na bhrā̍jate tu̱jā śava̍ḥ ||

**1.56.4**

de̱vī yadi̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ tvāvṛ̍dho̱taya̱ indra̱ṁ siṣa̍kty u̱ṣasa̱ṁ na sūrya̍ḥ ||

**1.56.5**

vi yat ti̱ro dha̱ruṇa̱m acyu̍ta̱ṁ rajo 'ti̍ṣṭhipo di̱va ātā̍su ba̱rhaṇā̍ ||

**1.56.6**

tvaṁ di̱vo dha̱ruṇa̍ṁ dhiṣa̱ oja̍sā pṛthi̱vyā i̍ndra̱ sada̍neṣu̱ māhi̍naḥ | tvaṁ su̱tasya̱ made̍ ariṇā a̱po vi vṛ̱trasya̍ sa̱mayā̍ pā̱ṣyā̍rujaḥ ||

**1.57.1**

pra maṁhi̍ṣṭhāya bṛha̱te bṛ̱hadra̍ye sa̱tyaśu̍ṣmāya ta̱vase̍ ma̱tim bha̍re | a̱pām i̍va prava̱ṇe yasya̍ du̱rdhara̱ṁ rādho̍ vi̱śvāyu̱ śava̍se̱ apā̍vṛtam ||

**1.57.2**

adha̍ te̱ viśva̱m anu̍ hāsad i̱ṣṭaya̱ āpo̍ ni̱mneva̱ sava̍nā ha̱viṣma̍taḥ | yat parva̍te̱ na sa̱maśī̍ta harya̱ta indra̍sya̱ vajra̱ḥ śnathi̍tā hira̱ṇyaya̍ḥ ||

**1.57.3**

a̱smai bhī̱māya̱ nama̍sā̱ sam a̍dhva̱ra uṣo̱ na śu̍bhra̱ ā bha̍rā̱ panī̍yase | yasya̱ dhāma̱ śrava̍se̱ nāme̍ndri̱yaṁ jyoti̱r akā̍ri ha̱rito̱ nāya̍se ||

**1.57.4**

i̱me ta̍ indra̱ te va̱yam pu̍ruṣṭuta̱ ye tvā̱rabhya̱ carā̍masi prabhūvaso | na̱hi tvad a̱nyo gi̍rvaṇo̱ gira̱ḥ sagha̍t kṣo̱ṇīr i̍va̱ prati̍ no harya̱ tad vaca̍ḥ ||

**1.57.5**

bhūri̍ ta indra vī̱rya1̱̍ṁ tava̍ smasy a̱sya sto̱tur ma̍ghava̱n kāma̱m ā pṛ̍ṇa | anu̍ te̱ dyaur bṛ̍ha̱tī vī̱rya̍m mama i̱yaṁ ca̍ te pṛthi̱vī ne̍ma̱ oja̍se ||

**1.57.6**

tvaṁ tam i̍ndra̱ parva̍tam ma̱hām u̱ruṁ vajre̍ṇa vajrin parva̱śaś ca̍kartitha | avā̍sṛjo̱ nivṛ̍tā̱ḥ sarta̱vā a̱paḥ sa̱trā viśva̍ṁ dadhiṣe̱ keva̍la̱ṁ saha̍ḥ ||

**1.58.1**

nū ci̍t saho̱jā a̱mṛto̱ ni tu̍ndate̱ hotā̱ yad dū̱to abha̍vad vi̱vasva̍taḥ | vi sādhi̍ṣṭhebhiḥ pa̱thibhī̱ rajo̍ mama̱ ā de̱vatā̍tā ha̱viṣā̍ vivāsati ||

**1.58.2**

ā svam adma̍ yu̱vamā̍no a̱jara̍s tṛ̱ṣv a̍vi̱ṣyann a̍ta̱seṣu̍ tiṣṭhati | atyo̱ na pṛ̱ṣṭham pru̍ṣi̱tasya̍ rocate di̱vo na sānu̍ sta̱naya̍nn acikradat ||

**1.58.3**

krā̱ṇā ru̱drebhi̱r vasu̍bhiḥ pu̱rohi̍to̱ hotā̱ niṣa̍tto rayi̱ṣāḻ ama̍rtyaḥ | ratho̱ na vi̱kṣv ṛ̍ñjasā̱na ā̱yuṣu̱ vy ā̍nu̱ṣag vāryā̍ de̱va ṛ̍ṇvati ||

**1.58.4**

vi vāta̍jūto ata̱seṣu̍ tiṣṭhate̱ vṛthā̍ ju̱hūbhi̱ḥ sṛṇyā̍ tuvi̱ṣvaṇi̍ḥ ||

**1.58.5**

tapu̍rjambho̱ vana̱ ā vāta̍codito yū̱the na sā̱hvām̐ ava̍ vāti̱ vaṁsa̍gaḥ ||

**1.58.6**

da̱dhuṣ ṭvā̱ bhṛga̍vo̱ mānu̍ṣe̱ṣv ā ra̱yiṁ na cāru̍ṁ su̱hava̱ṁ jane̍bhyaḥ | hotā̍ram agne̱ ati̍thi̱ṁ vare̍ṇyam mi̱traṁ na śeva̍ṁ di̱vyāya̱ janma̍ne ||

**1.58.7**

hotā̍raṁ sa̱pta ju̱hvo̱3̱̍ yaji̍ṣṭha̱ṁ yaṁ vā̱ghato̍ vṛ̱ṇate̍ adhva̱reṣu̍ | a̱gniṁ viśve̍ṣām ara̱tiṁ vasū̍nāṁ sapa̱ryāmi̱ praya̍sā̱ yāmi̱ ratna̍m ||

**1.58.8**

acchi̍drā sūno sahaso no a̱dya sto̱tṛbhyo̍ mitramaha̱ḥ śarma̍ yaccha | agne̍ gṛ̱ṇanta̱m aṁha̍sa uru̱ṣyorjo̍ napāt pū̱rbhir āya̍sībhiḥ ||

**1.58.9**

bhavā̱ varū̍thaṁ gṛṇa̱te vi̍bhāvo̱ bhavā̍ maghavan ma̱ghava̍dbhya̱ḥ śarma̍ | u̱ru̱ṣyāgne̱ aṁha̍so gṛ̱ṇanta̍m prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||

**1.59.1**

va̱yā id a̍gne a̱gnaya̍s te a̱nye tve viśve̍ a̱mṛtā̍ mādayante | vaiśvā̍nara̱ nābhi̍r asi kṣitī̱nāṁ sthūṇe̍va̱ janā̍m̐ upa̱mid ya̍yantha ||

**1.59.2**

mū̱rdhā di̱vo nābhi̍r a̱gniḥ pṛ̍thi̱vyā athā̍bhavad ara̱tī roda̍syoḥ | taṁ tvā̍ de̱vāso̍ 'janayanta de̱vaṁ vaiśvā̍nara̱ jyoti̱r id āryā̍ya ||

**1.59.3**

ā sūrye̱ na ra̱śmayo̍ dhru̱vāso̍ vaiśvāna̱re da̍dhire̱ 'gnā vasū̍ni | yā parva̍te̱ṣv oṣa̍dhīṣv a̱psu yā mānu̍ṣe̱ṣv asi̱ tasya̱ rājā̍ ||

**1.59.4**
