bṛ̱ha̱tī i̍va sū̱nave̱ roda̍sī̱ giro̱ hotā̍ manu̱ṣyo̱3̱̍ na dakṣa̍ḥ | sva̍rvate sa̱tyaśu̍ṣmāya pū̱rvīr vai̍śvāna̱rāya̱ nṛta̍māya ya̱hvīḥ ||

**1.59.5**

di̱vaś ci̍t te bṛha̱to jā̍tavedo̱ vaiśvā̍nara̱ pra ri̍rice mahi̱tvam | rājā̍ kṛṣṭī̱nām a̍si̱ mānu̍ṣīṇāṁ yu̱dhā de̱vebhyo̱ vari̍vaś cakartha ||

**1.59.6**

pra nū ma̍hi̱tvaṁ vṛ̍ṣa̱bhasya̍ voca̱ṁ yam pū̱ravo̍ vṛtra̱haṇa̱ṁ saca̍nte | vai̱śvā̱na̱ro dasyu̍m a̱gnir ja̍gha̱nvām̐ adhū̍no̱t kāṣṭhā̱ ava̱ śamba̍ram bhet ||

**1.59.7**

vai̱śvā̱na̱ro ma̍hi̱mnā vi̱śvakṛ̍ṣṭir bha̱radvā̍jeṣu yaja̱to vi̱bhāvā̍ | śā̱ta̱va̱ne̱ye śa̱tinī̍bhir a̱gniḥ pu̍ruṇī̱the ja̍rate sū̱nṛtā̍vān ||

**1.60.1**

vahni̍ṁ ya̱śasa̍ṁ vi̱datha̍sya ke̱tuṁ su̍prā̱vya̍ṁ dū̱taṁ sa̱dyoa̍rtham | dvi̱janmā̍naṁ ra̱yim i̍va praśa̱staṁ rā̱tim bha̍ra̱d bhṛga̍ve māta̱riśvā̍ ||

**1.60.2**

a̱sya śāsu̍r u̱bhayā̍saḥ sacante ha̱viṣma̍nta u̱śijo̱ ye ca̱ martā̍ḥ | di̱vaś ci̱t pūrvo̱ ny a̍sādi̱ hotā̱pṛcchyo̍ vi̱śpati̍r vi̱kṣu ve̱dhāḥ ||

**1.60.3**

taṁ navya̍sī hṛ̱da ā jāya̍mānam a̱smat su̍kī̱rtir madhu̍jihvam aśyāḥ ||

**1.60.4**

u̱śik pā̍va̱ko vasu̱r mānu̍ṣeṣu̱ vare̍ṇyo̱ hotā̍dhāyi vi̱kṣu | damū̍nā gṛ̱hapa̍ti̱r dama̱ ām̐ a̱gnir bhu̍vad rayi̱patī̍ rayī̱ṇām ||

**1.60.5**

taṁ tvā̍ va̱yam pati̍m agne rayī̱ṇām pra śa̍ṁsāmo ma̱tibhi̱r gota̍māsaḥ | ā̱śuṁ na vā̍jambha̱ram ma̱rjaya̍ntaḥ prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||

**1.61.1**

a̱smā id u̱ pra ta̱vase̍ tu̱rāya̱ prayo̱ na ha̍rmi̱ stoma̱m māhi̍nāya | ṛcī̍ṣamā̱yādhri̍gava̱ oha̱m indrā̍ya̱ brahmā̍ṇi rā̱tata̍mā ||

**1.61.2**

a̱smā id u̱ praya̍ iva̱ pra ya̍ṁsi̱ bharā̍my āṅgū̱ṣam bādhe̍ suvṛ̱kti | indrā̍ya hṛ̱dā mana̍sā manī̱ṣā pra̱tnāya̱ patye̱ dhiyo̍ marjayanta ||

**1.61.3**

a̱smā id u̱ tyam u̍pa̱maṁ sva̱rṣām bharā̍my āṅgū̱ṣam ā̱sye̍na | maṁhi̍ṣṭha̱m accho̍ktibhir matī̱nāṁ su̍vṛ̱ktibhi̍ḥ sū̱riṁ vā̍vṛ̱dhadhyai̍ ||

**1.61.4**

a̱smā id u̱ stoma̱ṁ saṁ hi̍nomi̱ ratha̱ṁ na taṣṭe̍va̱ tatsi̍nāya | gira̍ś ca̱ girvā̍hase suvṛ̱ktīndrā̍ya viśvami̱nvam medhi̍rāya ||

**1.61.5**

a̱smā id u̱ sapti̍m iva śrava̱syendrā̍yā̱rkaṁ ju̱hvā̱3̱̍ sam a̍ñje | vī̱raṁ dā̱nauka̍saṁ va̱ndadhyai̍ pu̱rāṁ gū̱rtaśra̍vasaṁ da̱rmāṇa̍m ||

**1.61.6**

a̱smā id u̱ tvaṣṭā̍ takṣa̱d vajra̱ṁ svapa̍stamaṁ sva̱rya1̱̍ṁ raṇā̍ya | vṛ̱trasya̍ cid vi̱dad yena̱ marma̍ tu̱jann īśā̍nas tuja̱tā ki̍ye̱dhāḥ ||

**1.61.7**

a̱syed u̍ mā̱tuḥ sava̍neṣu sa̱dyo ma̱haḥ pi̱tum pa̍pi̱vāñ cārv annā̍ | mu̱ṣā̱yad viṣṇu̍ḥ paca̱taṁ sahī̍yā̱n vidhya̍d varā̱haṁ ti̱ro adri̱m astā̍ ||

**1.61.8**

a̱smā id u̱ gnāś ci̍d de̱vapa̍tnī̱r indrā̍yā̱rkam a̍hi̱hatya̍ ūvuḥ | pari̱ dyāvā̍pṛthi̱vī ja̍bhra u̱rvī nāsya̱ te ma̍hi̱māna̱m pari̍ ṣṭaḥ ||

**1.61.9**

a̱syed e̱va pra ri̍rice mahi̱tvaṁ di̱vas pṛ̍thi̱vyāḥ pary a̱ntari̍kṣāt | sva̱rāḻ indro̱ dama̱ ā vi̱śvagū̍rtaḥ sva̱rir ama̍tro vavakṣe̱ raṇā̍ya ||

**1.61.10**

a̱syed e̱va śava̍sā śu̱ṣanta̱ṁ vi vṛ̍śca̱d vajre̍ṇa vṛ̱tram indra̍ḥ | gā na vrā̱ṇā a̱vanī̍r amuñcad a̱bhi śravo̍ dā̱vane̱ sace̍tāḥ ||

**1.61.11**

a̱syed u̍ tve̱ṣasā̍ ranta̱ sindha̍va̱ḥ pari̱ yad vajre̍ṇa sī̱m aya̍cchat | ī̱śā̱na̱kṛd dā̱śuṣe̍ daśa̱syan tu̱rvīta̍ye gā̱dhaṁ tu̱rvaṇi̍ḥ kaḥ ||

**1.61.12**

a̱smā id u̱ pra bha̍rā̱ tūtu̍jāno vṛ̱trāya̱ vajra̱m īśā̍naḥ kiye̱dhāḥ | gor na parva̱ vi ra̍dā tira̱śceṣya̱nn arṇā̍ṁsy a̱pāṁ ca̱radhyai̍ ||

**1.61.13**

a̱syed u̱ pra brū̍hi pū̱rvyāṇi̍ tu̱rasya̱ karmā̍ṇi̱ navya̍ u̱kthaiḥ | yu̱dhe yad i̍ṣṇā̱na āyu̍dhāny ṛghā̱yamā̍ṇo niri̱ṇāti̱ śatrū̍n ||

**1.61.14**

a̱syed u̍ bhi̱yā gi̱raya̍ś ca dṛ̱ḻhā dyāvā̍ ca̱ bhūmā̍ ja̱nuṣa̍s tujete | upo̍ ve̱nasya̱ jogu̍vāna o̱ṇiṁ sa̱dyo bhu̍vad vī̱ryā̍ya no̱dhāḥ ||

**1.61.15**

a̱smā id u̱ tyad anu̍ dāyy eṣā̱m eko̱ yad va̱vne bhūre̱r īśā̍naḥ | praita̍śa̱ṁ sūrye̍ paspṛdhā̱naṁ sauva̍śvye̱ suṣvi̍m āva̱d indra̍ḥ ||

**1.61.16**

e̱vā te̍ hāriyojanā suvṛ̱ktīndra̱ brahmā̍ṇi̱ gota̍māso akran | aiṣu̍ vi̱śvape̍śasa̱ṁ dhiya̍ṁ dhāḥ prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||

**1.62.1**

pra ma̍nmahe śavasā̱nāya̍ śū̱ṣam ā̍ṅgū̱ṣaṁ girva̍ṇase aṅgira̱svat | su̱vṛ̱ktibhi̍ḥ stuva̱ta ṛ̍gmi̱yāyārcā̍mā̱rkaṁ nare̱ viśru̍tāya ||

**1.62.2**

pra vo̍ ma̱he mahi̱ namo̍ bharadhvam āṅgū̱ṣya̍ṁ śavasā̱nāya̱ sāma̍ | yenā̍ na̱ḥ pūrve̍ pi̱tara̍ḥ pada̱jñā arca̍nto̱ aṅgi̍raso̱ gā avi̍ndan ||

**1.62.3**

indra̱syāṅgi̍rasāṁ ce̱ṣṭau vi̱dat sa̱ramā̱ tana̍yāya dhā̱sim | bṛha̱spati̍r bhi̱nad adri̍ṁ vi̱dad gāḥ sam u̱sriyā̍bhir vāvaśanta̱ nara̍ḥ ||

**1.62.4**

sa su̱ṣṭubhā̱ sa stu̱bhā sa̱pta viprai̍ḥ sva̱reṇādri̍ṁ sva̱ryo̱3̱̍ nava̍gvaiḥ | sa̱ra̱ṇyubhi̍ḥ phali̱gam i̍ndra śakra va̱laṁ rave̍ṇa darayo̱ daśa̍gvaiḥ ||

**1.62.5**

gṛ̱ṇā̱no aṅgi̍robhir dasma̱ vi va̍r u̱ṣasā̱ sūrye̍ṇa̱ gobhi̱r andha̍ḥ | vi bhūmyā̍ aprathaya indra̱ sānu̍ di̱vo raja̱ upa̍ram astabhāyaḥ ||

**1.62.6**

tad u̱ praya̍kṣatamam asya̱ karma̍ da̱smasya̱ cāru̍tamam asti̱ daṁsa̍ḥ | u̱pa̱hva̱re yad upa̍rā̱ api̍nva̱n madhva̍rṇaso na̱dya1̱̍ś cata̍sraḥ ||

**1.62.7**

dvi̱tā vi va̍vre sa̱najā̱ sanī̍ḻe a̱yāsya̱ḥ stava̍mānebhir a̱rkaiḥ | bhago̱ na mene̍ para̱me vyo̍ma̱nn adhā̍raya̱d roda̍sī su̱daṁsā̍ḥ ||

**1.62.8**

sa̱nād diva̱m pari̱ bhūmā̱ virū̍pe puna̱rbhuvā̍ yuva̱tī svebhi̱r evai̍ḥ | kṛ̱ṣṇebhi̍r a̱ktoṣā ruśa̍dbhi̱r vapu̍rbhi̱r ā ca̍rato a̱nyānyā̍ ||

**1.62.9**

sane̍mi sa̱khyaṁ sva̍pa̱syamā̍naḥ sū̱nur dā̍dhāra̱ śava̍sā su̱daṁsā̍ḥ ||  ā̱māsu̍ cid dadhiṣe pa̱kvam a̱ntaḥ paya̍ḥ kṛ̱ṣṇāsu̱ ruśa̱d rohi̍ṇīṣu ||

**1.62.10**

sa̱nāt sanī̍ḻā a̱vanī̍r avā̱tā vra̱tā ra̍kṣante a̱mṛtā̱ḥ saho̍bhiḥ | pu̱rū sa̱hasrā̱ jana̍yo̱ na patnī̍r duva̱syanti̱ svasā̍ro̱ ahra̍yāṇam ||

**1.62.11**

sa̱nā̱yuvo̱ nama̍sā̱ navyo̍ a̱rkair va̍sū̱yavo̍ ma̱tayo̍ dasma dadruḥ ||  pati̱ṁ na patnī̍r uśa̱tīr u̱śanta̍ṁ spṛ̱śanti̍ tvā śavasāvan manī̱ṣāḥ ||

**1.62.12**

sa̱nād e̱va tava̱ rāyo̱ gabha̍stau̱ na kṣīya̍nte̱ nopa̍ dasyanti dasma | dyu̱mām̐ a̍si̱ kratu̍mām̐ indra̱ dhīra̱ḥ śikṣā̍ śacīva̱s tava̍ na̱ḥ śacī̍bhiḥ ||

**1.62.13**

sa̱nā̱ya̱te gota̍ma indra̱ navya̱m ata̍kṣa̱d brahma̍ hari̱yoja̍nāya | su̱nī̱thāya̍ naḥ śavasāna no̱dhāḥ prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||

**1.63.1**

tvam ma̱hām̐ i̍ndra̱ yo ha̱ śuṣmai̱r dyāvā̍ jajñā̱naḥ pṛ̍thi̱vī ame̍ dhāḥ | yad dha̍ te̱ viśvā̍ gi̱raya̍ś ci̱d abhvā̍ bhi̱yā dṛ̱ḻhāsa̍ḥ ki̱raṇā̱ naija̍n ||

**1.63.2**

ā yad dharī̍ indra̱ vivra̍tā̱ ver ā te̱ vajra̍ṁ jari̱tā bā̱hvor dhā̍t | yenā̍viharyatakrato a̱mitrā̱n pura̍ i̱ṣṇāsi̍ puruhūta pū̱rvīḥ ||

**1.63.3**

tvaṁ sa̱tya i̍ndra dhṛ̱ṣṇur e̱tān tvam ṛ̍bhu̱kṣā narya̱s tvaṁ ṣāṭ | tvaṁ śuṣṇa̍ṁ vṛ̱jane̍ pṛ̱kṣa ā̱ṇau yūne̱ kutsā̍ya dyu̱mate̱ sacā̍han ||

**1.63.4**

tvaṁ ha̱ tyad i̍ndra codī̱ḥ sakhā̍ vṛ̱traṁ yad va̍jrin vṛṣakarmann u̱bhnāḥ ||  yad dha̍ śūra vṛṣamaṇaḥ parā̱cair vi dasyū̱m̐r yonā̱v akṛ̍to vṛthā̱ṣāṭ ||

**1.63.5**

tvaṁ ha̱ tyad i̱ndrāri̍ṣaṇyan dṛ̱ḻhasya̍ ci̱n martā̍nā̱m aju̍ṣṭau | vy a1̱̍smad ā kāṣṭhā̱ arva̍te var gha̱neva̍ vajriñ chnathihy a̱mitrā̍n ||

**1.63.6**

tvāṁ ha̱ tyad i̱ndrārṇa̍sātau̱ sva̍rmīḻhe̱ nara̍ ā̱jā ha̍vante | tava̍ svadhāva i̱yam ā sa̍ma̱rya ū̱tir vāje̍ṣv ata̱sāyyā̍ bhūt ||

**1.63.7**

tvaṁ ha̱ tyad i̍ndra sa̱pta yudhya̱n puro̍ vajrin puru̱kutsā̍ya dardaḥ | ba̱rhir na yat su̱dāse̱ vṛthā̱ varg a̱ṁho rā̍ja̱n vari̍vaḥ pū̱rave̍ kaḥ ||

**1.63.8**

tvaṁ tyāṁ na̍ indra deva ci̱trām iṣa̱m āpo̱ na pī̍paya̱ḥ pari̍jman | yayā̍ śūra̱ praty a̱smabhya̱ṁ yaṁsi̱ tmana̱m ūrja̱ṁ na vi̱śvadha̱ kṣara̍dhyai ||

**1.63.9**

akā̍ri ta indra̱ gota̍mebhi̱r brahmā̱ṇy oktā̱ nama̍sā̱ hari̍bhyām | su̱peśa̍sa̱ṁ vāja̱m ā bha̍rā naḥ prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||

**1.64.1**

vṛṣṇe̱ śardhā̍ya̱ suma̍khāya ve̱dhase̱ nodha̍ḥ suvṛ̱ktim pra bha̍rā ma̱rudbhya̍ḥ | a̱po na dhīro̱ mana̍sā su̱hastyo̱ gira̱ḥ sam a̍ñje vi̱dathe̍ṣv ā̱bhuva̍ḥ ||

**1.64.2**

te ja̍jñire di̱va ṛ̱ṣvāsa̍ u̱kṣaṇo̍ ru̱drasya̱ maryā̱ asu̍rā are̱pasa̍ḥ | pā̱va̱kāsa̱ḥ śuca̍ya̱ḥ sūryā̍ iva̱ satvā̍no̱ na dra̱psino̍ gho̱rava̍rpasaḥ ||

**1.64.3**

yuvā̍no ru̱drā a̱jarā̍ abho̱gghano̍ vava̱kṣur adhri̍gāva̱ḥ parva̍tā iva | dṛ̱ḻhā ci̱d viśvā̱ bhuva̍nāni̱ pārthi̍vā̱ pra cyā̍vayanti di̱vyāni̍ ma̱jmanā̍ ||

**1.64.4**

ci̱trair a̱ñjibhi̱r vapu̍ṣe̱ vy a̍ñjate̱ vakṣa̍ḥsu ru̱kmām̐ adhi̍ yetire śu̱bhe | aṁse̍ṣv eṣā̱ṁ ni mi̍mṛkṣur ṛ̱ṣṭaya̍ḥ sā̱kaṁ ja̍jñire sva̱dhayā̍ di̱vo nara̍ḥ ||

**1.64.5**

ī̱śā̱na̱kṛto̱ dhuna̍yo ri̱śāda̍so̱ vātā̍n vi̱dyuta̱s tavi̍ṣībhir akrata | du̱hanty ūdha̍r di̱vyāni̱ dhūta̍yo̱ bhūmi̍m pinvanti̱ paya̍sā̱ pari̍jrayaḥ ||

**1.64.6**

pinva̍nty a̱po ma̱ruta̍ḥ su̱dāna̍va̱ḥ payo̍ ghṛ̱tava̍d vi̱dathe̍ṣv ā̱bhuva̍ḥ | atya̱ṁ na mi̱he vi na̍yanti vā̱jina̱m utsa̍ṁ duhanti sta̱naya̍nta̱m akṣi̍tam ||

**1.64.7**

ma̱hi̱ṣāso̍ mā̱yina̍ś ci̱trabhā̍navo gi̱rayo̱ na svata̍vaso raghu̱ṣyada̍ḥ | mṛ̱gā i̍va ha̱stina̍ḥ khādathā̱ vanā̱ yad āru̍ṇīṣu̱ tavi̍ṣī̱r ayu̍gdhvam ||

**1.64.8**

si̱ṁhā i̍va nānadati̱ prace̍tasaḥ pi̱śā i̍va su̱piśo̍ vi̱śvave̍dasaḥ | kṣapo̱ jinva̍nta̱ḥ pṛṣa̍tībhir ṛ̱ṣṭibhi̱ḥ sam it sa̱bādha̱ḥ śava̱sāhi̍manyavaḥ ||

**1.64.9**

roda̍sī̱ ā va̍datā gaṇaśriyo̱ nṛṣā̍caḥ śūrā̱ḥ śava̱sāhi̍manyavaḥ | ā va̱ndhure̍ṣv a̱mati̱r na da̍rśa̱tā vi̱dyun na ta̍sthau maruto̱ rathe̍ṣu vaḥ ||

**1.64.10**

vi̱śvave̍daso ra̱yibhi̱ḥ samo̍kasa̱ḥ sammi̍ślāsa̱s tavi̍ṣībhir vira̱pśina̍ḥ | astā̍ra̱ iṣu̍ṁ dadhire̱ gabha̍styor ana̱ntaśu̍ṣmā̱ vṛṣa̍khādayo̱ nara̍ḥ ||

**1.64.11**

hi̱ra̱ṇyaye̍bhiḥ pa̱vibhi̍ḥ payo̱vṛdha̱ ujji̍ghnanta āpa̱thyo̱3̱̍ na parva̍tān | ma̱khā a̱yāsa̍ḥ sva̱sṛto̍ dhruva̱cyuto̍ dudhra̱kṛto̍ ma̱ruto̱ bhrāja̍dṛṣṭayaḥ ||

**1.64.12**

ghṛṣu̍m pāva̱kaṁ va̱nina̱ṁ vica̍rṣaṇiṁ ru̱drasya̍ sū̱nuṁ ha̱vasā̍ gṛṇīmasi | ra̱ja̱stura̍ṁ ta̱vasa̱m māru̍taṁ ga̱ṇam ṛ̍jī̱ṣiṇa̱ṁ vṛṣa̍ṇaṁ saścata śri̱ye ||

**1.64.13**

pra nū sa marta̱ḥ śava̍sā̱ janā̱m̐ ati̍ ta̱sthau va̍ ū̱tī ma̍ruto̱ yam āva̍ta | arva̍dbhi̱r vāja̍m bharate̱ dhanā̱ nṛbhi̍r ā̱pṛcchya̱ṁ kratu̱m ā kṣe̍ti̱ puṣya̍ti ||

**1.64.14**

ca̱rkṛtya̍m marutaḥ pṛ̱tsu du̱ṣṭara̍ṁ dyu̱manta̱ṁ śuṣma̍m ma̱ghava̍tsu dhattana | dha̱na̱spṛta̱m u̱kthya̍ṁ vi̱śvaca̍rṣaṇiṁ to̱kam pu̍ṣyema̱ tana̍yaṁ śa̱taṁ himā̍ḥ ||

**1.64.15**

nū ṣṭhi̱ram ma̍ruto vī̱rava̍ntam ṛtī̱ṣāha̍ṁ ra̱yim a̱smāsu̍ dhatta | sa̱ha̱sriṇa̍ṁ śa̱tina̍ṁ śūśu̱vāṁsa̍m prā̱tar ma̱kṣū dhi̱yāva̍sur jagamyāt ||

**1.65.1**

pa̱śvā na tā̱yuṁ guhā̱ cata̍nta̱ṁ namo̍ yujā̱naṁ namo̱ vaha̍ntam ||

**1.65.2**

sa̱joṣā̱ dhīrā̍ḥ pa̱dair anu̍ gma̱nn upa̍ tvā sīda̱n viśve̱ yaja̍trāḥ ||

**1.65.3**

ṛ̱tasya̍ de̱vā anu̍ vra̱tā gu̱r bhuva̱t pari̍ṣṭi̱r dyaur na bhūma̍ ||

**1.65.4**

vardha̍ntī̱m āpa̍ḥ pa̱nvā suśi̍śvim ṛ̱tasya̱ yonā̱ garbhe̱ sujā̍tam ||

**1.65.5**

pu̱ṣṭir na ra̱ṇvā kṣi̱tir na pṛ̱thvī gi̱rir na bhujma̱ kṣodo̱ na śa̱mbhu ||

**1.65.6**

atyo̱ nājma̱n sarga̍pratakta̱ḥ sindhu̱r na kṣoda̱ḥ ka ī̍ṁ varāte ||

**1.65.7**

jā̱miḥ sindhū̍nā̱m bhrāte̍va̱ svasrā̱m ibhyā̱n na rājā̱ vanā̍ny atti ||

**1.65.8**

yad vāta̍jūto̱ vanā̱ vy asthā̍d a̱gnir ha̍ dāti̱ romā̍ pṛthi̱vyāḥ ||

**1.65.9**

śvasi̍ty a̱psu ha̱ṁso na sīda̱n kratvā̱ ceti̍ṣṭho vi̱śām u̍ṣa̱rbhut ||

**1.65.10**

somo̱ na ve̱dhā ṛ̱tapra̍jātaḥ pa̱śur na śiśvā̍ vi̱bhur dū̱rebhā̍ḥ ||

**1.66.1**

ra̱yir na ci̱trā sūro̱ na sa̱ṁdṛg āyu̱r na prā̱ṇo nityo̱ na sū̱nuḥ ||

**1.66.2**

takvā̱ na bhūrṇi̱r vanā̍ siṣakti̱ payo̱ na dhe̱nuḥ śuci̍r vi̱bhāvā̍ ||

**1.66.3**

dā̱dhāra̱ kṣema̱m oko̱ na ra̱ṇvo yavo̱ na pa̱kvo jetā̱ janā̍nām ||

**1.66.4**

ṛṣi̱r na stubhvā̍ vi̱kṣu pra̍śa̱sto vā̱jī na prī̱to vayo̍ dadhāti ||

**1.66.5**

du̱roka̍śoci̱ḥ kratu̱r na nityo̍ jā̱yeva̱ yonā̱v ara̱ṁ viśva̍smai ||

**1.66.6**

ci̱tro yad abhrā̍ṭ chve̱to na vi̱kṣu ratho̱ na ru̱kmī tve̱ṣaḥ sa̱matsu̍ ||

**1.66.7**

sene̍va sṛ̱ṣṭāma̍ṁ dadhā̱ty astu̱r na di̱dyut tve̱ṣapra̍tīkā ||

**1.66.8**

ya̱mo ha̍ jā̱to ya̱mo jani̍tvaṁ jā̱raḥ ka̱nīnā̱m pati̱r janī̍nām ||

**1.66.9**

taṁ va̍ś ca̱rāthā̍ va̱yaṁ va̍sa̱tyāsta̱ṁ na gāvo̱ nakṣa̍nta i̱ddham ||

**1.66.10**

sindhu̱r na kṣoda̱ḥ pra nīcī̍r aino̱n nava̍nta̱ gāva̱ḥ sva1̱̍r dṛśī̍ke ||

**1.67.1**

vane̍ṣu jā̱yur marte̍ṣu mi̱tro vṛ̍ṇī̱te śru̱ṣṭiṁ rāje̍vāju̱ryam ||

**1.67.2**

kṣemo̱ na sā̱dhuḥ kratu̱r na bha̱dro bhuva̍t svā̱dhīr hotā̍ havya̱vāṭ ||

**1.67.3**

haste̱ dadhā̍no nṛ̱mṇā viśvā̱ny ame̍ de̱vān dhā̱d guhā̍ ni̱ṣīda̍n ||

**1.67.4**

vi̱dantī̱m atra̱ naro̍ dhiya̱ṁdhā hṛ̱dā yat ta̱ṣṭān mantrā̱m̐ aśa̍ṁsan ||

**1.67.5**

a̱jo na kṣāṁ dā̱dhāra̍ pṛthi̱vīṁ ta̱stambha̱ dyām mantre̍bhiḥ sa̱tyaiḥ ||

**1.67.6**

pri̱yā pa̱dāni̍ pa̱śvo ni pā̍hi vi̱śvāyu̍r agne gu̱hā guha̍ṁ gāḥ ||

**1.67.7**

ya ī̍ṁ ci̱keta̱ guhā̱ bhava̍nta̱m ā yaḥ sa̱sāda̱ dhārā̍m ṛ̱tasya̍ ||

**1.67.8**

vi ye cṛ̱tanty ṛ̱tā sapa̍nta̱ ād id vasū̍ni̱ pra va̍vācāsmai ||

**1.67.9**

vi yo vī̱rutsu̱ rodha̍n mahi̱tvota pra̱jā u̱ta pra̱sūṣv a̱ntaḥ ||

**1.67.10**

citti̍r a̱pāṁ dame̍ vi̱śvāyu̱ḥ sadme̍va̱ dhīrā̍ḥ sa̱mmāya̍ cakruḥ ||

**1.68.1**

śrī̱ṇann upa̍ sthā̱d diva̍m bhura̱ṇyuḥ sthā̱tuś ca̱ratha̍m a̱ktūn vy ū̍rṇot ||

**1.68.2**

pari̱ yad e̍ṣā̱m eko̱ viśve̍ṣā̱m bhuva̍d de̱vo de̱vānā̍m mahi̱tvā ||

**1.68.3**

ād it te̱ viśve̱ kratu̍ṁ juṣanta̱ śuṣkā̱d yad de̍va jī̱vo jani̍ṣṭhāḥ ||

**1.68.4**

bhaja̍nta̱ viśve̍ deva̱tvaṁ nāma̍ ṛ̱taṁ sapa̍nto a̱mṛta̱m evai̍ḥ ||

**1.68.5**

ṛ̱tasya̱ preṣā̍ ṛ̱tasya̍ dhī̱tir vi̱śvāyu̱r viśve̱ apā̍ṁsi cakruḥ ||

**1.68.6**

yas tubhya̱ṁ dāśā̱d yo vā̍ te̱ śikṣā̱t tasmai̍ ciki̱tvān ra̱yiṁ da̍yasva ||

**1.68.7**

hotā̱ niṣa̍tto̱ mano̱r apa̍tye̱ sa ci̱n nv ā̍sā̱m patī̍ rayī̱ṇām ||

**1.68.8**

i̱cchanta̱ reto̍ mi̱thas ta̱nūṣu̱ saṁ jā̍nata̱ svair dakṣai̱r amū̍rāḥ ||

**1.68.9**

pi̱tur na pu̱trāḥ kratu̍ṁ juṣanta̱ śroṣa̱n ye a̍sya̱ śāsa̍ṁ tu̱rāsa̍ḥ ||

**1.68.10**

vi rāya̍ aurṇo̱d dura̍ḥ puru̱kṣuḥ pi̱peśa̱ nāka̱ṁ stṛbhi̱r damū̍nāḥ ||

**1.69.1**

śu̱kraḥ śu̍śu̱kvām̐ u̱ṣo na jā̱raḥ pa̱prā sa̍mī̱cī di̱vo na jyoti̍ḥ ||

**1.69.2**

pari̱ prajā̍ta̱ḥ kratvā̍ babhūtha̱ bhuvo̍ de̱vānā̍m pi̱tā pu̱traḥ san ||

**1.69.3**

ve̱dhā adṛ̍pto a̱gnir vi̍jā̱nann ūdha̱r na gonā̱ṁ svādmā̍ pitū̱nām ||

**1.69.4**

jane̱ na śeva̍ ā̱hūrya̱ḥ san madhye̱ niṣa̍tto ra̱ṇvo du̍ro̱ṇe ||

**1.69.5**

pu̱tro na jā̱to ra̱ṇvo du̍ro̱ṇe vā̱jī na prī̱to viśo̱ vi tā̍rīt ||

**1.69.6**

viśo̱ yad ahve̱ nṛbhi̱ḥ sanī̍ḻā a̱gnir de̍va̱tvā viśvā̍ny aśyāḥ ||

**1.69.7**

naki̍ṣ ṭa e̱tā vra̱tā mi̍nanti̱ nṛbhyo̱ yad e̱bhyaḥ śru̱ṣṭiṁ ca̱kartha̍ ||

**1.69.8**

tat tu te̱ daṁso̱ yad aha̍n samā̱nair nṛbhi̱r yad yu̱kto vi̱ve rapā̍ṁsi ||

**1.69.9**

u̱ṣo na jā̱ro vi̱bhāvo̱sraḥ saṁjñā̍tarūpa̱ś cike̍tad asmai ||

**1.69.10**

tmanā̱ vaha̍nto̱ duro̱ vy ṛ̍ṇva̱n nava̍nta̱ viśve̱ sva1̱̍r dṛśī̍ke ||

**1.70.1**

va̱nema̍ pū̱rvīr a̱ryo ma̍nī̱ṣā a̱gniḥ su̱śoko̱ viśvā̍ny aśyāḥ ||

**1.70.2**

ā daivyā̍ni vra̱tā ci̍ki̱tvān ā mānu̍ṣasya̱ jana̍sya̱ janma̍ ||

**1.70.3**

garbho̱ yo a̱pāṁ garbho̱ vanā̍nā̱ṁ garbha̍ś ca sthā̱tāṁ garbha̍ś ca̱rathā̍m ||

**1.70.4**

adrau̍ cid asmā a̱ntar du̍ro̱ṇe vi̱śāṁ na viśvo̍ a̱mṛta̍ḥ svā̱dhīḥ ||

**1.70.5**

sa hi kṣa̱pāvā̍m̐ a̱gnī ra̍yī̱ṇāṁ dāśa̱d yo a̍smā̱ ara̍ṁ sū̱ktaiḥ ||

**1.70.6**

e̱tā ci̍kitvo̱ bhūmā̱ ni pā̍hi de̱vānā̱ṁ janma̱ martā̍m̐ś ca vi̱dvān ||

**1.70.7**

vardhā̱n yam pū̱rvīḥ kṣa̱po virū̍pāḥ sthā̱tuś ca̱ ratha̍m ṛ̱tapra̍vītam ||

**1.70.8**

arā̍dhi̱ hotā̱ sva1̱̍r niṣa̍ttaḥ kṛ̱ṇvan viśvā̱ny apā̍ṁsi sa̱tyā ||

**1.70.9**

goṣu̱ praśa̍sti̱ṁ vane̍ṣu dhiṣe̱ bhara̍nta̱ viśve̍ ba̱liṁ sva̍r ṇaḥ ||

**1.70.10**

vi tvā̱ nara̍ḥ puru̱trā sa̍paryan pi̱tur na jivre̱r vi vedo̍ bharanta ||

**1.70.11**

sā̱dhur na gṛ̱dhnur aste̍va̱ śūro̱ yāte̍va bhī̱mas tve̱ṣaḥ sa̱matsu̍ ||

**1.71.1**

upa̱ pra ji̍nvann uśa̱tīr u̱śanta̱m pati̱ṁ na nitya̱ṁ jana̍ya̱ḥ sanī̍ḻāḥ | svasā̍ra̱ḥ śyāvī̱m aru̍ṣīm ajuṣrañ ci̱tram u̱cchantī̍m u̱ṣasa̱ṁ na gāva̍ḥ ||

**1.71.2**

vī̱ḻu ci̍d dṛ̱ḻhā pi̱taro̍ na u̱kthair adri̍ṁ ruja̱nn aṅgi̍raso̱ rave̍ṇa | ca̱krur di̱vo bṛ̍ha̱to gā̱tum a̱sme aha̱ḥ sva̍r vividuḥ ke̱tum u̱srāḥ ||

**1.71.3**

dadha̍nn ṛ̱taṁ dha̱naya̍nn asya dhī̱tim ād id a̱ryo di̍dhi̱ṣvo̱3̱̍ vibhṛ̍trāḥ | atṛ̍ṣyantīr a̱paso̍ ya̱nty acchā̍ de̱vāñ janma̱ pra̱yasā va̱rdhaya̍ntīḥ ||

**1.71.4**

mathī̱d yad ī̱ṁ vibhṛ̍to māta̱riśvā̍ gṛ̱he-gṛ̍he śye̱to jenyo̱ bhūt | ād ī̱ṁ rājñe̱ na sahī̍yase̱ sacā̱ sann ā dū̱tya1̱̍m bhṛga̍vāṇo vivāya ||

**1.71.5**

ma̱he yat pi̱tra ī̱ṁ rasa̍ṁ di̱ve kar ava̍ tsarat pṛśa̱nya̍ś ciki̱tvān | sṛ̱jad astā̍ dhṛṣa̱tā di̱dyum a̍smai̱ svāyā̍ṁ de̱vo du̍hi̱tari̱ tviṣi̍ṁ dhāt ||

**1.71.6**

sva ā yas tubhya̱ṁ dama̱ ā vi̱bhāti̱ namo̍ vā̱ dāśā̍d uśa̱to anu̱ dyūn | vardho̍ agne̱ vayo̍ asya dvi̱barhā̱ yāsa̍d rā̱yā sa̱ratha̱ṁ yaṁ ju̱nāsi̍ ||

**1.71.7**

a̱gniṁ viśvā̍ a̱bhi pṛkṣa̍ḥ sacante samu̱draṁ na sra̱vata̍ḥ sa̱pta ya̱hvīḥ ||  na jā̱mibhi̱r vi ci̍kite̱ vayo̍ no vi̱dā de̱veṣu̱ prama̍tiṁ ciki̱tvān ||

**1.71.8**

ā yad i̱ṣe nṛ̱pati̱ṁ teja̱ āna̱ṭ chuci̱ reto̱ niṣi̍kta̱ṁ dyaur a̱bhīke̍ | a̱gniḥ śardha̍m anava̱dyaṁ yuvā̍naṁ svā̱dhya̍ṁ janayat sū̱daya̍c ca ||

**1.71.9**

mano̱ na yo 'dhva̍naḥ sa̱dya ety eka̍ḥ sa̱trā sūro̱ vasva̍ īśe | rājā̍nā mi̱trāvaru̍ṇā supā̱ṇī goṣu̍ pri̱yam a̱mṛta̱ṁ rakṣa̍māṇā ||

**1.71.10**

mā no̍ agne sa̱khyā pitryā̍ṇi̱ pra ma̍rṣiṣṭhā a̱bhi vi̱duṣ ka̱viḥ san | nabho̱ na rū̱paṁ ja̍ri̱mā mi̍nāti pu̱rā tasyā̍ a̱bhiśa̍ste̱r adhī̍hi ||

**1.72.1**

ni kāvyā̍ ve̱dhasa̱ḥ śaśva̍tas ka̱r haste̱ dadhā̍no̱ naryā̍ pu̱rūṇi̍ | a̱gnir bhu̍vad rayi̱patī̍ rayī̱ṇāṁ sa̱trā ca̍krā̱ṇo a̱mṛtā̍ni̱ viśvā̍ ||

**1.72.2**

a̱sme va̱tsam pari̱ ṣanta̱ṁ na vi̍ndann i̱cchanto̱ viśve̍ a̱mṛtā̱ amū̍rāḥ | śra̱ma̱yuva̍ḥ pada̱vyo̍ dhiya̱ṁdhās ta̱sthuḥ pa̱de pa̍ra̱me cārv a̱gneḥ ||

**1.72.3**

ti̱sro yad a̍gne śa̱rada̱s tvām ic chuci̍ṁ ghṛ̱tena̱ śuca̍yaḥ sapa̱ryān | nāmā̍ni cid dadhire ya̱jñiyā̱ny asū̍dayanta ta̱nva1̱̍ḥ sujā̍tāḥ ||

**1.72.4**

ā roda̍sī bṛha̱tī vevi̍dānā̱ḥ pra ru̱driyā̍ jabhrire ya̱jñiyā̍saḥ | vi̱dan marto̍ ne̱madhi̍tā ciki̱tvān a̱gnim pa̱de pa̍ra̱me ta̍sthi̱vāṁsa̍m ||

**1.72.5**

sa̱ṁjā̱nā̱nā upa̍ sīdann abhi̱jñu patnī̍vanto nama̱sya̍ṁ namasyan | ri̱ri̱kvāṁsa̍s ta̱nva̍ḥ kṛṇvata̱ svāḥ sakhā̱ sakhyu̍r ni̱miṣi̱ rakṣa̍māṇāḥ ||

**1.72.6**

triḥ sa̱pta yad guhyā̍ni̱ tve it pa̱dāvi̍da̱n nihi̍tā ya̱jñiyā̍saḥ | tebhī̍ rakṣante a̱mṛta̍ṁ sa̱joṣā̍ḥ pa̱śūñ ca̍ sthā̱tṝñ ca̱ratha̍ṁ ca pāhi ||

**1.72.7**

vi̱dvām̐ a̍gne va̱yunā̍ni kṣitī̱nāṁ vy ā̍nu̱ṣak chu̱rudho̍ jī̱vase̍ dhāḥ | a̱nta̱rvi̱dvām̐ adhva̍no deva̱yānā̱n ata̍ndro dū̱to a̍bhavo havi̱rvāṭ ||

**1.72.8**

svā̱dhyo̍ di̱va ā sa̱pta ya̱hvī rā̱yo duro̱ vy ṛ̍ta̱jñā a̍jānan | vi̱dad gavya̍ṁ sa̱ramā̍ dṛ̱ḻham ū̱rvaṁ yenā̱ nu ka̱m mānu̍ṣī̱ bhoja̍te̱ viṭ ||

**1.72.9**

ā ye viśvā̍ svapa̱tyāni̍ ta̱sthuḥ kṛ̍ṇvā̱nāso̍ amṛta̱tvāya̍ gā̱tum | ma̱hnā ma̱hadbhi̍ḥ pṛthi̱vī vi ta̍sthe mā̱tā pu̱trair adi̍ti̱r dhāya̍se̱ veḥ ||

**1.72.10**

adhi̱ śriya̱ṁ ni da̍dhu̱ś cāru̍m asmin di̱vo yad a̱kṣī a̱mṛtā̱ akṛ̍ṇvan | adha̍ kṣaranti̱ sindha̍vo̱ na sṛ̱ṣṭāḥ pra nīcī̍r agne̱ aru̍ṣīr ajānan ||

**1.73.1**

ra̱yir na yaḥ pi̍tṛvi̱tto va̍yo̱dhāḥ su̱praṇī̍tiś ciki̱tuṣo̱ na śāsu̍ḥ | syo̱na̱śīr ati̍thi̱r na prī̍ṇā̱no hote̍va̱ sadma̍ vidha̱to vi tā̍rīt ||

**1.73.2**

de̱vo na yaḥ sa̍vi̱tā sa̱tyama̍nmā̱ kratvā̍ ni̱pāti̍ vṛ̱janā̍ni̱ viśvā̍ | pu̱ru̱pra̱śa̱sto a̱mati̱r na sa̱tya ā̱tmeva̱ śevo̍ didhi̱ṣāyyo̍ bhūt ||

**1.73.3**

de̱vo na yaḥ pṛ̍thi̱vīṁ vi̱śvadhā̍yā upa̱kṣeti̍ hi̱tami̍tro̱ na rājā̍ | pu̱ra̱ḥsada̍ḥ śarma̱sado̱ na vī̱rā a̍nava̱dyā pati̍juṣṭeva̱ nārī̍ ||

**1.73.4**

taṁ tvā̱ naro̱ dama̱ ā nitya̍m i̱ddham agne̱ saca̍nta kṣi̱tiṣu̍ dhru̱vāsu̍ | adhi̍ dyu̱mnaṁ ni da̍dhu̱r bhūry a̍smi̱n bhavā̍ vi̱śvāyu̍r dha̱ruṇo̍ rayī̱ṇām ||

**1.73.5**

vi pṛkṣo̍ agne ma̱ghavā̍no aśyu̱r vi sū̱rayo̱ dada̍to̱ viśva̱m āyu̍ḥ | sa̱nema̱ vāja̍ṁ sami̱theṣv a̱ryo bhā̱gaṁ de̱veṣu̱ śrave̍se̱ dadhā̍nāḥ ||

**1.73.6**

ṛ̱tasya̱ hi dhe̍navo vāvaśā̱nā smadū̍dhnīḥ pī̱paya̍nta̱ dyubha̍ktāḥ | pa̱rā̱vata̍ḥ suma̱tim bhikṣa̍māṇā̱ vi sindha̍vaḥ sa̱mayā̍ sasru̱r adri̍m ||

**1.73.7**

tve a̍gne suma̱tim bhikṣa̍māṇā di̱vi śravo̍ dadhire ya̱jñiyā̍saḥ | naktā̍ ca ca̱krur u̱ṣasā̱ virū̍pe kṛ̱ṣṇaṁ ca̱ varṇa̍m aru̱ṇaṁ ca̱ saṁ dhu̍ḥ ||

**1.73.8**

yān rā̱ye martā̱n suṣū̍do agne̱ te syā̍ma ma̱ghavā̍no va̱yaṁ ca̍ | chā̱yeva̱ viśva̱m bhuva̍naṁ sisakṣy āpapri̱vān roda̍sī a̱ntari̍kṣam ||

**1.73.9**

arva̍dbhir agne̱ arva̍to̱ nṛbhi̱r nṝn vī̱rair vī̱rān va̍nuyāmā̱ tvotā̍ḥ | ī̱śā̱nāsa̍ḥ pitṛvi̱ttasya̍ rā̱yo vi sū̱raya̍ḥ śa̱tahi̍mā no aśyuḥ ||

**1.73.10**

e̱tā te̍ agna u̱cathā̍ni vedho̱ juṣṭā̍ni santu̱ mana̍se hṛ̱de ca̍ | śa̱kema̍ rā̱yaḥ su̱dhuro̱ yama̱ṁ te 'dhi̱ śravo̍ de̱vabha̍kta̱ṁ dadhā̍nāḥ ||

**1.74.1**

u̱pa̱pra̱yanto̍ adhva̱ram mantra̱ṁ vocemā̱gnaye̍ | ā̱re a̱sme ca̍ śṛṇva̱te ||

**1.74.2**

yaḥ snīhi̍tīṣu pū̱rvyaḥ sa̍ṁjagmā̱nāsu̍ kṛ̱ṣṭiṣu̍ | ara̍kṣad dā̱śuṣe̱ gaya̍m ||

**1.74.3**

u̱ta bru̍vantu ja̱ntava̱ ud a̱gnir vṛ̍tra̱hāja̍ni | dha̱na̱ṁja̱yo raṇe̍-raṇe ||

**1.74.4**

yasya̍ dū̱to asi̱ kṣaye̱ veṣi̍ ha̱vyāni̍ vī̱taye̍ | da̱smat kṛ̱ṇoṣy a̍dhva̱ram ||

**1.74.5**

tam it su̍ha̱vyam a̍ṅgiraḥ sude̱vaṁ sa̍haso yaho | janā̍ āhuḥ suba̱rhiṣa̍m ||

**1.74.6**

ā ca̱ vahā̍si̱ tām̐ i̱ha de̱vām̐ upa̱ praśa̍staye | ha̱vyā su̍ścandra vī̱taye̍ ||

**1.74.7**

na yor u̍pa̱bdir aśvya̍ḥ śṛ̱ṇve ratha̍sya̱ kac ca̱na | yad a̍gne̱ yāsi̍ dū̱tya̍m ||

**1.74.8**

tvoto̍ vā̱jy ahra̍yo̱ 'bhi pūrva̍smā̱d apa̍raḥ | pra dā̱śvām̐ a̍gne asthāt ||

**1.74.9**

u̱ta dyu̱mat su̱vīrya̍m bṛ̱had a̍gne vivāsasi | de̱vebhyo̍ deva dā̱śuṣe̍ ||

**1.75.1**

ju̱ṣasva̍ sa̱pratha̍stama̱ṁ vaco̍ de̱vapsa̍rastamam | ha̱vyā juhvā̍na ā̱sani̍ ||

**1.75.2**

athā̍ te aṅgirasta̱māgne̍ vedhastama pri̱yam | vo̱cema̱ brahma̍ sāna̱si ||

**1.75.3**

kas te̍ jā̱mir janā̍nā̱m agne̱ ko dā̱śva̍dhvaraḥ | ko ha̱ kasmi̍nn asi śri̱taḥ ||

**1.75.4**

tvaṁ jā̱mir janā̍nā̱m agne̍ mi̱tro a̍si pri̱yaḥ | sakhā̱ sakhi̍bhya̱ īḍya̍ḥ ||

**1.75.5**

yajā̍ no mi̱trāvaru̍ṇā̱ yajā̍ de̱vām̐ ṛ̱tam bṛ̱hat | agne̱ yakṣi̱ svaṁ dama̍m ||

**1.76.1**

kā ta̱ upe̍ti̱r mana̍so̱ varā̍ya̱ bhuva̍d agne̱ śaṁta̍mā̱ kā ma̍nī̱ṣā | ko vā̍ ya̱jñaiḥ pari̱ dakṣa̍ṁ ta āpa̱ kena̍ vā te̱ mana̍sā dāśema ||

**1.76.2**

ehy a̍gna i̱ha hotā̱ ni ṣī̱dāda̍bdha̱ḥ su pu̍rae̱tā bha̍vā naḥ | ava̍tāṁ tvā̱ roda̍sī viśvami̱nve yajā̍ ma̱he sau̍mana̱sāya̍ de̱vān ||

**1.76.3**

pra su viśvā̍n ra̱kṣaso̱ dhakṣy a̍gne̱ bhavā̍ ya̱jñānā̍m abhiśasti̱pāvā̍ | athā va̍ha̱ soma̍pati̱ṁ hari̍bhyām āti̱thyam a̍smai cakṛmā su̱dāvne̍ ||

**1.76.4**

pra̱jāva̍tā̱ vaca̍sā̱ vahni̍r ā̱sā ca̍ hu̱ve ni ca̍ satsī̱ha de̱vaiḥ | veṣi̍ ho̱tram u̱ta po̱traṁ ya̍jatra bo̱dhi pra̍yantar janita̱r vasū̍nām ||

**1.76.5**

yathā̱ vipra̍sya̱ manu̍ṣo ha̱virbhi̍r de̱vām̐ aya̍jaḥ ka̱vibhi̍ḥ ka̱viḥ san | e̱vā ho̍taḥ satyatara̱ tvam a̱dyāgne̍ ma̱ndrayā̍ ju̱hvā̍ yajasva ||

**1.77.1**

ka̱thā dā̍śemā̱gnaye̱ kāsmai̍ de̱vaju̍ṣṭocyate bhā̱mine̱ gīḥ | yo martye̍ṣv a̱mṛta̍ ṛ̱tāvā̱ hotā̱ yaji̍ṣṭha̱ it kṛ̱ṇoti̍ de̱vān ||

**1.77.2**

yo a̍dhva̱reṣu̱ śaṁta̍ma ṛ̱tāvā̱ hotā̱ tam ū̱ namo̍bhi̱r ā kṛ̍ṇudhvam | a̱gnir yad ver martā̍ya de̱vān sa cā̱ bodhā̍ti̱ mana̍sā yajāti ||

**1.77.3**

sa hi kratu̱ḥ sa marya̱ḥ sa sā̱dhur mi̱tro na bhū̱d adbhu̍tasya ra̱thīḥ | tam medhe̍ṣu pratha̱maṁ de̍va̱yantī̱r viśa̱ upa̍ bruvate da̱smam ārī̍ḥ ||

**1.77.4**

sa no̍ nṛ̱ṇāṁ nṛta̍mo ri̱śādā̍ a̱gnir giro 'va̍sā vetu dhī̱tim | tanā̍ ca̱ ye ma̱ghavā̍na̱ḥ śavi̍ṣṭhā̱ vāja̍prasūtā i̱ṣaya̍nta̱ manma̍ ||

**1.77.5**

e̱vāgnir gota̍mebhir ṛ̱tāvā̱ vipre̍bhir astoṣṭa jā̱tave̍dāḥ | sa e̍ṣu dyu̱mnam pī̍paya̱t sa vāja̱ṁ sa pu̱ṣṭiṁ yā̍ti̱ joṣa̱m ā ci̍ki̱tvān ||

**1.78.1**

a̱bhi tvā̱ gota̍mā gi̱rā jāta̍vedo̱ vica̍rṣaṇe | dyu̱mnair a̱bhi pra ṇo̍numaḥ ||

**1.78.2**

tam u̍ tvā̱ gota̍mo gi̱rā rā̱yaskā̍mo duvasyati | dyu̱mnair a̱bhi pra ṇo̍numaḥ ||

**1.78.3**

tam u̍ tvā vāja̱sāta̍mam aṅgira̱svad dha̍vāmahe | dyu̱mnair a̱bhi pra ṇo̍numaḥ ||

**1.78.4**

tam u̍ tvā vṛtra̱hanta̍ma̱ṁ yo dasyū̍m̐r avadhūnu̱ṣe | dyu̱mnair a̱bhi pra ṇo̍numaḥ ||

**1.78.5**

avo̍cāma̱ rahū̍gaṇā a̱gnaye̱ madhu̍ma̱d vaca̍ḥ | dyu̱mnair a̱bhi pra ṇo̍numaḥ ||

**1.79.1**

hira̍ṇyakeśo̱ raja̍so visā̱re 'hi̱r dhuni̱r vāta̍ iva̱ dhrajī̍mān | śuci̍bhrājā u̱ṣaso̱ nave̍dā̱ yaśa̍svatīr apa̱syuvo̱ na sa̱tyāḥ ||

**1.79.2**

ā te̍ supa̱rṇā a̍minanta̱m̐ evai̍ḥ kṛ̱ṣṇo no̍nāva vṛṣa̱bho yadī̱dam | śi̱vābhi̱r na smaya̍mānābhi̱r āgā̱t pata̍nti̱ miha̍ḥ sta̱naya̍nty a̱bhrā ||

**1.79.3**

yad ī̍m ṛ̱tasya̱ paya̍sā̱ piyā̍no̱ naya̍nn ṛ̱tasya̍ pa̱thibhī̱ raji̍ṣṭhaiḥ | a̱rya̱mā mi̱tro varu̍ṇa̱ḥ pari̍jmā̱ tvaca̍m pṛñca̱nty upa̍rasya̱ yonau̍ ||

**1.79.4**

agne̱ vāja̍sya̱ goma̍ta̱ īśā̍naḥ sahaso yaho | a̱sme dhe̍hi jātavedo̱ mahi̱ śrava̍ḥ ||

**1.79.5**

sa i̍dhā̱no vasu̍ṣ ka̱vir a̱gnir ī̱ḻenyo̍ gi̱rā | re̱vad a̱smabhya̍m purvaṇīka dīdihi ||

**1.79.6**

kṣa̱po rā̍jann u̱ta tmanāgne̱ vasto̍r u̱toṣasa̍ḥ | sa ti̍gmajambha ra̱kṣaso̍ daha̱ prati̍ ||

**1.79.7**

avā̍ no agna ū̱tibhi̍r gāya̱trasya̱ prabha̍rmaṇi | viśvā̍su dhī̱ṣu va̍ndya ||

**1.79.8**

ā no̍ agne ra̱yim bha̍ra satrā̱sāha̱ṁ vare̍ṇyam | viśvā̍su pṛ̱tsu du̱ṣṭara̍m ||

**1.79.9**

ā no̍ agne suce̱tunā̍ ra̱yiṁ vi̱śvāyu̍poṣasam | mā̱rḍī̱kaṁ dhe̍hi jī̱vase̍ ||

**1.79.10**

pra pū̱tās ti̱gmaśo̍ciṣe̱ vāco̍ gotamā̱gnaye̍ | bhara̍sva sumna̱yur gira̍ḥ ||

**1.79.11**

yo no̍ agne 'bhi̱dāsa̱ty anti̍ dū̱re pa̍dī̱ṣṭa saḥ | a̱smāka̱m id vṛ̱dhe bha̍va ||

**1.79.12**

sa̱ha̱srā̱kṣo vica̍rṣaṇir a̱gnī rakṣā̍ṁsi sedhati | hotā̍ gṛṇīta u̱kthya̍ḥ ||

**1.80.1**

i̱tthā hi soma̱ in made̍ bra̱hmā ca̱kāra̱ vardha̍nam | śavi̍ṣṭha vajri̱nn oja̍sā pṛthi̱vyā niḥ śa̍śā̱ ahi̱m arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.2**

sa tvā̍mada̱d vṛṣā̱ mada̱ḥ soma̍ḥ śye̱nābhṛ̍taḥ su̱taḥ | yenā̍ vṛ̱traṁ nir a̱dbhyo ja̱ghantha̍ vajri̱nn oja̱sārca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.3**

prehy a̱bhī̍hi dhṛṣṇu̱hi na te̱ vajro̱ ni ya̍ṁsate | indra̍ nṛ̱mṇaṁ hi te̱ śavo̱ hano̍ vṛ̱traṁ jayā̍ a̱po 'rca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.4**

nir i̍ndra̱ bhūmyā̱ adhi̍ vṛ̱traṁ ja̍ghantha̱ nir di̱vaḥ | sṛ̱jā ma̱rutva̍tī̱r ava̍ jī̱vadha̍nyā i̱māḥ a̱po 'rca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.5**

indro̍ vṛ̱trasya̱ dodha̍ta̱ḥ sānu̱ṁ vajre̍ṇa hīḻi̱taḥ | a̱bhi̱kramyāva̍ jighnate̱ 'paḥ sarmā̍ya co̱daya̱nn arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.6**

adhi̱ sānau̱ ni ji̍ghnate̱ vajre̍ṇa śa̱tapa̍rvaṇā | ma̱ndā̱na indro̱ andha̍sa̱ḥ sakhi̍bhyo gā̱tum i̍ccha̱ty arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.7**

indra̱ tubhya̱m id a̍dri̱vo 'nu̍ttaṁ vajrin vī̱rya̍m | yad dha̱ tyam mā̱yina̍m mṛ̱gaṁ tam u̱ tvam mā̱yayā̍vadhī̱r arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.8**

vi te̱ vajrā̍so asthiran nava̱tiṁ nā̱vyā̱3̱̍ anu̍ | ma̱hat ta̍ indra vī̱rya̍m bā̱hvos te̱ bala̍ṁ hi̱tam arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.9**

sa̱hasra̍ṁ sā̱kam a̍rcata̱ pari̍ ṣṭobhata viṁśa̱tiḥ | śa̱taina̱m anv a̍nonavu̱r indrā̍ya̱ brahmodya̍ta̱m arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.10**

indro̍ vṛ̱trasya̱ tavi̍ṣī̱ṁ nir a̍ha̱n saha̍sā̱ saha̍ḥ | ma̱hat tad a̍sya̱ pauṁsya̍ṁ vṛ̱traṁ ja̍gha̱nvām̐ a̍sṛja̱d arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.11**

i̱me ci̱t tava̍ ma̱nyave̱ vepe̍te bhi̱yasā̱ mahī̍ | yad i̍ndra vajri̱nn oja̍sā vṛ̱tram ma̱rutvā̱m̐ ava̍dhī̱r arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.12**

na vepa̍sā̱ na ta̍nya̱tendra̍ṁ vṛ̱tro vi bī̍bhayat | a̱bhy e̍na̱ṁ vajra̍ āya̱saḥ sa̱hasra̍bhṛṣṭir āya̱tārca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.13**

yad vṛ̱traṁ tava̍ cā̱śani̱ṁ vajre̍ṇa sa̱mayo̍dhayaḥ | ahi̍m indra̱ jighā̍ṁsato di̱vi te̍ badbadhe̱ śavo 'rca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.14**

a̱bhi̱ṣṭa̱ne te̍ adrivo̱ yat sthā jaga̍c ca rejate | tvaṣṭā̍ ci̱t tava̍ ma̱nyava̱ indra̍ vevi̱jyate̍ bhi̱yārca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.15**

na̱hi nu yād a̍dhī̱masīndra̱ṁ ko vī̱ryā̍ pa̱raḥ | tasmi̍n nṛ̱mṇam u̱ta kratu̍ṁ de̱vā ojā̍ṁsi̱ saṁ da̍dhu̱r arca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.80.16**

yām atha̍rvā̱ manu̍ṣ pi̱tā da̱dhyaṅ dhiya̱m atna̍ta | tasmi̱n brahmā̍ṇi pū̱rvathendra̍ u̱kthā sam a̍gma̱tārca̱nn anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.81.1**

indro̱ madā̍ya vāvṛdhe̱ śava̍se vṛtra̱hā nṛbhi̍ḥ | tam in ma̱hatsv ā̱jiṣū̱tem arbhe̍ havāmahe̱ sa vāje̍ṣu̱ pra no̍ 'viṣat ||

**1.81.2**

asi̱ hi vī̍ra̱ senyo 'si̱ bhūri̍ parāda̱diḥ | asi̍ da̱bhrasya̍ cid vṛ̱dho yaja̍mānāya śikṣasi sunva̱te bhūri̍ te̱ vasu̍ ||

**1.81.3**

yad u̱dīra̍ta ā̱jayo̍ dhṛ̱ṣṇave̍ dhīyate̱ dhanā̍ | yu̱kṣvā ma̍da̱cyutā̱ harī̱ kaṁ hana̱ḥ kaṁ vasau̍ dadho̱ 'smām̐ i̍ndra̱ vasau̍ dadhaḥ ||

**1.81.4**

kratvā̍ ma̱hām̐ a̍nuṣva̱dham bhī̱ma ā vā̍vṛdhe̱ śava̍ḥ | śri̱ya ṛ̱ṣva u̍pā̱kayo̱r ni śi̱prī hari̍vān dadhe̱ hasta̍yo̱r vajra̍m āya̱sam ||

**1.81.5**

ā pa̍prau̱ pārthi̍va̱ṁ rajo̍ badba̱dhe ro̍ca̱nā di̱vi | na tvāvā̍m̐ indra̱ kaś ca̱na na jā̱to na ja̍niṣya̱te 'ti̱ viśva̍ṁ vavakṣitha ||

**1.81.6**

yo a̱ryo ma̍rta̱bhoja̍nam parā̱dadā̍ti dā̱śuṣe̍ | indro̍ a̱smabhya̍ṁ śikṣatu̱ vi bha̍jā̱ bhūri̍ te̱ vasu̱ bhakṣī̍ya tava̱ rādha̍saḥ ||

**1.81.7**

made̍-made̱ hi no̍ da̱dir yū̱thā gavā̍m ṛju̱kratu̍ḥ | saṁ gṛ̍bhāya pu̱rū śa̱tobha̍yāha̱styā vasu̱ śiśī̱hi rā̱ya ā bha̍ra ||

**1.81.8**

mā̱daya̍sva su̱te sacā̱ śava̍se śūra̱ rādha̍se | vi̱dmā hi tvā̍ purū̱vasum upa̱ kāmā̍n sasṛ̱jmahe 'thā̍ no 'vi̱tā bha̍va ||

**1.81.9**

e̱te ta̍ indra ja̱ntavo̱ viśva̍m puṣyanti̱ vārya̍m | a̱ntar hi khyo janā̍nām a̱ryo vedo̱ adā̍śuṣā̱ṁ teṣā̍ṁ no̱ veda̱ ā bha̍ra ||

**1.82.1**

upo̱ ṣu śṛ̍ṇu̱hī giro̱ magha̍va̱n māta̍thā iva | ya̱dā na̍ḥ sū̱nṛtā̍vata̱ḥ kara̱ ād a̱rthayā̍sa̱ id yojā̱ nv i̍ndra te̱ harī̍ ||

**1.82.2**

akṣa̱nn amī̍madanta̱ hy ava̍ pri̱yā a̍dhūṣata | asto̍ṣata̱ svabhā̍navo̱ viprā̱ navi̍ṣṭhayā ma̱tī yojā̱ nv i̍ndra te̱ harī̍ ||

**1.82.3**

su̱sa̱ṁdṛśa̍ṁ tvā va̱yam magha̍van vandiṣī̱mahi̍ | pra nū̱nam pū̱rṇava̍ndhuraḥ stu̱to yā̍hi̱ vaśā̱m̐ anu̱ yojā̱ nv i̍ndra te̱ harī̍ ||

**1.82.4**

sa ghā̱ taṁ vṛṣa̍ṇa̱ṁ ratha̱m adhi̍ tiṣṭhāti go̱vida̍m | yaḥ pātra̍ṁ hāriyoja̱nam pū̱rṇam i̍ndra̱ cike̍tati̱ yojā̱ nv i̍ndra te̱ harī̍ ||

**1.82.5**

yu̱ktas te̍ astu̱ dakṣi̍ṇa u̱ta sa̱vyaḥ śa̍takrato | tena̍ jā̱yām upa̍ pri̱yām ma̍ndā̱no yā̱hy andha̍so̱ yojā̱ nv i̍ndra te̱ harī̍ ||

**1.82.6**

yu̱najmi̍ te̱ brahma̍ṇā ke̱śinā̱ harī̱ upa̱ pra yā̍hi dadhi̱ṣe gabha̍styoḥ | ut tvā̍ su̱tāso̍ rabha̱sā a̍mandiṣuḥ pūṣa̱ṇvān va̍jri̱n sam u̱ patnyā̍madaḥ ||

**1.83.1**

aśvā̍vati pratha̱mo goṣu̍ gacchati suprā̱vīr i̍ndra̱ martya̱s tavo̱tibhi̍ḥ | tam it pṛ̍ṇakṣi̱ vasu̍nā̱ bhavī̍yasā̱ sindhu̱m āpo̱ yathā̱bhito̱ vice̍tasaḥ ||

**1.83.2**

āpo̱ na de̱vīr upa̍ yanti ho̱triya̍m a̱vaḥ pa̍śyanti̱ vita̍ta̱ṁ yathā̱ raja̍ḥ | prā̱cair de̱vāsa̱ḥ pra ṇa̍yanti deva̱yum bra̍hma̱priya̍ṁ joṣayante va̱rā i̍va ||

**1.83.3**

adhi̱ dvayo̍r adadhā u̱kthya1̱̍ṁ vaco̍ ya̱tasru̍cā mithu̱nā yā sa̍pa̱ryata̍ḥ | asa̍ṁyatto vra̱te te̍ kṣeti̱ puṣya̍ti bha̱drā śa̱ktir yaja̍mānāya sunva̱te ||

**1.83.4**

ād aṅgi̍rāḥ pratha̱maṁ da̍dhire̱ vaya̍ i̱ddhāgna̍ya̱ḥ śamyā̱ ye su̍kṛ̱tyayā̍ | sarva̍m pa̱ṇeḥ sam a̍vindanta̱ bhoja̍na̱m aśvā̍vanta̱ṁ goma̍nta̱m ā pa̱śuṁ nara̍ḥ ||

**1.83.5**

ya̱jñair atha̍rvā pratha̱maḥ pa̱thas ta̍te̱ tata̱ḥ sūryo̍ vrata̱pā ve̱na āja̍ni | ā gā ā̍jad u̱śanā̍ kā̱vyaḥ sacā̍ ya̱masya̍ jā̱tam a̱mṛta̍ṁ yajāmahe ||

**1.83.6**

ba̱rhir vā̱ yat sva̍pa̱tyāya̍ vṛ̱jyate̱ 'rko vā̱ śloka̍m ā̱ghoṣa̍te di̱vi | grāvā̱ yatra̱ vada̍ti kā̱rur u̱kthya1̱̍s tasyed indro̍ abhipi̱tveṣu̍ raṇyati ||

**1.84.1**

asā̍vi̱ soma̍ indra te̱ śavi̍ṣṭha dhṛṣṇa̱v ā ga̍hi | ā tvā̍ pṛṇaktv indri̱yaṁ raja̱ḥ sūryo̱ na ra̱śmibhi̍ḥ ||

**1.84.2**

indra̱m id dharī̍ vaha̱to 'pra̍tidhṛṣṭaśavasam | ṛṣī̍ṇāṁ ca stu̱tīr upa̍ ya̱jñaṁ ca̱ mānu̍ṣāṇām ||

**1.84.3**

ā ti̍ṣṭha vṛtraha̱n ratha̍ṁ yu̱ktā te̱ brahma̍ṇā̱ harī̍ | a̱rvā̱cīna̱ṁ su te̱ mano̱ grāvā̍ kṛṇotu va̱gnunā̍ ||

**1.84.4**

i̱mam i̍ndra su̱tam pi̍ba̱ jyeṣṭha̱m ama̍rtya̱m mada̍m | śu̱krasya̍ tvā̱bhy a̍kṣara̱n dhārā̍ ṛ̱tasya̱ sāda̍ne ||

**1.84.5**

indrā̍ya nū̱nam a̍rcato̱kthāni̍ ca bravītana | su̱tā a̍matsu̱r inda̍vo̱ jyeṣṭha̍ṁ namasyatā̱ saha̍ḥ ||

**1.84.6**

naki̱ṣ ṭvad ra̱thīta̍ro̱ harī̱ yad i̍ndra̱ yaccha̍se | naki̱ṣ ṭvānu̍ ma̱jmanā̱ naki̱ḥ svaśva̍ ānaśe ||

**1.84.7**

ya eka̱ id vi̱daya̍te̱ vasu̱ martā̍ya dā̱śuṣe̍ | īśā̍no̱ apra̍tiṣkuta̱ indro̍ a̱ṅga ||

**1.84.8**

ka̱dā marta̍m arā̱dhasa̍m pa̱dā kṣumpa̍m iva sphurat | ka̱dā na̍ḥ śuśrava̱d gira̱ indro̍ a̱ṅga ||

**1.84.9**

yaś ci̱d dhi tvā̍ ba̱hubhya̱ ā su̱tāvā̍m̐ ā̱vivā̍sati | u̱graṁ tat pa̍tyate̱ śava̱ indro̍ a̱ṅga ||

**1.84.10**

svā̱dor i̱tthā vi̍ṣū̱vato̱ madhva̍ḥ pibanti gau̱rya̍ḥ | yā indre̍ṇa sa̱yāva̍rī̱r vṛṣṇā̱ mada̍nti śo̱bhase̱ vasvī̱r anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.84.11**

tā a̍sya pṛśanā̱yuva̱ḥ soma̍ṁ śrīṇanti̱ pṛśna̍yaḥ | pri̱yā indra̍sya dhe̱navo̱ vajra̍ṁ hinvanti̱ sāya̍ka̱ṁ vasvī̱r anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.84.12**

tā a̍sya̱ nama̍sā̱ saha̍ḥ sapa̱ryanti̱ prace̍tasaḥ | vra̱tāny a̍sya saścire pu̱rūṇi̍ pū̱rvaci̍ttaye̱ vasvī̱r anu̍ sva̱rājya̍m ||

**1.84.13**

indro̍ dadhī̱co a̱sthabhi̍r vṛ̱trāṇy apra̍tiṣkutaḥ | ja̱ghāna̍ nava̱tīr nava̍ ||

**1.84.14**

i̱cchann aśva̍sya̱ yac chira̱ḥ parva̍te̱ṣv apa̍śritam | tad vi̍dac charya̱ṇāva̍ti ||

**1.84.15**

atrāha̱ gor a̍manvata̱ nāma̱ tvaṣṭu̍r apī̱cya̍m | i̱tthā ca̱ndrama̍so gṛ̱he ||

**1.84.16**

ko a̱dya yu̍ṅkte dhu̱ri gā ṛ̱tasya̱ śimī̍vato bhā̱mino̍ durhṛṇā̱yūn | ā̱sanni̍ṣūn hṛ̱tsvaso̍ mayo̱bhūn ya e̍ṣām bhṛ̱tyām ṛ̱ṇadha̱t sa jī̍vāt ||

**1.84.17**

ka ī̍ṣate tu̱jyate̱ ko bi̍bhāya̱ ko ma̍ṁsate̱ santa̱m indra̱ṁ ko anti̍ | kas to̱kāya̱ ka ibhā̍yo̱ta rā̱ye 'dhi̍ bravat ta̱nve̱3̱̍ ko janā̍ya ||

**1.84.18**

ko a̱gnim ī̍ṭṭe ha̱viṣā̍ ghṛ̱tena̍ sru̱cā ya̍jātā ṛ̱tubhi̍r dhru̱vebhi̍ḥ | kasmai̍ de̱vā ā va̍hān ā̱śu homa̱ ko ma̍ṁsate vī̱tiho̍traḥ sude̱vaḥ ||

**1.84.19**

tvam a̱ṅga pra śa̍ṁsiṣo de̱vaḥ śa̍viṣṭha̱ martya̍m | na tvad a̱nyo ma̍ghavann asti marḍi̱tendra̱ bravī̍mi te̱ vaca̍ḥ ||

**1.84.20**

mā te̱ rādhā̍ṁsi̱ mā ta̍ ū̱tayo̍ vaso̱ 'smān kadā̍ ca̱nā da̍bhan | viśvā̍ ca na upamimī̱hi mā̍nuṣa̱ vasū̍ni carṣa̱ṇibhya̱ ā ||

**1.85.1**

pra ye śumbha̍nte̱ jana̍yo̱ na sapta̍yo̱ yāma̍n ru̱drasya̍ sū̱nava̍ḥ su̱daṁsa̍saḥ | roda̍sī̱ hi ma̱ruta̍ś cakri̱re vṛ̱dhe mada̍nti vī̱rā vi̱dathe̍ṣu̱ ghṛṣva̍yaḥ ||

**1.85.2**

ta u̍kṣi̱tāso̍ mahi̱māna̍m āśata di̱vi ru̱drāso̱ adhi̍ cakrire̱ sada̍ḥ | arca̍nto a̱rkaṁ ja̱naya̍nta indri̱yam adhi̱ śriyo̍ dadhire̱ pṛśni̍mātaraḥ ||

**1.85.3**

gomā̍taro̱ yac chu̱bhaya̍nte a̱ñjibhi̍s ta̱nūṣu̍ śu̱bhrā da̍dhire vi̱rukma̍taḥ | bādha̍nte̱ viśva̍m abhimā̱tina̱m apa̱ vartmā̍ny eṣā̱m anu̍ rīyate ghṛ̱tam ||

**1.85.4**

vi ye bhrāja̍nte̱ suma̍khāsa ṛ̱ṣṭibhi̍ḥ pracyā̱vaya̍nto̱ acyu̍tā ci̱d oja̍sā | ma̱no̱juvo̱ yan ma̍ruto̱ rathe̱ṣv ā vṛṣa̍vrātāsa̱ḥ pṛṣa̍tī̱r ayu̍gdhvam ||

**1.85.5**

pra yad rathe̍ṣu̱ pṛṣa̍tī̱r ayu̍gdhva̱ṁ vāje̱ adri̍m maruto ra̱ṁhaya̍ntaḥ | u̱tāru̱ṣasya̱ vi ṣya̍nti̱ dhārā̱ś carme̍vo̱dabhi̱r vy u̍ndanti̱ bhūma̍ ||

**1.85.6**

ā vo̍ vahantu̱ sapta̍yo raghu̱ṣyado̍ raghu̱patvā̍na̱ḥ pra ji̍gāta bā̱hubhi̍ḥ | sīda̱tā ba̱rhir u̱ru va̱ḥ sada̍s kṛ̱tam mā̱daya̍dhvam maruto̱ madhvo̱ andha̍saḥ ||

**1.85.7**

te̍ 'vardhanta̱ svata̍vaso mahitva̱nā nāka̍ṁ ta̱sthur u̱ru ca̍krire̱ sada̍ḥ | viṣṇu̱r yad dhāva̱d vṛṣa̍ṇam mada̱cyuta̱ṁ vayo̱ na sī̍da̱nn adhi̍ ba̱rhiṣi̍ pri̱ye ||

**1.85.8**

śūrā̍ i̱ved yuyu̍dhayo̱ na jagma̍yaḥ śrava̱syavo̱ na pṛta̍nāsu yetire | bhaya̍nte̱ viśvā̱ bhuva̍nā ma̱rudbhyo̱ rājā̍na iva tve̱ṣasa̍ṁdṛśo̱ nara̍ḥ ||

**1.85.9**

tvaṣṭā̱ yad vajra̱ṁ sukṛ̍taṁ hira̱ṇyaya̍ṁ sa̱hasra̍bhṛṣṭi̱ṁ svapā̱ ava̍rtayat | dha̱tta indro̱ nary apā̍ṁsi̱ karta̱ve 'ha̍n vṛ̱traṁ nir a̱pām au̍bjad arṇa̱vam ||

**1.85.10**

ū̱rdhvaṁ nu̍nudre 'va̱taṁ ta oja̍sā dādṛhā̱ṇaṁ ci̍d bibhidu̱r vi parva̍tam | dhama̍nto vā̱ṇam ma̱ruta̍ḥ su̱dāna̍vo̱ made̱ soma̍sya̱ raṇyā̍ni cakrire ||

**1.85.11**

ji̱hmaṁ nu̍nudre 'va̱taṁ tayā̍ di̱śāsi̍ñca̱nn utsa̱ṁ gota̍māya tṛ̱ṣṇaje̍ | ā ga̍cchantī̱m ava̍sā ci̱trabhā̍nava̱ḥ kāma̱ṁ vipra̍sya tarpayanta̱ dhāma̍bhiḥ ||

**1.85.12**

yā va̱ḥ śarma̍ śaśamā̱nāya̱ santi̍ tri̱dhātū̍ni dā̱śuṣe̍ yaccha̱tādhi̍ | a̱smabhya̱ṁ tāni̍ maruto̱ vi ya̍nta ra̱yiṁ no̍ dhatta vṛṣaṇaḥ su̱vīra̍m ||

**1.86.1**

maru̍to̱ yasya̱ hi kṣaye̍ pā̱thā di̱vo vi̍mahasaḥ | sa su̍go̱pāta̍mo̱ jana̍ḥ ||

**1.86.2**

ya̱jñair vā̍ yajñavāhaso̱ vipra̍sya vā matī̱nām | maru̍taḥ śṛṇu̱tā hava̍m ||

**1.86.3**

u̱ta vā̱ yasya̍ vā̱jino 'nu̱ vipra̱m ata̍kṣata | sa gantā̱ goma̍ti vra̱je ||

**1.86.4**

a̱sya vī̱rasya̍ ba̱rhiṣi̍ su̱taḥ somo̱ divi̍ṣṭiṣu | u̱ktham mada̍ś ca śasyate ||

**1.86.5**

a̱sya śro̍ṣa̱ntv ā bhuvo̱ viśvā̱ yaś ca̍rṣa̱ṇīr a̱bhi | sūra̍ṁ cit sa̱sruṣī̱r iṣa̍ḥ ||

**1.86.6**

pū̱rvībhi̱r hi da̍dāśi̱ma śa̱radbhi̍r maruto va̱yam | avo̍bhiś carṣaṇī̱nām ||

**1.86.7**

su̱bhaga̱ḥ sa pra̍yajyavo̱ maru̍to astu̱ martya̍ḥ | yasya̱ prayā̍ṁsi̱ parṣa̍tha ||

**1.86.8**

śa̱śa̱mā̱nasya̍ vā nara̱ḥ sveda̍sya satyaśavasaḥ | vi̱dā kāma̍sya̱ vena̍taḥ ||

**1.86.9**

yū̱yaṁ tat sa̍tyaśavasa ā̱viṣ ka̍rta mahitva̱nā | vidhya̍tā vi̱dyutā̱ rakṣa̍ḥ ||

**1.86.10**

gūha̍tā̱ guhya̱ṁ tamo̱ vi yā̍ta̱ viśva̍m a̱triṇa̍m | jyoti̍ṣ kartā̱ yad u̱śmasi̍ ||

**1.87.1**

pratva̍kṣasa̱ḥ prata̍vaso vira̱pśino 'nā̍natā̱ avi̍thurā ṛjī̱ṣiṇa̍ḥ | juṣṭa̍tamāso̱ nṛta̍māso a̱ñjibhi̱r vy ā̍najre̱ ke ci̍d u̱srā i̍va̱ stṛbhi̍ḥ ||

**1.87.2**

u̱pa̱hva̱reṣu̱ yad aci̍dhvaṁ ya̱yiṁ vaya̍ iva maruta̱ḥ kena̍ cit pa̱thā | ścota̍nti̱ kośā̱ upa̍ vo̱ rathe̱ṣv ā ghṛ̱tam u̍kṣatā̱ madhu̍varṇa̱m arca̍te ||

**1.87.3**

praiṣā̱m ajme̍ṣu vithu̱reva̍ rejate̱ bhūmi̱r yāme̍ṣu̱ yad dha̍ yu̱ñjate̍ śu̱bhe | te krī̱ḻayo̱ dhuna̍yo̱ bhrāja̍dṛṣṭayaḥ sva̱yam ma̍hi̱tvam pa̍nayanta̱ dhūta̍yaḥ ||

**1.87.4**

sa hi sva̱sṛt pṛṣa̍daśvo̱ yuvā̍ ga̱ṇo̱3̱̍ 'yā ī̍śā̱nas tavi̍ṣībhi̱r āvṛ̍taḥ | asi̍ sa̱tya ṛ̍ṇa̱yāvāne̍dyo̱ 'syā dhi̱yaḥ prā̍vi̱tāthā̱ vṛṣā̍ ga̱ṇaḥ ||

**1.87.5**

pi̱tuḥ pra̱tnasya̱ janma̍nā vadāmasi̱ soma̍sya ji̱hvā pra ji̍gāti̱ cakṣa̍sā | yad ī̱m indra̱ṁ śamy ṛkvā̍ṇa̱ āśa̱tād in nāmā̍ni ya̱jñiyā̍ni dadhire ||

**1.87.6**

śri̱yase̱ kam bhā̱nubhi̱ḥ sam mi̍mikṣire̱ te ra̱śmibhi̱s ta ṛkva̍bhiḥ sukhā̱daya̍ḥ | te vāśī̍manta i̱ṣmiṇo̱ abhī̍ravo vi̱dre pri̱yasya̱ māru̍tasya̱ dhāmna̍ḥ ||

**1.88.1**

ā vi̱dyunma̍dbhir marutaḥ sva̱rkai rathe̍bhir yāta ṛṣṭi̱madbhi̱r aśva̍parṇaiḥ | ā varṣi̍ṣṭhayā na i̱ṣā vayo̱ na pa̍ptatā sumāyāḥ ||

**1.88.2**

te̍ 'ru̱ṇebhi̱r vara̱m ā pi̱śaṅgai̍ḥ śu̱bhe kaṁ yā̍nti ratha̱tūrbhi̱r aśvai̍ḥ | ru̱kmo na ci̱traḥ svadhi̍tīvān pa̱vyā ratha̍sya jaṅghananta̱ bhūma̍ ||

**1.88.3**

śri̱ye kaṁ vo̱ adhi̍ ta̱nūṣu̱ vāśī̍r me̱dhā vanā̱ na kṛ̍ṇavanta ū̱rdhvā | yu̱ṣmabhya̱ṁ kam ma̍rutaḥ sujātās tuvidyu̱mnāso̍ dhanayante̱ adri̍m ||

**1.88.4**

ahā̍ni̱ gṛdhrā̱ḥ pary ā va̱ āgu̍r i̱māṁ dhiya̍ṁ vārkā̱ryāṁ ca̍ de̱vīm | brahma̍ kṛ̱ṇvanto̱ gota̍māso a̱rkair ū̱rdhvaṁ nu̍nudra utsa̱dhim piba̍dhyai ||

**1.88.5**

e̱tat tyan na yoja̍nam aceti sa̱svar ha̱ yan ma̍ruto̱ gota̍mo vaḥ | paśya̱n hira̍ṇyacakrā̱n ayo̍daṁṣṭrān vi̱dhāva̍to va̱rāhū̍n ||

**1.88.6**

e̱ṣā syā vo̍ maruto 'nubha̱rtrī prati̍ ṣṭobhati vā̱ghato̱ na vāṇī̍ | asto̍bhaya̱d vṛthā̍sā̱m anu̍ sva̱dhāṁ gabha̍styoḥ ||

**1.89.1**

ā no̍ bha̱drāḥ krata̍vo yantu vi̱śvato 'da̍bdhāso̱ apa̍rītāsa u̱dbhida̍ḥ | de̱vā no̱ yathā̱ sada̱m id vṛ̱dhe asa̱nn aprā̍yuvo rakṣi̱tāro̍ di̱ve-di̍ve ||

**1.89.2**

de̱vānā̍m bha̱drā su̍ma̱tir ṛ̍jūya̱tāṁ de̱vānā̍ṁ rā̱tir a̱bhi no̱ ni va̍rtatām | de̱vānā̍ṁ sa̱khyam upa̍ sedimā va̱yaṁ de̱vā na̱ āyu̱ḥ pra ti̍rantu jī̱vase̍ ||

**1.89.3**

tān pūrva̍yā ni̱vidā̍ hūmahe va̱yam bhaga̍m mi̱tram adi̍ti̱ṁ dakṣa̍m a̱sridha̍m | a̱rya̱maṇa̱ṁ varu̍ṇa̱ṁ soma̍m a̱śvinā̱ sara̍svatī naḥ su̱bhagā̱ maya̍s karat ||

**1.89.4**

tan no̱ vāto̍ mayo̱bhu vā̍tu bheṣa̱jaṁ tan mā̱tā pṛ̍thi̱vī tat pi̱tā dyauḥ | tad grāvā̍ṇaḥ soma̱suto̍ mayo̱bhuva̱s tad a̍śvinā śṛṇutaṁ dhiṣṇyā yu̱vam ||

**1.89.5**

tam īśā̍na̱ṁ jaga̍tas ta̱sthuṣa̱s pati̍ṁ dhiyaṁji̱nvam ava̍se hūmahe va̱yam | pū̱ṣā no̱ yathā̱ veda̍sā̱m asa̍d vṛ̱dhe ra̍kṣi̱tā pā̱yur ada̍bdhaḥ sva̱staye̍ ||

**1.89.6**

sva̱sti na̱ indro̍ vṛ̱ddhaśra̍vāḥ sva̱sti na̍ḥ pū̱ṣā vi̱śvave̍dāḥ | sva̱sti na̱s tārkṣyo̱ ari̍ṣṭanemiḥ sva̱sti no̱ bṛha̱spati̍r dadhātu ||

**1.89.7**

pṛṣa̍daśvā ma̱ruta̱ḥ pṛśni̍mātaraḥ śubha̱ṁyāvā̍no vi̱dathe̍ṣu̱ jagm̍ayaḥ | a̱gni̱ji̱hvā mana̍va̱ḥ sūra̍cakṣaso̱ viśve̍ no de̱vā ava̱sā ga̍mann i̱ha ||

**1.89.8**

bha̱draṁ karṇe̍bhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devā bha̱dram pa̍śyemā̱kṣabhi̍r yajatrāḥ | sthi̱rair aṅgai̍s tuṣṭu̱vāṁsa̍s ta̱nūbhi̱r vy a̍śema de̱vahi̍ta̱ṁ yad āyu̍ḥ ||

**1.89.9**

śa̱tam in nu śa̱rado̱ anti̍ devā̱ yatrā̍ naś ca̱krā ja̱rasa̍ṁ ta̱nūnā̍m | pu̱trāso̱ yatra̍ pi̱taro̱ bhava̍nti̱ mā no̍ ma̱dhyā rī̍riṣa̱tāyu̱r ganto̍ḥ ||

**1.89.10**

adi̍ti̱r dyaur adi̍tir a̱ntari̍kṣa̱m adi̍tir mā̱tā sa pi̱tā sa pu̱traḥ | viśve̍ de̱vā adi̍ti̱ḥ pañca̱ janā̱ adi̍tir jā̱tam adi̍ti̱r jani̍tvam ||

**1.90.1**

ṛ̱ju̱nī̱tī no̱ varu̍ṇo mi̱tro na̍yatu vi̱dvān | a̱rya̱mā de̱vaiḥ sa̱joṣā̍ḥ ||

**1.90.2**

te hi vasvo̱ vasa̍vānā̱s te apra̍mūrā̱ maho̍bhiḥ | vra̱tā ra̍kṣante vi̱śvāhā̍ ||

**1.90.3**

te a̱smabhya̱ṁ śarma̍ yaṁsann a̱mṛtā̱ martye̍bhyaḥ | bādha̍mānā̱ apa̱ dviṣa̍ḥ ||

**1.90.4**

vi na̍ḥ pa̱thaḥ su̍vi̱tāya̍ ci̱yantv indro̍ ma̱ruta̍ḥ | pū̱ṣā bhago̱ vandyā̍saḥ ||

**1.90.5**

u̱ta no̱ dhiyo̱ goa̍grā̱ḥ pūṣa̱n viṣṇa̱v eva̍yāvaḥ | kartā̍ naḥ svasti̱mata̍ḥ ||

**1.90.6**

madhu̱ vātā̍ ṛtāya̱te madhu̍ kṣaranti̱ sindha̍vaḥ | mādhvī̍r naḥ sa̱ntv oṣa̍dhīḥ ||

**1.90.7**

madhu̱ nakta̍m u̱toṣaso̱ madhu̍ma̱t pārthi̍va̱ṁ raja̍ḥ | madhu̱ dyaur a̍stu naḥ pi̱tā ||

**1.90.8**

madhu̍mān no̱ vana̱spati̱r madhu̍mām̐ astu̱ sūrya̍ḥ | mādhvī̱r gāvo̍ bhavantu naḥ ||

**1.90.9**

śaṁ no̍ mi̱traḥ śaṁ varu̍ṇa̱ḥ śaṁ no̍ bhavatv arya̱mā | śaṁ na̱ indro̱ bṛha̱spati̱ḥ śaṁ no̱ viṣṇu̍r urukra̱maḥ ||

**1.91.1**

tvaṁ soma pra cikito manīṣā tvaṁ rajiṣṭham anu neṣi panthām | tava praṇītī pitaro na indo deveṣu ratnam abhajanta dhīrāḥ ||

**1.91.2**

tvaṁ soma kratubhiḥ sukratur bhūs tvaṁ dakṣaiḥ sudakṣo viśvavedāḥ | tvaṁ vṛṣā vṛṣatvebhir mahitvā dyumnebhir dyumny abhavo nṛcakṣāḥ ||

**1.91.3**

rājño nu te varuṇasya vratāni bṛhad gabhīraṁ tava soma dhāma | śuciṣ ṭvam asi priyo na mitro dakṣāyyo aryamevāsi soma ||

**1.91.4**

yā te dhāmāni divi yā pṛthivyāṁ yā parvateṣv oṣadhīṣv apsu | tebhir no viśvaiḥ sumanā aheḻan rājan soma prati havyā gṛbhāya ||

**1.91.5**

tvaṁ somāsi satpatis tvaṁ rājota vṛtrahā | tvam bhadro asi kratuḥ ||

**1.91.6**

tvaṁ ca soma no vaśo jīvātuṁ na marāmahe | priyastotro vanaspatiḥ ||

**1.91.7**

tvaṁ soma mahe bhagaṁ tvaṁ yūna ṛtāyate | dakṣaṁ dadhāsi jīvase ||

**1.91.8**

tvaṁ naḥ soma viśvato rakṣā rājann aghāyataḥ | na riṣyet tvāvataḥ sakhā ||

**1.91.9**

soma yās te mayobhuva ūtayaḥ santi dāśuṣe | tābhir no vitā bhava ||

**1.91.10**

imaṁ yajñam idaṁ vaco jujuṣāṇa upāgahi | soma tvaṁ no vṛdhe bhava ||

**1.91.11**

soma gīrbhiṣ ṭvā vayaṁ vardhayāmo vacovidaḥ | sumṛḻīko na ā viśa ||

**1.91.12**

gayasphāno amīvahā vasuvit puṣṭivardhanaḥ | sumitraḥ soma no bhava ||

**1.91.13**

soma rārandhi no hṛdi gāvo na yavaseṣv ā | marya iva sva okye ||

**1.91.14**

yaḥ soma sakhye tava rāraṇad deva martyaḥ | taṁ dakṣaḥ sacate kaviḥ ||

**1.91.15**

uruṣyā ṇo abhiśasteḥ soma ni pāhy aṁhasaḥ | sakhā suśeva edhi naḥ ||

**1.91.16**

ā pyāyasva sam etu te viśvataḥ soma vṛṣṇyam | bhavā vājasya saṁgathe ||

**1.91.17**

ā pyāyasva madintama soma viśvebhir aṁśubhiḥ | bhavā naḥ suśravastamaḥ sakhā vṛdhe ||

**1.91.18**

saṁ te payāṁsi sam u yantu vājāḥ saṁ vṛṣṇyāny abhimātiṣāhaḥ | āpyāyamāno amṛtāya soma divi śravāṁsy uttamāni dhiṣva ||

**1.91.19**

yā te dhāmāni haviṣā yajanti tā te viśvā paribhūr astu yajñam | gayasphānaḥ prataraṇaḥ suvīro vīrahā pra carā soma duryān ||

**1.91.20**

somo dhenuṁ somo arvantam āśuṁ somo vīraṁ karmaṇyaṁ dadāti | sādanyaṁ vidathyaṁ sabheya pitṛśravaṇaṁ yo dadāśad asmai ||

**1.91.21**

aṣāḻhaṁ yutsu pṛtanāsu papriṁ svarṣām apsāṁ vṛjanasya gopām | bhareṣujāṁ sukṣitiṁ suśravasoṁ jayantaṁ tvām anu madema soma ||

**1.91.22**

tvam imā oṣadhīḥ soma viśvās tvam apo ajanayas tvaṁ gāḥ | tvam ā tatanthorv antarikṣaṁ tvaṁ jyotiṣā vi tamo vavartha ||

**1.91.23**

devena no manasā deva soma rāyo bhāgaṁ sahasāvann abhi yudhya | mā tvā tanad īśiṣe vīryasyobhayebhyaḥ pra cikitsā gaviṣṭau ||

**1.92.1**

etā u tyā uṣasaḥ ketum akrata pūrve ardhe rajaso bhānum añjate | niṣkṛṇvānā āyudhānīva dhṛṣṇavaḥ prati gāvo ruṣīr yanti mātaraḥ ||

**1.92.2**

ud apaptann aruṇā bhānavo vṛthā svāyujo aruṣīr gā ayukṣata | akrann uṣāso vayunāni pūrvathā ruśantam bhānum aruṣīr aśiśrayuḥ ||

**1.92.3**

arcanti nārīr apaso na viṣṭibhiḥ samānena yojanena parāvataḥ | iṣaṁ vahantīḥ sukṛte sudānave viśved aha yajamānāya sunvate ||

**1.92.4**

adhi peśāṁsi vapate nṛtūr ivāporṇute vakṣa usreva barjaham | jyotir viśvasmai bhuvanāya kṛṇvatī gāvo na vrajaṁ vy uṣā avar tamaḥ ||

**1.92.5**

praty arcī ruśad asyā adarśi vi tiṣṭhate bādhate kṛṣṇam abhvam | svaruṁ na peśo vidatheṣv añjañ citraṁ divo duhitā bhānum aśret ||

**1.92.6**

atāriṣma tamasas pāram asyoṣā ucchantī vayunā kṛṇoti | śriye chando na smayate vibhātī supratīkā saumanasāyājīgaḥ ||

**1.92.7**

bhāsvatī netrī sūnṛtānāṁ divaḥ stave duhitā gotamebhiḥ | prajāvato nṛvato aśvabudhyān uṣo goagrām̐ upa māsi vājān ||

**1.92.8**

uṣas tam aśyāṁ yaśasaṁ suvīraṁ dāsapravargaṁ rayim aśvabudhyam | sudaṁsasā śravasā yā vibhāsi vājaprasūtā subhage bṛhantam ||

**1.92.9**

viśvāni devī bhuvanābhicakṣyā pratīcī cakṣur urviyā vi bhāti | viśvaṁ jīvaṁ carase bodhayantī viśvasya vācam avidan manāyoḥ ||

**1.92.10**

punaḥ-punar jāyamānā purāṇī samānaṁ varṇam abhi śumbhamānā | śvaghnīva kṛtnur vija āminānā martasya devī jarayanty āyuḥ ||

**1.92.11**

vyūrṇvatī divo antām abodhy apa svasāraṁ sanutar yuyoti | praminatī manuṣyā yugāni yoṣā jārasya cakṣasā vi bhāti ||

**1.92.12**

paśūn na citrā subhagā prathānā sindhur na kṣoda urviyā vy aśvait | aminatī daivyāni vratāni sūryasya ceti raśmibhir dṛśānā ||

**1.92.13**

uṣas tac citram ā bharāsmabhyaṁ vājinīvati | yena tokaṁ ca tanayaṁ ca dhāmahe ||

**1.92.14**

uṣo adyeha gomaty aśvāvati vibhāvari | revad asme vy uccha sūnṛtāvati ||

**1.92.15**

yukṣvā hi vājinīvaty aśvām̐ adyāruṇām̐ uṣaḥ | athā no viśvā saubhagāny ā vaha ||

**1.92.16**

aśvinā vartir asmad ā gomad dasrā hiraṇyavat | arvāg rathaṁ samanasā ni yacchatam ||

**1.92.17**

yāv itthā ślokam ā divo jyotir janāya cakrathuḥ | ā na ūrjaṁ vahatam aśvinā yuvam ||

**1.92.18**

eha devā mayobhuvā dasrā hiraṇyavartanī | uṣarbudho vahantu somapītaye ||

**1.93.1**

agnīṣomāv imaṁ su me śṛṇutaṁ vṛṣaṇā havam | prati sūktāni haryatam bhavataṁ dāśuṣe mayaḥ ||

**1.93.2**

agnīṣomā yo adya vāṁ idaṁ vacaḥ saparyati | tasmai dhattaṁ suvīryaṁ gavāṁ poṣaṁ svaśvyam ||

**1.93.3**

agnīṣomā ya āhutiṁ yo vāṁ dāśād dhaviṣkṛtim | sa prajayā suvīryaṁ viśvam āyur vy aśnavat ||

**1.93.4**

agnīṣomā ceti tad vīryaṁ vāṁ yad amuṣṇītam avasam paṇiṁ gāḥ | avātiratam bṛsayasya śeṣo vindataṁ jyotir ekam bahubhyaḥ ||

**1.93.5**

yuvam etāni divi rocanāny agniś ca soma sakratū adhattam | yuvaṁ sindhūm̐r abhiśaster avadyād agnīṣomāv amuñcataṁ gṛbhītān ||

**1.93.6**

ānyaṁ divo mātariśvā jabhārāmathnād anyam pari śyeno adreḥ | agnīṣomā brahmaṇā vāvṛdhānoruṁ yajñāya cakrathur u lokam ||

**1.93.7**

agnīṣomā haviṣaḥ prasthi tasya vītaṁ haryataṁ vṛṣaṇā juṣethām | suśarmāṇā svavosā hi bhūtam athā dhattaṁ yajamānāya śaṁ yoḥ ||

**1.93.8**

yo agnīṣomā haviṣā saparyād devadrīcā manasā yo ghṛtena | tasya vrataṁ rakṣatam pātam aṁhaso viśe janāya mahi śarma yacchatam ||

**1.93.9**

agnīṣomā savedasā sahūtī vanataṁ giraḥ | saṁ devatrā babhūvathuḥ ||

**1.93.10**

agnīṣomāv anena vāṁ yo vāṁ ghṛtena dāśati | tasmai dīdayatam bṛhat ||

**1.93.11**

agnīṣomāv imāni no yuvaṁ havyā jujoṣatam | ā yātam upa naḥ sacā ||

**1.93.12**

agnīṣomā pipṛtam arvato na ā pyāyantām usriyā havyasūdaḥ | asme balāni maghawatsu dhattaṁ kṛṇutaṁ no adhvaraṁ śruṣṭimantam ||

**1.94.1**

imaṁ stomam arhate jātavedase ratham iva sam mahemā manīṣayā | bhadrā hi naḥ pramatir asya saṁsady agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.2**

yasmai tvam āyajase sa sādhaty anarvā kṣeti dadhate suvīryam | sa tūtāva nainam aśnoty aṁhatir agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.3**

śakema tvā samidhaṁ sādhayā dhiyas tve devā havir adanty āhutam | tvam ādityām̐ ā vaha tān hy uśmasy agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.4**

bharāmedhmaṁ kṛṇavāmā havīṁṣi te citayantaḥ parvaṇā-parvaṇā vayam | jīvātave prataraṁ sādhayā dhiyo gne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.5**

viśāṁ gopā asya caranti jantavo dvipac ca yad uta catuṣpad aktubhiḥ | citraḥ praketa uṣaso mahām̐ asy agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.6**

tvam adhvaryur uta hotāsi pūrvyaḥ praśāstā potā januṣā purohitaḥ | viśvā vidvām̐ ārtwijyā dhīra puṣyasy agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.7**

yo viśvataḥ supratīkaḥ sadṛṅṅ asi dūre cit san taḻid ivāti rocase | rātryāś cid andho ati deva paśyasy agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.8**

pūrvo devā bhavatu sunvato ratho smākaṁ śaṁso abhy astu dūḍhyaḥ | tad ā jānītota puṣyatā vaco gne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.9**

vadhair duḥśaṁsām̐ apa dūḍhyo jahi dūre vā ye anti vā ke cid atriṇaḥ | athā yajñāya gṛṇate sugaṁ kṛdhy agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.10**

yad ayukthā aruṣā rohitā rathe vātajūtā vṛṣabhasye va te ravaḥ | ād invasi vanino dhūmaketonāgne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.11**

adha svanād uta bibhyuḥ patatriṇo drapsā yat te yavasādo vy asthiran | sugaṁ tat te tāvakebhyo rathebhyo gne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.12**

ayam mitrasya varuṇasya dhāyase vayātām marutāṁ heḻo adbhutaḥ | mṛḻā su no bhūtv eṣāṁ manaḥ punar agne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.13**

devo devānām asi mitro adbhuto vasur vasūnām asi cāruḥ adhvare | śarman syāma tava sapratha stame gne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.14**

tat te bhadraṁ yat samiddhaḥ sve dame somāhuto jarase mṛḻayattamaḥ | dadhāsi ratnaṁ draviṇaṁ ca dāśuṣe gne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṁ tava ||

**1.94.15**

yasmai tvaṁ sudraviṇo dadāśo nāgāstvam adite sarvatātā | yam bhadreṇa śavasā codayāsi prajāvatā rādhasā te syāma ||

**1.94.16**

sa tvam agne saubhagatvasya vidvān asmākam āyuḥ pra tireha deva | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.95.1**

dve virūpe carataḥ svarthe anyānyā vatsam upa dhāpayete | harir anyasyām bhavati svadhāvāñ chukro anyasyāṁ dadṛśe suvarcāḥ ||

**1.95.2**

daśemaṁ tvaṣṭur janayanta garbham atandrāso yuvatayo vibhṛtram | tigmānīkaṁ svayaśasaṁ janeṣu virocamānam pari ṣīṁ nayanti ||

**1.95.3**

trīṇi jānā pari bhūṣanty asya samudra ekaṁ divy ekam apsu | pūrvām anu pra diśam pārthivānām ṛtūn praśāsad vi dadhāv anuṣṭhu ||

**1.95.4**

ka imaṁ vo niṇyam ā ciketa vatso mātṝr janayata svadhābhiḥ | bahvīnāṁ garbho apasām upasthān mahān kavir niś carati svadhāvān ||

**1.95.5**

āviṣṭyo vardhate cāruḥ āsu jihmānām ūrdhvaḥ svayaśā upasthe | ubhe tvaṣṭur bibhyatur jāyamānāt pratīcī siṁham prati joṣayete ||

**1.95.6**

ubhe bhadre joṣayete na mene gāvo na vāśrā upa tasthuhr evaiḥ | sa dakṣāṇāṁ dakṣapatir babhūvāñjanti yaṁ dakṣiṇato havirbhiḥ ||

**1.95.7**

ud yaṁyamīti saviteva bāhū ubhe sicau yatate bhīma ṛñjan | uc chukram atkaṁ ajate simasmān navā mātṛbhyo vasanā jahāti ||

**1.95.8**

tveṣaṁ rūpaṁ kṛṇuta uttaraṁ yat sampṛñcānaḥ sadane gobhir adbhiḥ | kavir budhnam pari marmṛjyate dhīḥ sā devatātā samitir babhūva ||

**1.95.9**

uru te jrayaḥ pary eti budhnaṁ virocamānam mahiṣasya dhāma | viśvebhir agne svayaśobhir iddho dabdhebhiḥ pāyubhiḥ pāhy asmān ||

**1.95.10**

dhanvan srotaḥ kṛṇute gātum ūrmiṁ śukrair ūrmibhir abhi nakṣati kṣām | viśvā sanāni jaṭhareṣu dhatte ntar navāsu carati prasūṣu ||

**1.95.11**

evā no agne samidhā vṛdhāno revat pāvaka śravase vi bhāhi | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.96.1**

sa pratnathā sahasā jāyamānaḥ sadyaḥ kāvyāni baḻ adhatta viśvā | āpaś ca mitraṁ dhiṣaṇā ca sādhan devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.2**

sa pūrvayā nividā kavyatāyor imāḥ prajā ajanayan manūnām | vivasvatā cakṣasā dyām apaś ca devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.3**

tam īḻata prathamaṁ yajñasādhaṁ viśa ārīr āhutam ṛñjasānam | ūrjaḥ putram bharataṁ sṛpradānum devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.4**

sa mātariśvā puruvārapuṣṭir vidad gātuṁ tanayāya svarvit | viśāṁ gopā janitā rodasyor devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.5**

naktoṣāsā varṇam āmemyāne dhāpayete śiśum ekaṁ samīcī | dyāvākṣāmā rukmo antar vi bhāti devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.6**

rāyo budhnaḥ saṁgamano vasūnāṁ yajñasya ketur manmasādhano veḥ | amṛtatvaṁ rakṣamāṇāsa enaṁ devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.7**

nū ca purā ca sadanaṁ rayīṇāṁ jātasya ca jāyamānasya ca kṣām | sataś ca gopām bhavataś ca bhūrer devā agniṁ dhārayan draviṇodām ||

**1.96.8**

draviṇodā draviṇasas turasya draviṇodāḥ sanarasya pra yaṁsat | draviṇodā vīravatīm iṣaṁ no draviṇodā rāsate dīrgham āyuḥ ||

**1.96.9**

evā no agne samidhā vṛdhāno revat pāvaka śravase vi bhāhi | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.97.1**

apa naḥ śośucad agham agne śuśugdhy ā rayim | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.2**

sukṣetriyā sugātuyā vasūyā ca yajāmahe | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.3**

pra yad bhandiṣṭha eṣāṁ prāsmākāsaś ca sūrayaḥ | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.4**

pra yat te agne sūrayo jāyemahi pra te vayam | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.5**

pra yad agneḥ sahasvato viśvato yanti bhānavaḥ | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.6**

tvaṁ hi viśvatomukha viśvataḥ paribhūr asi | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.7**

dviṣo no viśvatomukhāti nāveva pāraya | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.97.8**

sa naḥ sindhum iva nāvayāti parṣā svastaye | apa naḥ śośucad agham ||

**1.98.1**

vaiśvānarasya sumatau syāma rājā hi kaṁ bhuvanānām abhiśrīḥ | ito jāto viśvam idaṁ vi caṣṭe vaiśvānaro yatate sūryeṇa ||

**1.98.2**

pṛṣṭo divi pṛṣṭo agniḥ pṛthivyām pṛṣṭo viśvā oṣadhīr ā viveśa | vaiśvānaraḥ sahasā pṛṣṭo agniḥ sa no divā sa riṣaḥ pātu naktam ||

**1.98.3**

vaiśvānara tava tat satyam astv asmān rāyo maghavānaḥ sacantām | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.99.1**

jātavedase sunavāma somaṁ arātīyato ni dahāti vedaḥ | sa naḥ parṣad ati durgāṇi viśvā nāveva sindhuṁ duritāty agniḥ ||

**1.100.1**

sa yo vṛṣā vṛṣṇyebhiḥ samokā maho divaḥ pṛthivyāś ca samrāṭ | satīnasatvā havyo bhareṣu marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.2**

yasyānāptaḥ sūryasyeva yāmo bhare-bhare vṛtrahā śuṣmo asti | vṛṣantamaḥ sakhibhiḥ svebhir evair marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.3**

divo na yasya retaso dughānāḥ panthāso yanti śavasāparītāḥ | taraddveṣāḥ sāsahiḥ pauṁsyebhir marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.4**

so aṅgirobhir aṅgirastamo bhūd vṛṣā vṛṣabhiḥ sakhibhiḥ sakhā san | ṛgmibhir ṛgmī gātubhir jyeṣṭho marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.5**

sa sūnubhir na rudrebhir ṛbhvā nṛṣāhye sāsahvām̐ amitrān | sanīḻebhiḥ śravasyāni tūrvan marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.6**

sa manyumīḥ samadanasya kartāsmākebhir nṛbhiḥ sūryaṁ sanat | asminn ahan satpatiḥ puruhūto marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.7**

tam ūtayo raṇayañ chūrasātau taṁ kṣemasya kṣitayaḥ kṛṇvata trām | sa viśvasya karuṇasyeśa eko marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.8**

tam apsanta śavasa utsaveṣu naro naram avase taṁ dhanāya | so andhe cit tamasi jyotir vidan marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.9**

sa savyena yamati vrādhataś cit sa dakṣiṇe saṁgṛbhītā kṛtāni | sa kīriṇā cit sanitā dhanāni marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.10**

sa grāmebhiḥ sanitā sa rathebhir vide viśvābhiḥ kṛṣṭibhir nv adya | sa pauṁsyebhir abhibhūr aśastīr marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.11**

sa jāmibhir yat samajāti mīḻhe jāmibhir vā puruhūta evaiḥ | apāṁ tokasya tanayasya jeṣe marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.12**

sa vajrabhṛd dasyuhā bhīma ugraḥ sahasracetāḥ śatanītha ṛbhvā | camrīṣo na śavasā pāñcajanyo marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.13**

tasya vajraḥ krandati smat svarṣā divo na tveṣo ravathaḥ śimīvān | taṁ sacante sanayas taṁ dhanāni marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.14**

yasyājasraṁ śavasā mānam uktham paribhujad rodasī viśvataḥ sīm | sa pāriṣat kratubhir mandasāno marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.15**

na yasya devā devatā na martā āpaś cana śavaso ntam āpuḥ | sa prarikvā tvakṣasā kṣmo divaś ca marutvān no bhavatv indra ūtī ||

**1.100.16**

rohic chyāvā sumadaṁśur lalāmīr dyukṣā rāya ṛjrāśvasya | vṛṣaṇvantam bibhratī dhūrṣu rathaṁ mandrā ciketa nāhuṣīṣu vikṣu ||

**1.100.17**

etat tyat ta indra vṛṣṇa ukthaṁ vārṣāgirā abhi gṛṇanti rādhaḥ | ṛjrāśvaḥ praṣṭibhir ambarīṣaḥ sahadevaḥ bhayamānaḥ surādhāḥ ||

**1.100.18**

dasyūñ chimyūm̐ś ca puruhūta evair hatvā pṛthivyāṁ śarvā ni barhīt | sanat kṣetraṁ sakhibhiḥ śvitnyebhiḥ sanat sūryaṁ sanad apaḥ suvajraḥ ||

**1.100.19**

viśvāhendro adhivaktā no stv aparihvṛtāḥ sanuyāma vājam | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.101.1**

pra mandine pitumad arcatā vaco yaḥ kṛṣṇagarbhā nirahann ṛjiśvanā | avasyavo vṛṣaṇaṁ vajradakṣiṇam marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.2**

yo vyaṁsaṁ jāhṛṣāṇena manyunā yaḥ śambaraṁ yo ahan piprum avratam | indro yaḥ śuṣṇam aśuṣaṁ ny āvṛṇaṅ marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.3**

yasya dyāvāpṛthivī pauṁsyam mahad yasya vrate varuṇo yasya sūryaḥ | yasyendrasya sindhavaḥ saścati vratam marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.4**

yo aśvānāṁ yo gavāṁ gopatir vaśī ya āritaḥ karmaṇi-karmaṇi sthiraḥ | vīḻoś cid indro yo asunvato vadho marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.5**

yo viśvasya jagataḥ prāṇatas patir yo brahmaṇe prathamo gā avindat | indro yo dasyūm̐r adharām̐ avātiran marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.6**

yaḥ śūrebhir havyo yaś ca bhīrubhir yo dhāvadbhir hūyate yaś ca jigyubhiḥ | indraṁ yaṁ viśvā bhuvanābhi saṁdadhur marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.7**

rudrāṇām eti pradiśā vicakṣaṇo rudrebhir yoṣā tanute pṛthu jrayaḥ | indram manīṣā abhy arcati śrutam marutvantaṁ sakhyāya havāmahe ||

**1.101.8**

yad vā marutvaḥ parame sadhasthe yad vāvame vṛjane mādayāse | ata ā yāhy adhvaraṁ no acchā tvāyā haviś cakṛmā satyarādhaḥ ||

**1.101.9**

tvāyendra somaṁ suṣumā sudakṣa tvāyā haviś cakṛmā brahmavāhaḥ | adhā niyutvaḥ sagaṇo marudbhir asmin yajñe barhiṣi mādayasva ||

**1.101.10**

mādayasva haribhir ye ta indra vi ṣyasva śipre vi sṛjasva dhene | ā tvā suśipra harayo vahantūśan havyāni prati no juṣasva ||

**1.101.11**

marutstotrasya vṛjanasya gopā vayam indreṇa sanuyāma vājam | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.102.1**

imāṁ te dhiyam pra bhare maho mahīm asya stotre dhiṣaṇā yat ta ānaje | tam utsave ca prasave ca sāsahim indraṁ devāsaḥ śavasāmadann anu ||

**1.102.2**

asya śravo nadyaḥ sapta bibhrati dyāvākṣāmā pṛthivī darśataṁ vapuḥ | asme sūryācandramasābhicakṣe śraddhe kam indra carato vitarturam ||

**1.102.3**

taṁ smā ratham maghavan prāva sātaye jaitraṁ yaṁ te anumadāma saṁgame | ājā na indra manasā puruṣṭuta tvāyadbhyo maghavañ charma yaccha naḥ ||

**1.102.4**

vayaṁ jayema tvayā yujā vṛtam asmākam aṁśam ud avā bhare-bhare | asmabhyam indra varivaḥ sugaṁ kṛdhi pra śatrūṇām maghavan vṛṣṇyā ruja ||

**1.102.5**

nānā hi tvā havamānā janā ime dhanānāṁ dhartar avasā vipanyavaḥ | asmākaṁ smā ratham ā tiṣṭha sātaye jaitraṁ hīndra nibhṛtam manas tava ||

**1.102.6**

gojitā bāhū amitakratuḥ simaḥ karman-karmaṁ chatamūtiḥ khajaṁkaraḥ | akalpa indraḥ pratimānam ojasāthā janā vi hvayante siṣāsavaḥ ||

**1.102.7**

ut te śatān maghavann uc ca bhūyasa ut sahasrād ririce kṛṣṭiṣu śravaḥ | amātraṁ tvā dhiṣaṇā titviṣe mahy adhā vṛtrāṇi jighnase puraṁdara ||

**1.102.8**

triviṣṭidhātu pratimānam ojasas tisro bhūmīr nṛpate trīṇi rocanā | atīdaṁ viśvam bhuvanaṁ vavakṣithāśatrur indra januṣā sanād asi ||

**1.102.9**

tvāṁ deveṣu prathamaṁ havāmahe tvam babhūtha pṛtanāsu sāsahiḥ | semaṁ naḥ kārum upamanyum udbhidam indraḥ kṛṇotu prasave ratham puraḥ ||

**1.102.10**

tvaṁ jigetha na dhanā ruroddhithārbheṣv ājā maghavan mahatsu ca | tvām ugram avase saṁ śiśīmahy athā na indra havaneṣu codaya ||

**1.102.11**

viśvāhendro adhivaktā no astv aparihvṛtāḥ sanuyāma vājam | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.103.1**

tat ta indriyas paramam parācair adhārayanta kavayaḥ puredam | kṣamedam anyad divy anyad asya sam ī pṛcyate samaneva ketuḥ ||

**1.103.2**

sa dhārayat pṛthivīm paprathac ca vajreṇa hatvā nir apaḥ sasarja | ahann ahim abhinad rauhinaṁ vy ahan vyaṁsam maghavā śacībhiḥ ||

**1.103.3**

sa jātūbharmā śraddadhāna ojaḥ puro vibhindan acarad vi dāsīḥ | vidvān vajrin dasave hetim asyāryaṁ saho vardhayā dyumnam indra ||

**1.103.4**

tad ūcuṣe mānuṣemā yugāni kīrtenyaṁ maghavā nāma bibhrat | upaprayann dasyuhatyāya vajrī yad dha sūnuḥ śravase nāma dadhe ||

**1.103.5**

tad asyedam paśyatā bhūri puṣṭaṁ śrad indrasya dhattana vīryāya | sa gā avindat so avindat aśvān sa oṣadhīḥ so apaḥ sa vanāni ||

**1.103.6**

bhūrikarmaṇe vṛṣabhāya vṛṣṇe satyaśuṣmāya sunavāma somam | ya ādṛtyā paripanthīva śūro 'yajvano vibhajann eti vedaḥ ||

**1.103.7**

tad indra preva vīryaṁ cakartha yat santam vajreṇābodhayas ahim | anu tvā patnīr hṛṣitaṁ vayaś ca viśve devāso amadann anu tvā ||

**1.103.8**

śuṣṇaṁ pipruṁ kuyavaṁ vṛtram indra yadāvadhīr vi puraḥ śambarasya | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.104.1**

yoniṣ ṭa indra niṣade akāri tam ā ni ṣīda svāno nārvā | vimucyā vayo avasāyāśvān doṣā vastor vahīyasaḥ prapitve ||

**1.104.2**

o tye nara indram ūtaye gur nū cit tān sadyo adhvano jagamyāt | devāso manyuṁ dāsasya ścamnan te na ā vakṣan suvitāya varṇam ||

**1.104.3**

ava tmanā bharate ketavedā ava tmanā bharate phenam udan | kṣīreṇa snātaḥ kuyavasya yoṣe hate te syātām pravaṇe śiphāyāḥ ||

**1.104.4**

yuyopa nābhir uparasya āyoḥ pra pūrvābhis tirate rāṣṭi śūraḥ | añjasī kuliśī vīrapatnī payo hinvānā udabhir bharante ||

**1.104.5**

prati yat syā nīthādarśi daśyor oko nācchā sadanaṁ jānatī gāt | adha smā no maghavañ carkṛtād in mā no magheva niṣṣapī parā dāḥ ||

**1.104.6**

sa tvaṁ na indra sūrye so apsvānāgāstvā ā bhaja jīvaśaṁse | māntarāṁ bhujam ā rīriṣo naḥ śraddhitaṁ te mahata indriāya ||

**1.104.7**

adhā manye śrat te asmā adhāyi vṛṣā codasva mahate dhanāya | mā no akṛte puruhūta yonāv indra kṣudhyadbhyo vaya āsutim dāḥ ||

**1.104.8**

mā no vadhīr indra mā parā dā mā naḥ priyā bhojanāni pra moṣīḥ | āṇḍā mā no maghavañ chakra nir bhen mā naḥ pātrā bhet sahajānuṣāṇi ||

**1.104.9**

arvāṅ ehi somakāmaṁ tvāhur ayaṁ sutas tasya pibā madāya | uruvyacā jaṭhara ā vṛṣasva piteva naḥ śṛṇuhi hūyamānaḥ ||

**1.105.1**

candramā apsvantara ā suparṇo dhāvate divi | na vo hiraṇyanemayaḥ padaṁ vindanti vidyuto vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.2**

artham id vā u arthinā ā jāyā yuvate patim | tuñjāte vṛṣṇyam payaḥ paridāya rasaṁ duhe vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.3**

mo ṣu devā adaḥ svar ava pādi divas pari | mā somyasya śambhuvaḥ śūne bhūma kadā cana vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.4**

yajñam pṛcchāmy avamaṁ sa tad dūto vi vocati | kva ṛtam pūrvyaṁ gataṁ kas tad bibharti nūtano vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.5**

amī ye devāḥ sthana triṣv ā rocane divaḥ | kad va ṛtaṁ kad anṛtaṁ kva pratnā va āhutir vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.6**

kad va ṛtasya dharṇasi kad varuṇasya cakṣaṇam | kad aryamṇo mahas pathāti krāmema dūḍhyo vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.7**

ahaṁ so asmi yaḥ purā sute vadāmi kāni cit | tam māvyanty ādhyo vṛko na tṛṣṇajam mṛgaṁ vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.8**

sam mā tapanty abhitaḥ sapatnīr iva parśavaḥ | mūṣo na śiśnā vy adanti mādhyaḥ stotāraṁ te śatakrato vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.9**

amī ye sapta raśmayas tatra me nābhir ātatā | tritas tad vedāptyaḥ sa jāmitvāya rebhati vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.10**

amī ye pañcokṣaṇo madhye tasthur maho divaḥ | devatrā nu pravācyaṁ sadhrīcīnā ni vāvṛtur vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.11**

suparṇā eta āsate madhya ārodhane divaḥ | te sedhanti patho vṛkaṁ tarantaṁ yahvatīr apo vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.12**

navyaṁ tad ukthyaṁ hitaṁ devāsaḥ supravācanam | ṛtam arṣanti sindhavaḥ satyaṁ tātāna sūryo vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.13**

agne tava tyad ukthyaṁ deveṣv asty āpyam | sa naḥ satto manuṣvad ā devān yakṣi viduṣṭaro vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.14**

satto hotā manuṣvad ā devām̐ acchā viduṣṭaraḥ | agnir havyā suṣūdati devo deveṣu medhiro vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.15**

brahmā kṛṇoti varuṇo gātuvidaṁ tam īmahe | vy ūrṇoti hṛdā matiṁ navyo jāyatām ṛtaṁ vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.16**

asau yaḥ panthā ādityo divi pravācyaṁ kṛtaḥ | na sa devā atikrame tam martāso na paśyatha vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.17**

tritaḥ kūpe avahito devān havata ūtaye | tac śuśrāva bṛhaspatiḥ kṛṇvann aṁhūraṇād uru vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.18**

aruṇo māṁ sakṛd vṛkaḥ pathā yantaṁ dadarśa hi | uj jihīte nicāyyā taṣṭeva pṛṣṭyāmayī vittam me asya rodasī ||

**1.105.19**

enāṅgūṣeṇa vayam indravanto abhi ṣyāma vṛjane sarvavīrāḥ | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.106.1**

indram mitraṁ varuṇam agnim ūtaye mārutaṁ śardho aditiṁ havāmahe | rathaṁ na durgād vasavaḥ sudānavo viśvasmān no aṁhaso niṣ pipartana ||

**1.106.2**

ta ādityā ā gatā sarvatātaye bhūta devā vṛtratūryeṣu śambhuvaḥ | rathaṁ na durgād vasavaḥ sudānavo viśvasmān no aṁhaso niṣ pipartana ||

**1.106.3**

avantu naḥ pitaraḥ supravācanā uta devī devapūtre ṛtāvṛdhā | rathaṁ na durgād vasavaḥ sudānavo viśvasmān no aṁhaso niṣ pipartana ||

**1.106.4**

narāśaṁsaṁ vājinaṁ vājayann iha kṣayadvīram pūṣaṇaṁ sumnair īmahe | rathaṁ na durgād vasavaḥ sudānavo viśvasmān no aṁhaso niṣ pipartana ||

**1.106.5**

bṛhaspate sadam in naḥ sugaṁ kṛdhi śaṁ yor yat te manurhitaṁ tad īmahe | rathaṁ na durgād vasavaḥ sudānavo viśvasmān no aṁhaso niṣ pipartana ||

**1.106.6**

indraṁ kutso vṛtrahaṇaṁ śacīpatiṁ kāṭe nibāḷha ṛṣir ahvad ūtaye | rathaṁ na durgād vasavaḥ sudānavo viśvasmān no aṁhaso niṣ pipartana ||

**1.106.7**

devair no devy aditir ni pātu devas trātā trāyatām aprayucchan | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.107.1**

yajño devānāṁ praty eti sumnam ādityāso bhavatā mṛḻayantaḥ | ā vo 'rvācī sumatirvavṛtyād aṁhoś cid yā varivovittarāsat ||

**1.107.2**

upa no devā avasā gamantu aṅgirasāṁ sāmabhiḥ stūyamānāḥ | indra indriyair marutaḥ marudbhir ādityair no aditiḥ śarma yaṁsat ||

**1.107.3**

tan na indras tad varuṇas tad agnis tad aryamā tat savitā cano dhāt | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.108.1**

ya indrāgnī citratamoratho vām abhi viśvāni bhuvanāni caṣṭe | tenā yātaṁ sarathaṁ tasthivāṁsā athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.2**

yāvad idaṁ bhuvanaṁ viśvam asty uruvyacā varimatā gabhīram | tāvāṁ ayaṁ pātave somo astv aram indrāgnī manase yuvabhyām ||

**1.108.3**

cakrethe hi sadhriyaṅ nāma bhadraṁ sadhrīcīnā vṛtrahaNā uta sthaḥ | tāv indrāgnī sadhrya-ñcā niṣadyā vṛṣṇaḥ somasya vṛṣaṇā vṛṣethām ||

**1.108.4**

samiddheṣv agniṣv ānajānā yatasrucā barhir u tistirāṇā | tīvraiḥ somai pariṣiktebhir arvāg endrāgnī saumanasāya yātam ||

**1.108.5**

yānīndrāgnī cakrethur vīryāṇi yāni rūpāṇy uta vṛṣṇyāni | yā vāṁ pratnāni sakhyā śivāni tebhiḥ somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.6**

yad abravam prathamaṁ vāṁ vṛṇāno 'yaṁ somo asurair no vivhavyaḥ | tāṁ satyāṁ śraddhām abhy ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.7**

yad indrāgnī madhataḥ sve duroṇe yad brahmaṇi rājani vā yajatrā | ataḥ pari vṛṣaṇāv ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.8**

yad indrāgnī yaduṣu turvaśeṣu yad druhyuṣv anuṣu pūruṣu sthaḥ | ataḥ pari vṛṣaṇāv ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.9**

yad indrāgnī avamasyāṁ pṛthivyāṁ madhyamasyāṁ paramasyāṁ uta sthaḥ | ataḥ pari vṛṣaṇāv ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.10**

yad indrāgnī paramasyāṁ pṛthivyāṁ madhyamasyāṁ avamasyāṁ uta sthaḥ | ataḥ pari vṛṣaṇāv ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.11**

yad indrāgnī divi ṣṭho yat pṛthivyāṁ yat parvateṣv oṣadhīṣv apsu | ataḥ pari vṛṣaṇāv ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.12**

yad indrāgnī uditā sūryasya madhye divaḥ svadhayā mādayethe | ataḥ pari vṛṣaṇāv ā hi yātam athā somasya pibataṁ sutasya ||

**1.108.13**

evendrāgnī papivāṁsā sutasya viśvāsmabhyaṁ saṁ jayataṁ dhanāni | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.109.1**

vi hy akhyam manasā vasya icchann indrāgnī jñāsa uta vā sajātān | nānyā yuvat pramatirastī mahyaṁ sa vāṁ dhiyaṁ vājayantīm atakṣam ||

**1.109.2**

aśravaṁ hi bhūridāvattarā vāṁ vijāmātur uta vā ghā syālāt | athā somasya prayatī yuvabhyām indrāgnī stomaṁ janayāmi navyam ||

**1.109.3**

mā cchedma raśmīṁr iti nādhamānāḥ pitṝṇāṁ śaktīr anuyacchamānāḥ | indrāgnibhyāṁ kaṁ vṛṣaṇo madanti tā hy adrī dhiṣaṇāyā upasthe ||

**1.109.4**

yuvābhyāṁ devī dhiṣaṇā madāyendrāgnī somam uśatī sunoti | tāv aśvinā bhadrahastā supāṇī ā dhāvatam madhunā pṛṅktam apsu ||

**1.109.5**

yuvām indrāgnī vasuno vibhāge tavastamā śuśrava vṛtrahatye | tāv āsadyā barhiṣi yajñe asmin pra carṣaṇī mādayethāṁ sutasya ||

**1.109.6**

pra carṣaṇibhyaḥ pṛtanāhaveṣu pra pṛthivyā riricāthe divaś ca | pra sindhumhyaḥ pra giribhyo mahitvā prendrāgnī viśvā bhuvanāty anyā ||

**1.109.7**

ā bharataṁ śikṣataṁ vajrabāhū asmāṁ indrāgnī avataṁ śacībhiḥ | ime nu te raśmayaḥ sūryasya yebhiḥ sapitvam pitaro na āsan ||

**1.109.8**

puraṁdarā śikṣataṁ vajrahastā asmāṁ indrāgnī avataṁ bhareṣu | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.110.1**

tatam me apas tad u tāyate punaḥ svādiṣṭhā dhītir ucathāya śasyate | ayaṁ samudra iha viśvadebyaḥ svāhākṛtasya saṁ u tṛpṇuta ṛbhavaḥ ||

**1.110.2**

ābhogayam pra yad icchanta aitanāpākāḥ prāñco mama ke cid āpayaḥ | saudhanvanāsaś caritasya bhūmanāgacchata saviturdāśuṣo gṛham ||

**1.110.3**

tat savitā vo amṛtatvam āsuvad agohyaṁ yac chrāvayanta aitana | tyaṁ cic camasam asurasya bhakṣaṇam ekaṁ santam akṛṇutā caturvayam ||

**1.110.4**

viṣṭvī śamī taraṇitvena vāghato martāsaḥ santo amṛtatvam ānaśuḥ | saudhanvanā ṛbhavaḥ sūracakṣasaḥ saṁvatsare saṁ apṛcyanta dhītibhiḥ ||

**1.110.5**

kṣetram iva vi mamus tejanenaṁ ekaṁ pātraṁ ṛbhavo jehamānam | upastutā upamaṁ nādhamānā amartyeṣu śrava icchamānāḥ ||

**1.110.6**

ā manīṣām antarikṣasya nṛbhyaḥ sruceva ghṛtaṁ juhavāma vidmanā | taraṇitvā ye pitur asya saścira ṛbhavo vājam aruhan divo rajaḥ ||

**1.110.7**

ṛbhur na indraḥ śavasā navīyān ṛbhur vājebhir vasubhir vasur dadiḥ | yuṣmākaṁ devā avasāhani priye abhi tiṣṭhema pṛtsutyīr asunvatām ||

**1.110.8**

niś carmaṇa ṛbhavo gām apiṁśata saṁ vatsenāsṛjatā mātaram punaḥ | saudhanvanāsaḥ svapasyayā naro jīvrī yuvānā pitarākṛṇotana ||

**1.110.9**

vājebhir no vājasātāv aviḍḍhy ṛbhumāṁ indra citram ā darṣi rādhaḥ | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.111.1**

takṣan rathaṁ suvṛtaṁ vidmanāpasas takṣan harī indrāvāhā vṛṣaṇvasū | takṣan pitṛbhyām ṛbhavo yuvad vayas takṣan vatsāya mātaraṁ sacābhuvam ||

**1.111.2**

ā no yajñāya takṣata ṛbhumad vayaḥ kratve dakṣāya suprajāvatīm iṣam | yathā kṣayāma sarvavīrayā viśā tan naḥ śardhāya dhāsathā sv indriyam ||

**1.111.3**

ā takṣata sātiṁ asmabhyam ṛbhavaḥ sātiṁ rathāya sātiṁ arvate naraḥ | sātiṁ no jaitrīṁ sam maheta viśvahā jāmim ajāmiṁ pṛtanāsu sakṣaṇim ||

**1.111.4**

ṛbhukṣaṇam indram ā huva ūtaya ṛbhūn vājān marutaḥ somapītaye | ubhā mitrāvaruṇā nūnam aśvinā te no hinvantu sātaye dhiye jiṣe ||

**1.111.5**

ṛbhur bharāya saṁ śiśātu sātiṁ samaryajid vājo asmāṁ aviṣṭu | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.112.1**

īḷe dyāvāpṛthivī pūrvacittaye agniṁ gharmaṁ surucaṁ yāmann iṣṭaye | yābhir bhare kāram aṁśāya jinvathas tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.2**

yuvor dānāya subharā asaścato ratham ā tasthur vacasaṁ na mantave | yābhir dhiyo avathaḥ karmann iṣṭaye tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.3**

yuvaṁ tāsāṁ divyasya praśāsane viśāṁ kṣayatho amṛtasya majmanā | yābhir dhenuṁ aśvam pinvatho narā tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.4**

yābhiḥ parijmā tanayasya majmanā dvimātā tūrṣu taraṇir vibhūṣati | yābhis trimantus abhavad vicakṣaṇas tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.5**

yābhīr rebhaṁ nivṛtaṁ sitam adbhya ud vandanam airayataṁ svar dṛśe | yābhiḥ kaṇvam pra siṣāsantam āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.6**

yābhir antakaṁ jasamānam āraṇe bhujyuṁ yābhir avyathibhir jijinvathuḥ | yābhiḥ karkandhum vayyaṁ ca jinvathas tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.7**

yābhiḥ śucantim dhanasāṁ suṣaṁsadam taptaṁ gharmam omyāvantam atraye | yābhiḥ pṛśnigum purukutsam āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.8**

yābhiḥ śacībhir vṛṣaṇā parāvṛjam prāndhaṁ śroṇaṁ cakṣasa etave kṛthaḥ | yābhir vartikāṁ grasitām amuñcataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.9**

yābhiḥ sindhum madhumantam asaścataṁ vasiṣṭhaṁ yābhir ajarāv ajinvatam | yābhiḥ kutsaṁ śrutaryaṁ naryam āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.10**

yābhir viśpalāṁ dhanasām atharvaṁ sahasramīḷha ājāv ajinvatam | yābhir vaśam aśvyam preṇim āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.11**

yābhiḥ sudānū auśijāya vaṇije dīrghaśravase madhu kośo akṣarat | kakṣīvantaṁ stotāraṁ yābhir āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.12**

yābhī rasāṁ kṣodasodnaḥ pipinvathu ranāśvaṁ yābhī ratham āvataṁ jiṣe | yābhis triśoka usriyā udājata tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.13**

yābhiḥ sūryam pariyāthaḥ parāvati mandhātāraṁ kṣaitrapatyeṣv āvataṁ | yābhir vipram pra bharadvājam āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.14**

yābhir mahām atithigvaṁ kaśojuvaṁ divodāsaṁ śambara-hatya āvataṁ | yābhiḥ pūrbhidye trasadasyu māvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.15**

yābhir vaṁraṁ vipipānam upastutaṁ kaliṁ yābhir vittajāniṁ duvasyathaḥ | yābhir vyaśvam uta pṛthim āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.16**

yābhir narā śayave yābhir atraye yābhiḥ purā manave gātum īṣathuḥ | yābhiḥ śārīr ājataṁ syūmaraśmaye tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.17**

yābhiḥ paṭharvā jaṭharasya majmanā agnir nādīdec cita iddho ajmann ā | yābhiḥ śaryātam avattho mahādhane tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.18**

yābhir aṅgiro manasā niranyatho 'graṁ gacchatho vivare goarṇasaḥ | yābhir manuṁ śūram iṣā samāvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.19**

yābhiḥ patnīr vimadāya nyūhathu ā gha vā yābhir aruṇīr aśikṣatam | yābhiḥ sudāsa ūhathuḥ sudevyaṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.20**

yābhiḥ śaṁtātī bhavatho dadāśuṣe bhujyuṁ yābhir avattho yābhir adhrigum | omyāvatīṁ subharāṁ ṛtastubhaṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.21**

yābhiḥ kṛśānum asane duvasyatho jave yābhir yūno arvantam āvataṁ | madhu priyaṁ bharatho yat saraḍbhyas tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.22**

yābhir naraṁ goṣuyudhaṁ nṛṣāhye kṣetrasya sātā tanayasya jinvathaḥ | yābhī rathāṁ avatho yābhir arvatas tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.23**

yābhiḥ kutsam ārjuneyaṁ śatakratū pra turvītiṁ pra ca dabhītiṁ āvataṁ | yābhir dhvasantiṁ puruṣantiṁ āvataṁ tābhir ū ṣu ūtibhir aśvinā gatam ||

**1.112.24**

apnasvantīm aśvinā vācam asme kṛtaṁ no dasrā vṛṣaṇā manīṣām | adyūtye 'vase ni hvaye vāṁ vṛdhe ca no bhavataṁ vājasātau ||

**1.112.25**

dyubhir aktubhiḥ pari pātam asmān ariṣṭebhir aśvinā saubhāgebhiḥ | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.113.1**

idaṁ śreṣṭhaṁ jyotiṣāṁ jyotir āgāc citraḥ praketho ajaniṣṭa vibhvā | yathā prasūtā savituḥ savāyam̐ evā rātry uṣase yonim āraik ||

**1.113.2**

ruśadvatsā ruśatī śvetyāgād āraig u kṛṣṇā sadanāny asyāḥ | samānabandhu amṛte anūcī dyāvā varṇaṁ carata āminā ne ||

**1.113.3**

samāno adhvā svasor anantas tam anyānyā carato devaśiṣṭe | na methete na tasthatuḥ sumeke naktoṣāsā samanasā virūpe ||

**1.113.4**

bhāsvatī netrī sūnṛtānām aceti citrā vi duro na āvaḥ | prārpyā jagad vy u no rāyo akhyad uṣā ajīgar bhuvanāni viśvā ||

**1.113.5**

jihmaśye caritave maghony ābhogaya iṣṭaye rāya u tvam | dabhrham paśyadbhya urviyā vicakṣa uṣā ajīgar bhuvanāni viśvā ||

**1.113.6**

kṣatrāya tvam śravase tvam mahīyā iṣṭaye tvam artham iva tvam ityai | visadṛśā jīvitābhipracakṣa uṣā ajīgar bhuvanāni viśvā ||

**1.113.7**

eṣā divo duhitā praty adarśi vyucchantī yuvatiḥ śukravāsāḥ | viśvasyeśānā pārthavasya vasva uṣo adyeha subhage vy uccha ||

**1.113.8**

parāyatīnām anv eti pātham āyatīnāṁ prathamā śaśvatīnām | vyucchantī jīvam udīrayantī uṣā mṛtaṁ kaṁ ca na bodhayanntī ||

**1.113.9**

uṣo yad agniṁ samidhe cakartha vi yad āvaś cakṣasā sūryasya | yan mānuṣān yakṣyamāṇāṁ ajīgas tad deveṣu cakṛṣe bhadram apnaḥ ||

**1.113.10**

kiyāty ā yat samayā bhavāti yā vyūṣur yāś ca nūnaṁ vyucchān | anu pūrvāḥ kṛpate vāvaśānā pradīdhyānā joṣam anyābhir eti ||

**1.113.11**

īyuṣ ṭe ye pūrvataraṁ apaśyan vyucchantīm uṣasaṁ martyāsaḥ | asmābhir ū nu praticakṣyābhūd o te yanti ye aparīṣu paśyān ||

**1.113.12**

yāvayadveṣā ṛtapā ṛtejāḥ sumnāvarī sūnṛtā īrayantī | sumaṅgalīr bibhratī devavītiṁ ihādyoṣaḥ śreṣṭhatamā vy uccha ||

**1.113.13**

śaśvat purośā vy uvāsa devy atho adyedaṁ vy āvo maghonī | atho vy ucchād uttarāṁ anu dyūn ajarāmṛtā carati svadhābhiḥ ||

**1.113.14**

vy añjibhir diva ātāsv adyaud apa kṛṣṇāṁ nirṇijaṁ devy āvaḥ | prabodhayantī aruṇebhir aśvair oṣā yāti suyujā rathena ||

**1.113.15**

āvahantī poṣyā vāryāṇi citraṁ ketuṁ kṛṇute cekitānā | īyuṣīṇām upamā śaśvatīnāṁ vibhātīnāṁ prathamoṣā vy aśvait ||

**1.113.16**

ud īrdhvaṁ jīvo asur na āgād apa prāgāt tama ā jyotir eti | āraik panthāṁ yātave sūryāyāganma yatra pratiranta āyuḥ ||

**1.113.17**

syūmanā vāca ud iyarti vahniḥ stavāno rebha uṣaso vibhātīḥ | adyā tad uccha gṛṇate maghony asme āyur ni didīhi prajāvat ||

**1.113.18**

yā gomatīr uṣasaḥ sarvavīrā vyucchanti dāśuṣe martyāya | vāyor iva sūnṛtānām udarke tā aśvadā aśnavat somasutva ||

**1.113.19**

mātā devānām aditir anīkaṁ yajñasya ketuṁ bṛhatī vi bhāhi | praśastikṛd brahmaṇe no vy ucchā no jane janaya viśvavāre ||

**1.113.20**

yac citram apna uṣaso vahantī jānāya śaśamānāya bhadram | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.114.1**

imā rudrāya tavase kapardine kṣayadvīrāya pra bharāmahe matīḥ | yathā śam asad dvipade catuṣpade viśvam puṣṭaṁ grāme asmin anāturam ||

**1.114.2**

mṛḻā no rudro uta no mayas kṛdhi kṣayadvīrāya namasā vidhema te | yac chaṁ ca yoś ca manur āyeje pitā tad aśyāma tava rudra praṇītiṣu ||

**1.114.3**

aśyāma te sumatim devayajyayā kṣayadvīrasya tava rudra mīḍhvaḥ | sumnāyann id viśo asmākam ā car ariṣṭavīrā juhavāma te haviḥ ||

**1.114.4**

tveṣaṁ vayam rudraṁ yajñasādham vaṅkuṁ kavim avase ni hvayāmahe | āre asmad daivyaṁ heḻo asyatu sumatim id vayam asyā vṛṇīmahe ||

**1.114.5**

divo varāham aruṣaṁ kapardinam tveṣaṁ rūpaṁ namasā ni hvayāmahe | haste bibhrad bheṣajā vāryāṇi śarma varma cchardi asmabhyaṁ yaṁsat ||

**1.114.6**

idam pitre marutām ucyate vacaḥ svādoḥ svādīyo rudrāya vardhanam | rāsvā ca no amṛta martabhojanaṁ tmane tokāya tanayāya mṛḻa ||

**1.114.7**

mā no mahāntam uta mā no arbhakam mā na ukṣantam uta mā na ukṣitam | mā no vadhīḥ pitaram ota mātaraṁ mā naḥ priyās tanvo rudra rīriṣaḥ ||

**1.114.8**

mā nas toke tanaye mā na āyau mā no goṣu mā no aśveṣu rīriṣaḥ | vīrān mā no rudra bhāmito vadhīr haviṣmantaḥ sadam it tvā havāmahe ||

**1.114.9**

upa te stomān paśupā ivākaram rāsvā pitar marutāṁ sumnam asme | bhadrā hi te sumatir mṛḻayattamā athā vayam ava it te vṛṇīmahe ||

**1.114.10**

āre te goghnam uta pūruṣaghnaṁ kṣayadvīra sumnam asme te astu | mṛḻā ca no adhi ca brūhi devādhā ca naḥ śarma yaccha dvibarhāḥ ||

**1.114.11**

avocāma namo asmā avasyavaḥ śṛṇotu no havaṁ rudro marutvān | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.115.1**

citraṁ devānāṁ ud agād anīkaṁ cakṣur mitrasya varuṇasyāgneḥ | āprā dyāvāpṛthivī antariksaṁ sūrya ātmā jagatas tasthūṣaś ca ||

**1.115.2**

sūryo devīm uṣasaṁ rocamānāṁ maryo na yoṣām abhy eti paścāt | yatrā naro devayanto yugāni vitanvate prati bhadrāya bhadram ||

**1.115.3**

bhadrā aśvā haritaḥ sūryasya citrā etagvā anumādyāsaḥ | namasyanto diva ā pṛṣṭham asthuḥ pari dyāvāpṛthivī yanti sadyaḥ ||

**1.115.4**

tat sūryasya devatvaṁ tan mahitvaṁ madhyā kartor vitataṁ saṁ jabhāra | yaded ayukta haritaḥ sadhasthād ād rātrī vāsas tanute simasmai ||

**1.115.5**

tan mitrasya varuṇasyābhicakṣe sūryo rūpaṁ kṛṇute dyor upasthe | anantam anyad ruśad asya pājaḥ kṛṣṇam anyad dharitaḥ saṁ bharanti ||

**1.115.6**

adyā devā uditā sūryasya nir aṁhasaḥ pipṛtā nir avadyāt | tan no mitro varuṇo māmahantām aditiḥ sindhuḥ pṛthivī uta dyauḥ ||

**1.116.1**

nāsatyābhyām barhir iva pra vṛñje stomāṁ iyarmy abhriyeva vātaḥ | yāv arbhagāya vimadāya jāyāṁ senājuvā nyūhathū rathena ||

**1.116.2**

vīḷupatmabhir āśuhemabhir vā devānāṁ vā jūtibhiḥ śāśadānā | tad rāsabho nāsatyā sahasram ājā yamasya pradhane jigāya ||

**1.116.3**

tugro ha bhujyum aśvinodameghe rayiṁ na kaś cin mamṛvāṁ avāhāḥ | tam ūhathu naubhir ātmanvatībhir antarikṣaprūdbhir apodakābhiḥ ||

**1.116.4**

tisraḥ kṣapas trir ahātitvrajatdbhir nāsatyā bhujyum ūhathuḥ pataṁgaiḥ | samudrasya dhanvann ārdrasyā pāre tribhī rathai śatapadbhiḥ ṣaḷaśvaiḥ ||

**1.116.5**

anārambhaṇe tad avīrayethām anāsthāne agrabhaṇe samudre | yad aśvinā ūhathu bhujyum astaṁ śatāritrāṁ nāvam ātasthivāṁsam ||

**1.116.6**

yam aśvinā dadathuḥ śvetam aśvam aghāśvāya śaśvad it svasti | tad vāṁ dātram mahi kīrtenyam bhūt paidvo vājī sadam id dhavyo aryaḥ ||

**1.116.7**

yuvaṁ narā stuvate pajriyāya kakṣīvate aradataṁ puraṁdhim | kārotarāc chaphād aśvasya vṛṣṇaḥ śataṁ kumbhām asiñcataṁ surāyāḥ ||

**1.116.8**

himenāgniṁ ghraṁsam avārayethāṁ pitumatīm ūrjam asmā adhattam | ṛbīse atriṁ aśvinā vanitam un ninyathuḥ sarvagaṇaṁ svasti ||

**1.116.9**

parāvataṁ nāsatyānudethām uccābudhnaṁ cakrathur jihmabāram | kṣarann āpo na pāyanāya rāye sahasrāya tṛṣyate gotamasya ||

**1.116.10**
